We designate the VLM as the ``creative brain'' for requirement comprehension, task planning, and aesthetic judgment, while utilizing the image generation model as an on-demand ``visual world simulator'' to synthesize standalone visual assets. Operating under an ``imagine first, then act'' closed-loop workflow, the agent generates isolated assets, writes native HTML/CSS, and iteratively refines the design against visual rendering feedback.
  Furthermore, Agent Design Replay faithfully reproduces the creative and reasoning trajectory akin to that of professional human designers. Ultimately, the system delivers editable artifacts with decoupled layers and real text, enabling users to perform intuitive mouse dragging and layout adjustments on a graphical user interface. Validations on posters, infographics, and other scenarios show that this paradigm successfully achieves both refined aesthetics and production-grade editability.

</details>


### [87] [Continuous Actions from Discrete Minds: Latent-Aligned Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04070)
*Ruoyu Yao,Yusen Xie,Qingzhao Liu,Pei Liu,Zewei Yang,Yipeng Zhu,Xiaolong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaPla是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，通过潜在对齐规划(Latent-aligned Planning)将语义理解无缝映射到精确的车辆运动执行，在nuScenes基准上降低长时L2误差15.52%，并在NVIDIA AlpaSim模拟器上将成功率提升33.34个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型的离散推理与自动驾驶中连续、受物理约束的动态特性之间存在显著的模态鸿沟。现有VLA方法依赖离散码本查找，不可避免引入量化误差，且自回归生成耗时严重，难以兼顾语义理解精度与物理可行的运动执行。

Method: LaPla包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于残差VQ-VAE的动作分词器，捕获车辆运动学并编码轨迹特征到结构化潜在空间；(2) 将该表示重新用作物理先验(physical prior)，而非离散码本查找，以弥合高维语义与原始动作空间之间的模态差距；(3) 引入并发动作查询(concurrent action queries)，在单次前向传播中对多模态上下文(多视角图像、历史动作、文本指令)进行因果关注，将隐藏状态直接投影到预训练的VQ-VAE潜在空间，随后由冻结解码器将连续潜在变量转换为动作，消除量化误差并绕开自回归生成。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上实现具有竞争力的开环性能，长时L2误差相比最先进VLA方法降低15.52%；在NVIDIA AlpaSim模拟器的闭环评估中，成功率提升33.34个百分点，推断延迟显著降低，确保平滑的驾驶进度。

Conclusion: LaPla通过潜在对齐规划有效弥合了离散语义推理与连续物理动作之间的鸿沟，消除了量化误差并显著提升推理效率，为VLA框架在自动驾驶中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between the discrete reasoning of Vision-Language Models and the continuous, physics-constrained nature of autonomous driving remains a significant challenge. In this work, we introduce LaPla, a unified Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework featuring latent-aligned planning to seamlessly ground semantic understanding in precise motion execution. We first design an action tokenizer based on a residual vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE), capturing vehicle kinematics and encoding trajectory features into a structured latent space. Rather than discrete codebook lookups that inevitably introduce quantization errors, LaPla repurposes this representation as a physical prior to bridge the modality gap between high-dimensional semantics and the raw action space. Specifically, given multimodal inputs integrating multi-view images, historical actions, and textual instructions, LaPla incorporates concurrent action queries to causally attend to the multimodal context in a single forward pass, projecting hidden states directly into the pretrained VQ-VAE latent space. The frozen decoder then translates these continuous latents into actions, effectively eliminating quantization errors and ensuring physically plausible trajectories while bypassing time-consuming autoregressive generation. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes benchmark demonstrate that LaPla achieves competitive open-loop performance, reducing long-horizon L2 error by 15.52% compared to state-of-the-art VLA methods. Closed-loop evaluations on the NVIDIA AlpaSim simulator further confirm its superior capability in ensuring smooth driving progress, improving the success rate by 33.34 percentage points with significantly reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [88] [TAP-Path: Task-Adaptive Structural and Token Pruning for Efficient and Trustworthy Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04071)
*Mehedi Hasan,Ashfak Yeafi,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAP-Path，一种面向病理学基础模型的任务自适应压缩框架，直接对预训练Virchow2编码器进行结构重构而非蒸馏到独立学生网络，通过去除冗余块、输入自适应patch-token剪枝、多深度特征恢复和轻量门控任务头，将编码器参数减少24.96%（631.24M→473.70M），计算量减少35.20%，在32类病理基准上取得87.98%测试准确率，优于完整Virchow2（86.89%）和UNI2-h（87.67%），并在外部CPTAC数据集上保持高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型在表示学习上有显著收益，但近年高性能模型往往依赖数亿参数的高推理成本编码器，严重制约了实际部署和临床应用，因此需要在不牺牲精度的前提下实现大规模病理基础模型的高效压缩与加速。

Method: TAP-Path采用任务自适应压缩框架，具体包括：验证集驱动的transformer块选择与物理移除冗余块、输入自适应的patch-token剪枝、多深度特征恢复机制，以及轻量门控任务头。该方法不将教师模型蒸馏为独立学生模型，而是直接重构预训练的Virchow2编码器结构。

Result: 从32个transformer块中保留24个，保留70%的patch token；编码器参数减少24.96%（631.24M降至473.70M），分析计算量减少35.20%（340.13G降至220.40G FLOPs）。在32类病理基准上取得87.98±0.067%测试准确率、81.26±0.49%平衡准确率、82.38±0.48% macro-F1，Brier分数0.1800±0.0005，失败检测AUROC 0.9047±0.0060。在433个CPTAC外部样本上获得91.22±0.83%准确率。次级分析中验证集专用的rare-aware目标改善了稀有类别平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的结构级与token级稀疏化能够同时改善大规模病理基础模型的精度-效率权衡，且在内部与外部评估下均保持可靠性与泛化能力，为病理基础模型的高效部署提供了可行的压缩方案。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models improve transferable representation learning for histopathology, but recent gains often rely on encoders with hundreds of millions of parameters and high inference cost. We propose TAP-Path, a task-adaptive compression framework that directly restructures a pretrained Virchow2 encoder rather than distilling it into a separate student. TAP-Path combines validation-driven transformer-block selection, physical removal of redundant blocks, input-adaptive patch-token pruning, multi-depth feature recovery, and a lightweight gated task head. The final model retains 24 of 32 transformer blocks and 70% of patch tokens after pruning, reducing encoder parameters by 24.96% (631.24M to 473.70M) and analytical encoder compute by 35.20% (340.13G to 220.40G FLOPs). Across three task-head optimization seeds, TAP-Path achieved $87.98 \pm 0.067%$ test accuracy, $81.26 \pm 0.49%$ balanced accuracy, and $82.38 \pm 0.48%$ macro-F1 on a 32-class histopathology benchmark, compared with 86.89% for full Virchow2 and 87.67% for UNI2-h. TAP-Path achieved a Brier score of $0.1800 \pm 0.0005$ and failure-detection AUROC of $0.9047 \pm 0.0060$. A validation-only rare-aware objective improved rare-class balanced accuracy in a secondary operating analysis. Frozen external evaluation on 433 CPTAC samples yielded $91.22 \pm 0.83%$ accuracy and $91.10 \pm 0.81%$ balanced accuracy. These results show that task-adaptive structural and token sparsification can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of large pathology foundation models while preserving reliability under internal and external evaluation.

</details>


### [89] [CORE: Improving Compositional Reasoning in MLLM Embedding via Reranker Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04083)
*Tingyu Song,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang,Dingkun Long,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Yilun Zhao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE方法，通过将跨注意力重排序器（reranker）的组合判断蒸馏到嵌入模型中，解决多模态大模型（MLLM）嵌入在组合检索上的不足。CORE合成覆盖五种组合匹配级别的候选列表，引入Rank-KL目标函数让嵌入模型复现重排序器的细粒度排序。实验表明Rank-KL性能最强，CORE-RERANKER-8B在三个组合推理基准上平均达82.7%，CORE-EMBED-8B达到最佳嵌入性能，且不牺牲常规检索表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM嵌入模型在组合检索中表现有限，难以区分包含相同概念但属性-对象绑定不同的场景（例如"红衣女孩"与"女孩穿红鞋"这类组合差异）。然而，同一骨干网络在被用作跨注意力重排序器时能解决此类区分问题。作者受此启发，希望将重排序器的组合判断能力蒸馏到嵌入模型中，从而获得兼具高效检索与组合理解能力的嵌入表示。

Method: 1) 提出CORE框架：合成跨五个组合匹配级别（组合匹配的逐步难度）的候选列表作为训练数据。2) 引入Rank-KL目标函数，训练嵌入模型复现重排序器输出的细粒度排序分布。3) 在该统一数据与调优预算下，对比对比学习（contrastive learning）、点对CoSENT和列表式Rank-KL三种训练范式。4) 提出分级（graded）评估协议以更细粒度衡量组合检索能力。

Result: 在COLA、SUGARCREPE++和NEGBENCH三个组合推理基准上：CORE-RERANKER-8B平均准确率达82.7%，优于Jina-Reranker 10.7个百分点；CORE-EMBED-8B在所有评估的嵌入模型中取得最佳总平均分数（0.666）。实验还发现CoSENT和Rank-KL比对比学习更能有效利用多级监督信号。改进还迁移到MCMR基准，且不损失在COCO和Flickr30K上的检索性能。

Conclusion: 列表级排名蒸馏（Rank-KL）是比对比学习和点对损失更有效的策略，能将重排序器的组合判别能力成功迁移到嵌入模型。该方法证明了"重排序器强但嵌入弱"的矛盾可通过知识蒸馏弥合，为组合多模态检索提供了在不牺牲常规检索性能前提下的高效嵌入方案。

Abstract: MLLM-based embedding models remain limited in compositional retrieval, often failing to distinguish scenes containing the same concepts but different attribute-object bindings. Yet the same backbone can resolve such distinctions when used as a cross-attentive reranker, motivating us to distill its compositional judgments into the embedding model. We propose CORE, which synthesizes candidate lists spanning five compositional matching levels and introduces a Rank-KL objective that trains the embedding model to reproduce the reranker's fine-grained ranking. We further introduce a graded evaluation protocol and compare contrastive learning, pairwise CoSENT, and listwise Rank-KL under the same data and tuning budget. Our comparison shows that both CoSENT and Rank-KL use the multi-level supervision more effectively than contrastive learning, with Rank-KL achieving the strongest overall performance. Across three compositional reasoning benchmarks (COLA, SUGARCREPE++, NEGBENCH), CORE-RERANKER-8B achieves an 82.7% total average, outperforming Jina-Reranker by 10.7 points, while CORE-EMBED-8B achieves the best total average (0.666) among all evaluated embedding models. The improvements transfer to the MCMR benchmark without sacrificing retrieval performance on COCO and Flickr30K.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Semantic Understanding from Digital Foveation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04088)
*Caterina Caccavella,Vittorio Fra,Andreas Ziegler,Giulia D'Angelo,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受生物视觉启发的数字中央凹主动视觉语义分割管线，通过显著性驱动的注视点选择、高分辨率中央凹观测与低分辨率上下文结合，在极低计算成本下实现接近密集分割基准的语义理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集语义分割在所有图像区域均匀分配计算资源，然而场景复杂度和任务相关性并不均匀。受生物视觉机制启发，作者探究是否可以通过数字中央凹感知（对某些区域进行高分辨率观测、其他区域低分辨率处理）来更高效地实现语义理解，而非对所有像素进行统一密集处理。

Method: 提出一个轻量级主动视觉管线，包含四个核心组件：（1）显著性驱动的注视点（fixation）选择机制；（2）高分辨率中央凹观测；（3）低分辨率上下文信息（外周视觉）；（4）语义积累与自适应计算。与传统密集预测指标不同，作者引入目标级（object-level）评估来衡量稀疏观测下的语义理解能力，并在ADE20K-Object数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在ADE20K-Object上，单次中央凹观测达到基线Top-1准确率的95.9%和Top-3准确率的96.9%，但仅需4.7%的计算量；在场景层面，通过语义积累可恢复基线目标召回率的90.6%，仅使用58.6%的计算量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当计算资源被选择性分配时，从稀疏观测中即可涌现出显著的语义理解能力，突出了主动视觉作为均匀密集处理的高效替代方案的价值，同时激励超越传统像素级分割指标的评估协议设计。

Abstract: Dense semantic segmentation allocates computational resources uniformly across the entire image, regardless of scene complexity or task relevance. Inspired by biological vision, we investigate whether semantic understanding can be achieved more efficiently through digital foveated perception. We introduce a lightweight active-vision pipeline that combines saliency-driven fixation selection, high-resolution foveal observations, low-resolution contextual information, semantic accumulation, and adaptive computation. Beyond conventional dense prediction metrics, we use object-level evaluation to measure semantic understanding under sparse observations. On ADE20K-Object, a single foveated observation achieves 95.9% of the baseline Top-1 accuracy and 96.9% of the baseline Top-3 accuracy while requiring only 4.7% of the computational cost. At the scene level, semantic accumulation recovers 90.6% of the baseline object recall while using 58.6% of the computation. These results suggest that substantial semantic understanding can emerge from sparse observations when computation is allocated selectively, highlighting active vision as an efficient alternative to uniform dense processing and motivating evaluation protocols beyond conventional pixel-wise segmentation metrics.

</details>


### [91] [The Shape of Time: Video-Token Contrast for Temporal Understanding in VideoLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04110)
*Yumeng Shi,Quanyu Long,Yin Wu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-Contrast，一种面向视频语言模型（VideoLM）的表示级时序反事实训练目标。现有VideoLM虽接收有序视频流，但主要监督信号作用于生成文本而非视频token表示，导致模型可通过物体、场景和语言先验等捷径学习时序答案。VT-Contrast对选定的后期层最后一帧视频token进行监督，通过对比保序视图与按Kendall tau距离分级打乱顺序的同视频反事实样本，无需改动架构即可提升时序理解基准上的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoLM的一个关键缺陷是"按序观看帧不代表表征时间"：模型接收有序视频流，但主要监督只作用于生成文本，而非应先涌现事件动态的视频token表示。这种监督错位使得模型能借助物体、场景和语言先验等捷径回答时序问题，内部视频表示无需真正捕获事件进展。

Method: 提出VT-Contrast，一种表示级时序反事实对比目标。其核心设计回答"时序监督应作用于何处"与"应暴露何种时序差异"两个问题：(1) 监督选定的后期层最后一帧视频token，该位置预期在语言生成前已集成时序信息；(2) 对比保序视图与同一视频被打乱顺序的反事实视图，并以Kendall tau距离对打乱程度分级。该方法无需架构改动，且兼容多种VideoLM训练任务。

Result: VT-Contrast在多个时序理解基准上显著提升了模型的整体性能，且不依赖架构改动，可与多种VideoLM训练任务兼容。

Conclusion: 通过将时序监督直接注入视频token表示层而非仅依赖文本生成监督，可以有效破除模型利用捷径进行时序推理的倾向，为VideoLM提供了一种轻量且通用的时序理解增强方案。

Abstract: Seeing frames in order does not mean representing time. Modern VideoLMs receive ordered video streams, yet their main supervision acts on generated text rather than video-token representations where event dynamics should first emerge. This mismatch allows models to learn temporal answers from shortcuts such as objects, scenes, and language priors, without requiring internal video representations to capture event progression. To address this, we propose VT-Contrast, a representation-level temporal counterfactual objective for VideoLMs. Its design asks where temporal supervision should act and what temporal differences it should expose. VT-Contrast supervises selected late-layer last-frame video tokens, where temporal information is expected to be integrated before language generation, and contrasts order-preserving views with same-video reordered counterfactuals graded by Kendall tau distance. It requires no architectural changes, is compatible with diverse VideoLM training tasks, and improves overall performance across temporal understanding benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/ANDgate99/VT-Contrast.

</details>


### [92] [BooM-VVT: Boosting Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On with Image-Level Pseudo Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04120)
*Wei Zhang,Xin Li,Peishu Shi,Jialin Gao,Xuekang Peng,Zhichao Lian,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了BooM-VVT，一个无掩码的视频虚拟试穿（VVT）框架，采用关键帧驱动范式，通过多阶段训练策略利用图像级伪数据实现无掩码定位学习，并提出Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling进行关键帧采样以提高服装一致性，引入Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE实现关键帧与目标视频帧之间的时空对应以精确传递服装细节，同时构建大规模多视角试穿数据集OmniView以支持复杂视角下的视频试穿生成，实验证明其在时间一致性和服装保真度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法依赖掩码来定位试穿区域，难以应对大幅度运动和严重遮挡；无掩码图像级试穿方法虽有效但难以扩展到视频，因为构建视频级伪数据成本过高；此外，粗粒度关键帧采样和多视角试穿数据稀缺导致服装一致性问题难以解决。

Method: BooM-VVT采用以下策略：(1)多阶段训练策略，利用图像级伪数据进行无掩码定位学习，减少对昂贵视频级伪数据的依赖；(2)Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling，基于服装相关身体区域选择关键帧以更好地捕捉服装外观；(3)Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE建立关键帧与视频帧间的时空对应关系，实现服装细节精确传递；(4)构建OmniView大规模多视角试穿数据集支持复杂视角下的视频试穿任务。

Result: 大量实验表明BooM-VVT在时间一致性和服装保真度方面均优于现有方法，特别是在复杂相机视角和多样试穿任务下表现突出。

Conclusion: BooM-VVT通过无掩码关键帧驱动范式有效解决了视频虚拟试穿中的关键挑战，包括掩码依赖、服装不一致和视角受限等问题，为视频虚拟试穿提供了一种更稳健且成本更低的解决方案。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on (VVT) aims to generate realistic videos of a person wearing a target garment. Recent methods leverage a keyframe-driven video generation paradigm to improve in-the-wild performance, yet they still rely on masks to localize try-on regions, making them vulnerable to large motions and severe occlusions. Although mask-free image-based try-on methods have shown promising results by leveraging large-scale pseudo data, extending this paradigm to videos remains difficult, as constructing video-level pseudo data is prohibitively expensive. Furthermore, coarse keyframe sampling and the scarcity of multi-view try-on data limit existing keyframe-driven methods in maintaining garment consistency and handling diverse try-on tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose BooM-VVT, a mask-free VVT framework built upon the keyframe-driven paradigm. To achieve mask-free VVT, we introduce a multi-stage training strategy that leverages image-level pseudo data for mask-free localization learning, substantially reducing the need for costly video-level pseudo data. To improve garment consistency, we propose Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling, which selects keyframes based on garment-relevant body regions to better capture garment appearance. We further introduce Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE to establish spatiotemporal correspondences between keyframes and target video frames for accurate garment-detail transfer. Finally, we construct OmniView, a large-scale multi-view try-on dataset to support reliable try-on video generation under complex camera viewpoints and diverse try-on tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BooM-VVT achieves superior temporal consistency and garment fidelity over existing methods. Project page: https://boomvvt.github.io/boomvvt.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Retrieval: Progressive Latent Memory Evolution for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04131)
*Hongyu Qu,Guangming Yao,Ling Xing,Xiaobin Hu,Rongxing Ding,Guibin Zhang,Fan Zhang,Yi Yuan,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LatentStream，一种渐进式潜工作记忆框架，将流式视频记忆从"存储-检索"范式转变为"检索-内化"范式，通过分层流式记忆组织、分层潜记忆进化和渐进式置信度引导优化，将历史视频证据内化为紧凑的固定长度潜记忆，实现流式视频理解并达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法通常采用"存储-检索"范式，即把历史观察压缩到外部记忆库中，并在查询到来时检索相关证据作为额外视觉上下文。但该范式将历史证据始终保持为外部视觉上下文，无法将其内化为紧凑、不断演化的潜在记忆来持续引导流式推理，限制了流式理解的效率与能力。

Method: LatentStream包含三个协同组件：(1)查询无关的分层流式记忆（Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory），通过Jenks引导的自适应凝聚在固定记忆预算下将视觉历史组织为短期、中期和长期三个层次；(2)分层潜记忆进化（Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution），为各组潜记忆token赋予逐步扩大的记忆感受野，使其能从对应范围迭代检索历史证据并内化为紧凑的固定长度潜记忆；(3)渐进式置信度引导的潜记忆优化（Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization），从分组预测熵构建分层渐进奖励，联合细化潜记忆token与检索到的证据，鼓励更自信的流式推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，LatentStream在现有的在线和离线视频基准测试上取得了新的最先进结果。

Conclusion: 通过将流式记忆从"存储-检索"转变为"检索-内化"范式，LatentStream成功地将历史证据内化为紧凑的演进式潜记忆，显著提升了流式视频理解在多模态大语言模型上的表现，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process continuous visual inputs and respond to user queries under strict causality and bounded memory. Existing approaches typically compress historical observations into an external memory bank and retrieve query-relevant evidence as additional visual context. Though effective, this store-and-retrieve paradigm keeps historical evidence as external visual context, preventing it from being internalized into a compact, evolving latent memory that can continuously guide streaming reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce LatentStream, a progressive latent working memory framework that shifts streaming memory from store-and-retrieve to retrieve-and-internalize. Specifically, LatentStream comprises three coordinated components. First, Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory organizes visual history into short-, mid-, and long-term levels under a fixed memory budget through Jenks-guided adaptive consolidation. Once a query arrives, Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution equips groups of latent memory tokens with progressively expanding memory receptive fields, enabling them to iteratively retrieve historical evidence from their corresponding scopes and internalize it into a compact, fixed-length latent memory. Finally, Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization constructs a hierarchical progression reward from group-wise predictive entropy and jointly refines the latent memory tokens and retrieved evidence, encouraging increasingly confident streaming reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LatentStream achieves new state-of-the-art results on existing online and offline video benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [Persistent Identity Preservation in Generative Image Models: A Benchmark and Evaluation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04151)
*Mengwei Ren,Xuaner Zhang,Zhihao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了生成式图像模型中的身份保持问题，通过对多种身份表示范式（输入上下文、可训练模型参数、持久身份层）进行基准测试，发现强图像质量和指令遵循能力并不保证身份保真度，而持久身份表示方法能显著降低身份退化，表明身份应作为与底层生成模型独立组合的持久知识被建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像模型虽然能够生成高质量图像、遵循复杂指令和进行精确编辑，但在生成或编辑特定主体时难以保持身份一致性（如姿势、表情、外观、视角或场景变化时身份漂移）。不同方法对身份表示位置的选择存在根本性差异，但缺乏系统性比较与评估，身份保持问题尚未被充分理解。

Method: 本文对三种身份表示范式进行了系统基准测试：(1) 通过输入上下文表示身份（如GPT-Image-2, NB2）；(2) 通过可训练的主体特定模型参数表示身份（如LoRA）；(3) 通过跨代际和编辑可复用的持久身份层表示身份（如PHOTA IDENTITY）。基准覆盖了主体驱动生成、编辑、修复和多主体设置，设计了逐步加大身份保持压力的任务。

Result: 身份保持仍然是当前生成基础模型的独特局限性：图像质量和指令遵循能力强并不必然意味着身份保真度强；在迭代编辑、小主体尺度、严重图像退化和多主体组合等场景下身份退化更加显著。持久身份表示在生成、编辑和修复任务中均能显著降低身份退化，在应用于不同基础模型时能持续改进身份保持，同时保持相当的指令遵循度和感知图像质量。

Conclusion: 身份并非从日益强大的生成模型中简单涌现，而是可以被表示为与底层生成模型独立组合的持久主体知识。这一发现对主体驱动图像生成与编辑的研究方向具有重要启示。

Abstract: Generative image models can now produce high-quality images, follow complex instructions, and support precise edits, but they still struggle to preserve who or what is being depicted. When generating or editing images of a specific subject, identity may drift as the pose, expression, appearance, viewpoint, or surrounding scene changes. Existing subject-driven methods make fundamentally different choices about where identity is represented: through the input context (GPT-Image-2, NB2), as trainable subject-specific model parameters (LoRA), or as a persistent identity layer (PHOTA IDENTITY) reusable across generations and edits. We systematically benchmark these paradigms across subject-driven generation, editing, restoration, and multi-subject settings, with tasks designed to increasingly stress identity preservation. Our results show that identity preservation remains a distinct limitation of current generative foundation models: strong image quality and instruction following do not necessarily imply strong identity fidelity, and identity degradation becomes more pronounced under iterative edits, small subject scales, severe image degradation, and multi-subject composition. Persistent identity substantially reduces this degradation across generation, editing, and restoration, consistently improving identity preservation when applied to different foundation models while maintaining comparable instruction adherence and perceptual image quality. These results suggest that identity does not simply emerge from increasingly capable generative models, but can instead be represented as persistent subject knowledge that is composed independently with the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [95] [Zero-Shot Novel Depth Synthesis Using 3D Foundation Models Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04174)
*Denis M. Akola,David F. Fouhey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用3D基础模型(如VGGT)的内部表征来推断新视角下的3D场景信息，提出了一种名为Z3D的方法，通过对3DFM表征进行潜在扩散来估计未见视图中的点映射图，从而预测跨多个数据集的新视图真实深度图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型(3DFMs)如VGGT已在3D视觉领域取得突破，能够通过前馈Transformer预测统一的丰富表征。作者假设这些模型在解决3D重建任务时，必须学习包含大量3D场景通用知识的内在表征，因此想要探究能否利用这些内部表征来从新视图中推断3D信息。

Method: 首先展示可以从3DFM的内部表征中解码隐藏表面，然后提出Z3D方法，即在3DFM表征上进行潜在扩散(latent diffusion)，以估计未见视图中的点映射图(pointmaps)。

Result: Z3D能够跨多个数据集为新视图预测出逼真的深度图。

Conclusion: 3DFM的内部表征确实包含足够的3D场景通用知识，足以支持从新视角推断3D结构；通过潜在扩散在这些表征上进行操作是一种有效的生成式3D推断途径。

Abstract: 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs) such as VGGT have recently pushed the boundaries of 3D vision by predicting rich unified representations with feed-foward transformers. The scene representations learned by these models enable strong performance on multiple 3D vision tasks. In this paper, we investigate using their internal representations to infer 3D in the scene from new views. Our hypothesis is that in order to solve the task of 3D reconstruction, these models need to learn a representation that includes a large amount of general knowledge about 3D scenes. After showing that it is possible to decode hidden surfaces from internal 3DFM representations, we propose a method, Z3D, that estimates pointmaps in unseen views by doing latent diffusion on 3DFM representation. We show that Z3D can predict realistic depth maps for new views across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [96] [Seeing Before Synthesizing: VLM-Guided Transition Event Discovery for Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04183)
*Ye-Chan Kim,Seunghee Choi,SeungJu Cha,Si-Woo Kim,Hwiseon Kim,Hyungee Kim,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SBS框架，用于弱监督密集视频描述生成。与以往直接由LLM合成过渡性文本并固定分配到所有事件间隔不同，SBS先借助VLM生成间隔区域的帧级叙述并检测语义转换点，仅在有实际过渡处自适应地提供视觉锚定的语言指导，并通过混合时间中点与语义变化点来细化事件间时间掩码。在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作利用LLM合成辅助过渡性标题来增强视觉-语言对齐，但这些标题缺乏视觉依据，且被刚性分配到每个事件间空隙的固定位置和时长，无法反映真实视频中的语义过渡。

Method: SBS框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 利用VLM为事件间隔生成逐帧叙述，并通过跨帧的语义变化检测转换点；(2) 对于识别出的转换，通过融合时间中点与语义变化点来细化事件间时间掩码，并选择最大化视觉-语言对齐的掩码宽度。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2数据集上的实验表明，SBS在描述生成和事件定位两方面均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过先视觉感知、后语言合成的方式提供自适应且有视觉依据的语言指导，能更准确地处理弱监督密集视频描述中的事件转换问题，验证了从视觉信号出发引导语言合成建模的有效性。

Abstract: Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning aims to localize and describe multiple events in untrimmed videos given only an ordered set of event-level captions per video. Recent work synthesizes auxiliary transition captions via LLM to provide additional vision-language alignment, but these captions lack visual grounding and are rigidly assigned to every inter-event gap at a fixed location and duration. To address these, we propose Seeing Before Synthesizing (SBS), a framework that adaptively provides visually grounded linguistic guidance only where warranted. Leveraging a VLM, we generate frame-level narratives for the inter-event gaps and detect transitions from the semantic variation across them. For identified transitions, we then refine inter-event temporal masks by blending the temporal midpoint with the semantic change point and selecting the width that maximizes vision-language alignment. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both captioning and localization.

</details>


### [97] [One Editor, Many Edits: A Unified Training-Free Framework for Diverse Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04190)
*Adheesh Sunil Juvekar,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Kiet A. Nguyen,Muntasir Wahed,Nabeel Bashir,Xiaona Zhou,Tianjiao Yu,Vedant Shah,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditVid是一个无需训练的视频编辑框架，通过稀疏因果记忆、基于对应关系的后注意力token注入和软潜变量混合三个模块，在统一框架内同时支持指令引导和主体引导的视频编辑，在FiVE基准上达到78.16的准确率，显著优于最强的无需训练基线（58.95），并在用户研究中获得51.8%的总体偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑涵盖多种编辑范式，但在单一统一框架内同时实现高质量的指令引导和主体引导编辑仍然具有挑战性，现有方法难以兼顾局部一致性、长程身份保持和编辑局部性。

Method: EditVid是一个训练自由的框架，包含三个核心模块：1）稀疏因果记忆用于维持局部一致性；2）基于对应关系的后注意力token注入用于长程身份保持；3）软潜变量混合用于保证编辑的局部性。该框架在同一架构下支持指令引导和参考引导的编辑任务，包括风格迁移、属性修改、物体插入、局部编辑和主体替换。

Result: 在FiVE基准上，EditVid达到78.16的FiVE-Acc，而最强的评估训练自由基线仅为58.95；在IVEBench上也获得具有竞争力的结果。用户研究表明，EditVid相比7种竞争方法获得51.8%的总体偏好。

Conclusion: EditVid证明了无需训练的统一框架能够同时胜任指令引导和主体引导的视频编辑，在多个任务类型上超越现有训练自由基线，并通过用户研究验证了其实际编辑质量的优越性。

Abstract: Video editing spans diverse editing paradigms, yet achieving high-quality instruction-guided and subject-guided editing within a single unified framework remains challenging. We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [98] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Puffin-World，一个统一的多模态架构，将物理理解、空间模拟和3D世界生成/重建集成为一个整体，无需外部离线模块；联合建模物理（重力场、纬度）、几何（深度）和外观（图像）三种原生世界状态，并配以统一的Omni-Camera表征和跨未来帧的物理动态传播策略。配套构建含15M视觉-语言-相机三元组与1M轨迹的Puffin-16M数据集，支持模仿与自校准世界探索等闭环任务，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D世界生成与空间理解通常依赖分离的外部模块（如独立深度估计、物理引擎），缺乏统一的架构来实现物理一致性、几何重建和外观生成的协同。作者希望构建一个无需外部模块、能内生联合建模物理、几何与外观的统一多模态框架，以实现物理一致且视觉稳定的3D世界生成与交互。

Method: 1) 统一建模三种原生世界状态：物理（重力场与纬度）、几何（深度）、外观（图像）；2) 引入统一的Omni-Camera相机表征，支持多样任务与灵活运动；3) 提出跨未来帧传播物理动力学的策略，将绝对相机属性锚定于真实世界以实现物理一致生成；4) 在同一生成过程中耦合外观与几何，联合合成未来视图并重建底层几何；5) 构建Puffin-16M大规模数据集（15M视觉-语言-相机三元组和1M运动轨迹），支持跨任务闭环应用（模仿、自校准世界探索）。

Result: Puffin-World实现了统一的架构整合物理理解、空间模拟与3D世界生成/重建，无需外部离线模块。通过Omni-Camera表征和物理动力学传播实现了物理一致与视觉稳定的世界生成，并在外观-几何联合生成下支持多任务协同的闭环应用（模仿和自校准世界探索）。已开放代码、模型与数据集。

Conclusion: Puffin-World展示了通过统一架构联合建模物理、几何与外观三种世界状态的可行性，为无需外部模块的物理一致3D世界生成提供了新范式，其支撑的大型数据集Puffin-16M与开源资源有望推动该领域后续研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Principia基准，通过同一场景中成对物体间的运动关系一致性来评估视频模型中的牛顿物理推理，这种评估方式与传统依赖帧率、物体尺度等绝对运动测量不同，与校准无关。在六个最先进的视频生成模型测试中，最高得分仅为0.42（VBench上均约0.8），视觉语言模型在检测物理违规方面最佳准确率仅67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视频模型物理推理能力很困难，因为绝对运动测量依赖于帧率、物体尺度和相机校准，这些参数在生成视频中通常模糊或不可用。因此需要一种与校准无关的评估方法。

Method: 提出Principia基准：当同一场景中两个物体遵循相同物理定律时，它们的运动必须满足可预测的关系，这些关系与校准无关。覆盖八种物理现象（重力、恢复系数、摩擦、转动惯量、抛体运动、动量、摆和质量-弹簧振荡），涵盖平动、转动、碰撞和振荡动力学。同时提出校准无关的"一致性得分"来直接量化图像空间中的物理违规。

Result: 在来自六个最先进视频生成器的数千次生成中，没有模型在Principia上超过0.42，尽管它们在VBench上均得分约0.8。视觉语言模型检测关系性物理违规的能力测试中，最佳模型仅达到67%准确率，大多数接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的视频生成模型在基础牛顿物理推理上严重不足——传统的VBench评分高企但Principia得分很低，说明现有评估体系未能覆盖物理一致性；同时视觉语言模型对物理违规的检测也远未可靠。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [100] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Scal3R，一种将在线3D重建中的位姿回归从固定首帧锚点改为多参考相对位姿查询的方法。通过向完全冻结的骨干网络注入轻量级可学习token（约占参数1%），结合非对称注意力机制查询相对多个关键帧的位姿，并配合带闭环的在线位姿图优化系统抑制长时漂移。相比在线基线，平均ATE降低60%以上，在KITTI、Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet和7-Scenes等数据集上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D重建模型在长视频上表现差，原因是相对于固定首帧锚点回归位姿会迫使模型在远超训练分布之外进行外推，导致微小漂移不断积累并放大为严重的几何坍塌。然而作者观察到单帧深度在这种失效中保持稳定，说明backbone的局部几何完好，只有全局位姿头崩溃，这种解耦现象启发了新方法设计。

Method: Scal3R将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询问题。具体包括：(1)使用约占参数1%的轻量级可学习token，通过非对称注意力注入完全冻结的backbone中；(2)该机制查询相对于多个过去关键帧的位姿；(3)引入带闭环的在线位姿图优化系统来抑制长距离漂移；(4)整个系统在单块GPU上8小时内达到收敛。

Result: 在KITTI上相比在线基线，平均ATE减少超过60%；在Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet和7-Scenes数据集上均取得最先进（SOTA）性能；在单GPU上8小时即可收敛，训练效率高。

Conclusion: 作者通过解耦观察发现关键的失效模式（局部深度稳定而全局位姿崩溃），利用多参考相对位姿查询替代单锚点外推，结合冻结backbone和轻量query token，有效解决了在线长视频3D重建的漂移问题。方法在多个基准上达到SOTA，计算开销低，验证了该解耦与设计范式的有效性。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenMatch，一个基于Transformer的统一模型用于3D形状对应估计。该方法在部分形状匹配数据集BeCoS上训练，无需重新训练即可泛化到完整形状匹配，通过自适应曲率引导的网格token化及自注意力和交叉注意力机制，在多个基准上取得了最优性能并实现亚秒级推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动3D形状对应估计方法在部分观测和强非等距变形下仍然表现不佳。基于学习的方法常依赖手工设计的描述子或基于模板的表示，而基于函数映射的生成模型则存在推理成本高、可解释性差以及对部分形状泛化能力不足等问题。

Method: TokenMatch是一个前馈的Transformer统一架构。其核心思想是利用形状曲率引导将网格自适应地token化为补丁，从而有效学习形状特定的几何描述子。模型使用自注意力机制学习补丁级和点级关系，并通过交叉注意力机制学习形状对之间的密集对应关系。模型仅在BeCoS（非等距部分到部分形状匹配数据集）上训练。

Result: 在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等多个部分和完整形状匹配基准上评估，TokenMatch在多数情况下于平均测地误差和交集比（IoU）指标上优于现有方法，同时推理速度更快，达到亚秒级。

Conclusion: TokenMatch展示了仅使用部分形状数据训练即可泛化到完整形状匹配的能力，验证了曲率引导的自适应token化结合Transformer注意力机制在3D形状对应估计中的有效性，为部分到部分及完整形状匹配提供了一个统一且高效的新范式。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S³T（Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time）是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态跟踪框架。它利用时间采样密度作为特权信息，让稠密采样的教师视图引导同权重的稀疏学生视图匹配其下一词元分布，从而实现无标签、无独立教师、无奖励信号的自我蒸馏训练，同时不增加推理成本。在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（模型汤化）、+2.70（结合视觉编码器适配），并且从无标签合成片段学到的能力可迁移到真实视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频状态跟踪模型依赖标注数据或外部教师/奖励信号，缺乏完全自包含的自监督学习方法。先前自进化方法在状态跟踪任务上几乎不带来提升，作者假设对同一片段更稠密的时序采样能更准确地恢复运行状态，从而可作为瘦引导信号。

Method: S³T将时间采样密度作为特权信息：稠密视图的同一视频片段作为教师，稀疏视图作为具有相同权重的学生，学生通过自蒸馏学习匹配教师的下一词元分布。模型自身生成学习目标，无需标签、独立教师网络或奖励信号。这种密集-稀疏对齐的自蒸馏目标是全自包含的，训练过程可视为一种连续视频时间上的自我蒸馏。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T使VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（配合模型汤化souping）、+2.70（配合额外视觉编码器适配）；先前的自进化方法对状态跟踪几乎不产生变化。从无标签合成视频片段中习得的能力可迁移至真实视频，在VSTAT-YouTube状态跟踪问题上提升+7.95，在MVBench Action Count上提升+4.50。

Conclusion: S³T证明仅利用时间采样密度这一自生成特权信息即可有效改进视频状态跟踪，突破了传统自监督方法对标注或外部信号的依赖，且学到的时序状态感知能力具有跨域（合成到真实）泛化性，为连续视频状态跟踪提供了一种无监督的实用训练范式。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Where Does Harness-Optimization Value Live? Localized Gains and the Budget-Splitting Trap in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02889)
*Michael Nguyen,Wei Chen Tan,Nurul Aisyah Hassan,Arvind Raman,Li Hua Lim,Ahmad Faiz Razak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HARNESSEVO框架，将LLM智能体的"驾驭层"(harness)分解为角色、任务策略、工具/格式规则、反思/控制四个独立可进化的插槽，并通过留一进/留一出归因分析每个插槽的贡献。在ALFWorld上发现几乎所有优化价值都集中在反思/控制插槽(+0.119)，其他插槽均无效；均匀预算切分甚至有害(导致预算低于搜索下限而全部冻结)，将预算集中于高信用插槽可恢复性能(0.761)。在WebShop上所有插槽冻结为空，表明任务依赖此效应的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反思式提示进化方法通常将整个文字脚手架(harnes)作为单一平铺字符串进行优化，没有区分优化价值在不同组件(角色、策略、格式规则、控制)中的实际分布。作者希望在结构化进化之前先做信用分配，理解优化价值的真正所在。

Method: 将harnes分解为四个可分别进化的插槽：角色、任务策略、工具/格式规则、反思/控制。在同一反思式优化器下进行iso-budget设置，使用leave-one-in与leave-one-out归因方法量化每个插槽的边际贡献。在ALFWorld(7B冻结骨干)与WebShop上验证，并考察预算分配策略(均匀切分vs集中分配)的影响。

Result: ALFWorld上HARNESSEVO整体成功率(0.657)较stock harness(0.642)与flat-string进化(0.642)无显著提升；但插槽级分析显示绝大多数价值集中在反思/控制插槽(+0.119 leave-one-in)，其余插槽均无效。均匀分配预算有害：64次rollout分配至四个插槽各16次低于优化器有效搜索下限，导致全部冻结为空；集中预算于高信用插槽在减半预算下达到0.761。WebShop上所有插槽均冻结为空且所有方法打平，表明该任务缺乏可循环、可言语化的控制失败，而非预算饥饿。

Conclusion: harnes优化价值具有局部性(高度集中而非均匀分布)；均匀预算切分可能主动有害；结构化智能体进化之前应先进行信用分配。优化效果是任务依赖的，并非所有任务都适合此类反射式harnes进化。

Abstract: A growing body of work improves frozen large language models (LLMs) as agents by evolving their harness: the textual scaffolding around the model, including persona, strategy, format rules, and control heuristics. Existing reflective prompt-evolution methods usually optimize this harness as one flat string. We instead ask where the optimization value actually resides. We introduce HARNESSEVO, which decomposes the harness into four separately evolvable slots: role, task-strategy, tool/format-rules, and reflection/control. Using the same reflective optimizer under an iso-budget setting, we pair this decomposition with leave-one-in and leave-one-out attribution to measure the contribution of each slot.
  On ALFWorld with a frozen 7B backbone, HARNESSEVO does not significantly improve the overall binary success rate over either the stock harness or flat-string evolution: 0.657 versus 0.642 and 0.642, respectively. However, the slot-level analysis reveals that nearly all useful optimization value is localized in the reflection/control slot, which achieves a leave-one-in gain of +0.119. The other slots are individually null. We further show that uniform budget splitting is harmful: allocating 64 rollouts across four slots leaves only 16 per slot, below the optimizer's effective search floor, causing every slot to freeze at its empty seed. Concentrating the budget on the high-credit control slot recovers the lost gain, reaching 0.761 with half the split budget.
  The effect is task-contingent. On WebShop, all slots freeze empty and all methods tie, indicating a genuine absence of recurrent, verbalizable control failures rather than budget starvation. Overall, our results suggest that harness value is localized, uniform budget splitting can be actively harmful, and credit assignment should precede structured agent-evolution.

</details>


### [104] [Bounded Personas Match Retrieval on Classification but Not Regression for a Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02890)
*JaeHa Yoon,Minjun Park,Seoyeon Kim,Jiwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Choi,Dohyun Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaLink——一种无需训练的个性化语言智能体方法，将用户交互历史蒸馏为有界的三字段人设（persona），并通过递归自评估精炼（在用户自己的标注历史上用冻结的7B骨干模型自我评估、重写人设、仅在无回退时保留）。在LaMP-2（15路新闻分类）上，PersonaLink达到0.745-0.755准确率，与BM25检索（0.760-0.765）在统计上无显著差异，首次清晰刻画了蒸馏型人设在何种任务上可匹敌检索型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言智能体的两种主流策略——检索（retrieval）与蒸馏（distillation）——之间存在未解决的权衡：检索准确但每查询需付出随历史增长的选择与上下文成本；蒸馏紧凑、有界、免查询且有可解释性，但普遍被认为牺牲精度。此前尚无人清晰刻画蒸馏人设在哪些任务上能匹配检索的性能，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: PersonaLink是训练无关的蒸馏方法：将用户历史压缩为有界的三字段人设，并通过递归精炼流程迭代优化——每一轮在用户自己的标注历史切片上自我评估冻结的agent，基于错误重写人设，且仅当其在切片上不回退时才保留新版本。所有比较共用同一冻结的7B骨干模型，仅上下文内容不同，从而隔离了"表征方式"与"模型能力"的影响。

Result: 在LaMP-2基准的200个用户上（15路新闻分类任务），PersonaLink达到0.745-0.755的准确率，与BM25检索方法（0.760-0.765）在统计上无法区分，表明蒸馏型人设在分类任务上可追平甚至匹敌检索型方法，呈现出清晰的任务类型不对称性。

Conclusion: 结果表明"蒸馏人设必然不如检索"的普遍假设并不成立——至少在分类这类任务上蒸馏可以达到与检索相当的性能。设计上通过冻结同一骨干模型来隔离表征影响，使结论具有方法论上的可靠性。这一发现为个性化agent在有界、可解释且免查询代价的人设与精确但高成本检索之间做选择提供了新的实证依据。

Abstract: A personalized language agent must convert a user's interaction history into behavior on each new request at inference time. Two strategies dominate. Retrieval pulls a few of the user's most relevant past items into the prompt, which is accurate but pays a per-query selection and context cost that grows with the history. Distillation instead compresses the history once into a compact natural-language persona, which is bounded, query-independent, and interpretable, but is widely assumed to sacrifice accuracy. Whether, and on which tasks, a distilled persona can match retrieval has not been characterized cleanly. We introduce PersonaLink, a training-free method that distills a user's history into a bounded three-field persona and recursively refines it: each pass self-evaluates the frozen agent on a held-out slice of the user's own labeled history, rewrites the persona from its errors, and keeps the result only when it does not regress on that slice. Because every comparison shares one frozen 7B backbone and differs only in what is placed in context, the design isolates the effect of representation from that of the model. The result is a clear task-type asymmetry. On 200 users of LaMP-2 (15-way news categorization), PersonaLink reaches 0.745-0.755 accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from BM25 retrieval (0.760-0.765).

</details>


### [105] [Counterexamples as Feedback for Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02892)
*Sidhesh Badrinarayan,Adithya Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A-CEGIS是一个轻量级框架，利用反例作为反馈来评估自然语言到正则表达式合成中的多轮迭代改进能力。在30个NL-RX-Turk任务上，诊断性反例反馈在四轮预算内解决了90%的任务（对比零样本生成的17%、通用自纠错的27%和仅错误反馈的23%），完整诊断运行最终解决了隐藏集上的全部任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单轮代码生成指标无法反映部署agent的一个核心能力——在收到具体反馈后能否修复错误产物（artifact）。因此需要一个能评估多轮细化（multi-turn refinement）能力的框架。

Method: 提出A-CEGIS框架：(1) agent提出正则表达式；(2) 确定性oracle在全匹配语义下检查；(3) 生成紧凑的假阳性或假阴性反例作为下一轮的反馈。采用四轮消融预算评估，并加入hardening机制进行完整诊断运行。

Result: 在30个NL-RX-Turk任务上，A-CEGIS以90%的任务解决率（四轮预算内）显著优于零样本生成（17%）、通用自纠错（27%）和仅错误反馈（23%）。完整诊断运行在最终轮解决了隐藏集上的全部任务，平均2.7轮达到成功，在定向探测后鲁棒成功率为77%。

Conclusion: A-CEGIS能有效衡量agent在多轮交互中的改进效率，并为原始保留（held-out）用例之外提供了实用的鲁棒性检查机制，凸显了反例反馈在多轮agent评估中的价值。

Abstract: Single-turn code-generation metrics understate a central property of deployed agents: whether they can repair a wrong artifact after receiving concrete feedback. This paper presents A-CEGIS, a lightweight framework that uses counterexamples as feedback for evaluating multi-turn refinement in natural-language-to-regex synthesis. An agent proposes a regex, a deterministic oracle checks it under full-match semantics, and compact false-positive or false-negative witnesses guide the next turn. On 30 NL-RX-Turk tasks, diagnostic counterexample feedback solves 90\% of tasks within a four-turn ablation budget, compared with 17% for zero-shot generation, 27% for generic self-correction, and 23% for error-only feedback. In a full diagnostic run with hardening, all tasks are solved on the hidden set by the final turn, with mean time-to-success of 2.7 turns and robust success of 77% after targeted probing. These results show that A-CEGIS measures how efficiently an agent improves across turns while adding a practical robustness check beyond the original held-out cases.

</details>


### [106] [Probe Generalization as Subspace Selection for OOD Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02893)
*Daniel Yoo,Adrians Skapars*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究线性探针（linear probes）在语言模型激活上的跨域泛化问题。通过在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上对3个欺骗检测数据集的实验，作者发现将激活投影到训练分布的主成分（PCs）子集上可实现近乎与直接训练于测试分布相当的性能。进一步利用LLM法官对PC的语义解释进行筛选，可缩小基线到oracle差距的25%-78%。研究表明探针的OOD鲁棒性主要由子空间选择决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针能用于检测语言模型激活中的行为与概念，但在分布外（out-of-distribution, OOD）样本上可能无法迁移。理解探针何时以及为何能够跨域泛化，对于构建可靠、可迁移的行为检测机制至关重要。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上训练线性探针于欺骗检测数据集，然后在3个held-out数据集上评估跨域迁移表现。方法包括：(1) 将激活投影到训练分布中少量主成分上以提升跨域迁移；(2) 使用LLM法官对每个PC的最高/最低激活示例进行语义评分，判断其是否蕴含可迁移的欺骗方向；(3) 在最高得分的PC上训练探针。

Result: 投影到少量PC上使跨域迁移性能几乎匹配直接在测试分布上训练的探针。通过LLM法官筛选可迁移PC再训练探针，在Insider Trading Report上闭合了baseline-to-oracle差距的78%，在Sandbagging上闭合了25%。源探针权重集中的方向编码源特定的表面特征，而真正可迁移的方向以更抽象的方式编码同一对比，且能被自然语言描述捕捉。

Conclusion: 探针的OOD鲁棒性很大程度上取决于子空间（subspace）的选择。选择编码抽象概念的PC（而非源特定的表面特征方向）是实现跨域迁移的关键。这为可迁移的线性行为探针设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Linear probes can be used to detect behaviors and concepts inside language model activations, but may fail to transfer to out-of-distribution examples. When studying the generalization performance of Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct probes over 3 held-out deception detection datasets, we find that projecting inputs onto a small subset of principal components (PCs) from the training distribution of activations enables cross-domain transfer that nearly matches the performance of probes trained directly on the test distribution. Furthermore, we find that PC interpretations can be used to find a subset of those transferable PCs. By using an LLM judge to score each PC on whether its most/ least activating examples imply a transferable deception direction, then probing on the highest-scoring PCs, we close the baseline-to-oracle gap by 78% on Insider Trading Report and by 25% on Sandbagging. The directions a source probe weights heavily appear to encode source-specific surface features, while the directions that actually transfer appear to encode the same contrast more abstractly, in a way natural language descriptions can capture. Broadly, our results suggest that the OOD robustness of probes is largely determined by subspace selection.

</details>


### [107] [R$^{2}$Adapter: A Routing and Rewriting Adapter for Efficient Hybrid RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02894)
*Yucan Guo,Miao Su,Saiping Guan,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Zixuan Li,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出R²Adapter，一种轻量级即插即用的路由与重写适配器，用于在vanilla RAG和graph-based RAG之间动态分配查询，减少不必要的图检索开销，同时通过重写不确定的图路由查询来提高检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla RAG处理简单查询高效但难以应对关系型或多跳推理；Graph-based RAG虽能解决该问题但推理复杂度和延迟更高。现实用户查询复杂度差异大，固定的RAG策略并非最优，而现有的混合文本-图RAG方法依赖启发式或基于LLM的路由，导致不必要开销且过度依赖底层LLM。

Method: 提出R²Adapter，一个轻量级的即插即用路由与重写适配器：(1) 通过路由机制仅将真正需要图推理的查询分配给graph-based RAG，减少不必要的图检索开销；(2) 对不确定的图路由查询进行重写，以更好地暴露其多跳推理需求，提高检索质量且无需额外监督。该适配器是模型无关的，可无缝集成到多种vanilla和graph-based RAG流水线中。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上的大量实验表明，R²Adapter将graph-based RAG的使用率降低了最多59%，同时保持了可比的答案准确性。

Conclusion: R²Adapter为混合RAG系统提供了一种高效且自适应的解决方案，通过智能路由和重写机制在保证回答准确性的前提下显著降低图检索开销，且具有模型无关性和良好的可集成性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a prevailing paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with non-parametric knowledge. Vanilla RAG efficiently handles simple queries but struggles with relational or multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based RAG alleviates this issue but incurs higher inference complexity and latency. In practice, user queries can differ significantly in their complexity, rendering a fixed RAG strategy suboptimal. However, existing hybrid text-graph RAG methods typically rely on heuristic and LLM-based routing, resulting in unnecessary overhead and strong dependence on the underlying LLM. To address these challenges, we propose R$^{2}$Adapter, a lightweight plug-in Routing and Rewriting Adapter designed to allocate queries between vanilla and graph-based RAG dynamically. By routing only the queries that genuinely benefit from graph-based reasoning, R$^{2}$Adapter reduces unnecessary graph retrieval overhead. Additionally, uncertain graph-routed queries are rewritten to better expose their multi-hop reasoning requirements, improving retrieval quality without additional supervision. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that R$^{2}$Adapter reduces graph-based RAG usage by up to 59% while maintaining comparable answer accuracy. This adapter is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into diverse vanilla and graph-based RAG pipelines, providing an efficient and adaptive solution for hybrid RAG systems.

</details>


### [108] [BharatGather: A Culturally-Informed Benchmark Dataset for Misinformation and Fake News Detection in Indian Public Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02895)
*Parth Bramhecha,Smit Deshmukh,Sairaj Bodhale,Adwait Borate,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BharatGather，一个专为印度大型公共集会场景中的二元错误信息分类而设计的多源数据集，包含14,646条记录，通过网页抓取、多媒体转录提取和LLM合成增强的混合流程构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型公共事件（如宗教节日、政治集会、文化聚会）日益容易受到错误信息快速传播的影响，对公共安全和社会凝聚力构成重大风险。现有的自动假新闻检测基准未能捕捉印度情境特有的社会文化细微差别和事件特定动态，亟需一个针对印度大规模集会场域定制的资源。

Method: 采用混合pipeline构建语料库：(1)系统化抓取主流事实核查平台的网页数据；(2)提取多媒体转录内容；(3)利用大语言模型（LLM）进行合成增强以保障叙事多样性。最终建成包含14,646条记录的数据集，专为二元错误信息分类任务设计。

Result: 产出了一个针对印度大规模集会场域的多源数据集BharatGather，其语料库内含14,646条记录，并建立了一个用于评估文化感知检测系统在高压公共环境中性能的严格基准。

Conclusion: BharatGather通过提供针对印度事件感知错误信息独特复杂性的资源，促进了文化感知检测系统的开发，并为评估其在高风险公共环境中的表现建立了严格基准。

Abstract: Large-scale public events, such as religious festivals, political rallies, and cultural gatherings, are increasingly vulnerable to the rapid dissemination of misinformation, posing substantial risks to public safety and social cohesion. While automated fake news detection has seen significant methodological progress, existing benchmarks frequently fail to capture the socio-cultural nuances and event-specific dynamics characteristic of the Indian context. This paper introduces BharatGather, a curated, multi-source dataset specifically engineered for binary misinformation classification within the ecosystem of Indian mass gatherings. The corpus comprises 14,646 records constructed through a hybrid pipeline involving systematic web scraping of prominent fact-checking platforms, multimedia transcript extraction, and Large Language Model (LLM)-mediated synthetic augmentation to ensure narrative diversity. By providing a resource tailored to the unique complexities of event-aware misinformation in India, this work facilitates the development of culturally informed detection systems and establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating their performance in high-stakes public environments.

</details>


### [109] [PiPMRE: A Pipeline Based on Language Model for Medical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02896)
*Jiaxin Duan,Fengyu Lu,Junfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型的流水线框架PiPMRE用于医学关系抽取，通过关系生成器和关系过滤器两步完成，无需设计标签模式，在两个公开数据集上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往将医学关系抽取视为序列标注任务的方法，要么需要设计复杂的标签方案，要么因医学实体间关系复杂而无法抽取多重关系。作者从语言学视角重新审视该任务，希望避免标签模式设计的局限。

Method: 提出PiPMRE流水线框架，包含两部分：关系生成器（利用简单模板对输入文本进行重构，使实体和关系按上下文顺序生成多个关系三元组）和关系过滤器（对每个三元组打分，仅保留通过边界的作为最终结果）。无需标签模式。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，PiPMRE相比先前最优方法平均提升了5.6个召回率点和4.4个准确率点，且在小样本（few-shot）设置下同样表现出优越性。

Conclusion: PiPMRE通过语言生成与过滤相结合的方式有效提升了医学关系抽取性能，避免了标签模式设计的缺陷，并在多种实验设置下验证了其先进性。

Abstract: Medical relation extraction (MRE) is commonly known for extracting entities and their relations jointly from a medical text, which has attracted considerable attention in recent years. Previous studies treat MRE as a sequence tagging task, which results in either a challenging design of the tagging schema or a failed extraction of multiple relations, due to intricate relationships among medical entities. In this work, we review the task from the linguistic perspective and propose a novel pipeline framework, PiPMRE, developed on language models to enhance MRE performance. Specifically, PiPMRE consists of a relation generator and a relation filter. Given a text, the generator first yields multiple relational triplets, and then the filter scores each triplet and retains only those that pass the borderline as the final results. Implementing PiPMRE requires no tagging schema; instead, we use a simple template to reformulate the input text, ensuring that entities and relations are generated in a contextual order. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate the advancement of PiPMRE. It surpasses the previous state-of-the-art by an average of 5.6 recall points and 4.4 accuracy points. PiPMRE's superiority is also demonstrated in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [110] [The Analyst in the Prompt: Role, Retrieval, and Memory Biases in LLM Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03218)
*Ahmed Asaad,Amr Mohamed,Yang Zhang,Omneya Abdelsalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在个性化用户语境（如记忆、个人资料、角色提示）下的证据判断偏差问题。通过在3,575份SEC文件中测试十二种LLM，作者发现用户语境对证据判断的影响主要来自模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解读差异，而非检索到不同证据。两种缓解策略（将投资理念表述为用户档案而非助手角色，以及分离基于证据与个性化的输出）虽能减少偏差但无法完全消除，且效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地利用用户语境（如记忆、个人资料和角色提示）来个性化响应，但个性化可能影响基于证据的判断——相同的证据在不同用户语境下可能导致不同结论。金融领域（特别是SEC文件解读）是一个高风险的场景，值得系统研究这一问题的严重程度及其来源。

Method: 使用3,575份SEC文件，在十二种不同的LLM上进行实验。通过对比三类设置——人物角色条件化的检索（persona-conditioned retrieval）、中性检索（neutral retrieval）和记忆框架化语境（memory-framed context）——来分离证据选择效应与解读效应的影响。同时测试两种缓解策略：将投资理念表述为用户档案而非助手角色，以及将基于证据的输出与个性化输出分离。

Result: 大多数用户语境溢出效应来自模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解读差异，而非证据检索的差异。两种缓解策略均能减少溢出效应，但无法完全消除，且其有效性在不同模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: 用户语境对LLM证据判断的影响主要通过解读渠道而非证据选择渠道产生；现有缓解策略部分有效但不够全面，模型的个性化设计与证据完整性之间存在需要权衡的张力，需进一步研究更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly use user context such as memory, profiles, and role prompts to personalize their responses. This personalization can affect evidence-based judgment: the same evidence may lead to different conclusions under different user contexts. Finance provides a high-stakes setting to study this problem because decisions often depend on interpreting long and complex documents. We test this using 3,575 SEC filings across twelve LLMs. We compare persona-conditioned retrieval, neutral retrieval, and memory-framed context to separate the effect of evidence selection from the effect of interpretation. We find that most user-context spillover comes from how models interpret the same evidence under different roles, rather than from retrieving different evidence. We then test two simple mitigation strategies: expressing the same investor mindset as a user profile instead of an assistant role, and separating evidence-based and personalized outputs. Both reduce spillover, but neither removes it completely, and their effectiveness varies substantially across models.

</details>


### [111] [Margins, Not Windows: Training-Free Per-Step Lossy Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02897)
*Oszkár Urbán,Young D. Kwon,Stylianos I. Venieris,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptiveSpec，一种无需训练的逐步骤投机解码方法，通过每步自适应调整验证规则（宽松token匹配容错）和草稿树结构（深度/宽度/节点数）来同时优化投机解码的两个固定决策，相比EAGLE-3在SGLang引擎上吞吐量最高提升56%，任务准确率恢复93%至完全无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有树注意力投机解码方法（如EAGLE-3）通常将两个关键决策固定：(1)严格的token匹配验证规则和(2)静态草稿树形状。先前工作仅在受限假设下单独放宽某一决策——要么依赖长草稿链进行无需训练的有损验证，要么在固定token预算下进行自适应树形调整。缺乏能同时、逐步骤地自适应调整这两个正交轴的方法。

Method: AdaptiveSpec无需训练，逐步骤同时自适应两个决策：(1)每步margin规则——当目标模型在草稿token上的概率与其top-1概率之比超过阈值时允许不匹配的草稿token被采纳，该规则不依赖草稿链长度或底层草稿器架构；(2)每步树策略——利用草稿top-1置信度与滚动接受历史（反映近期草稿-目标一致性的融合信号）动态调整草稿树的深度、宽度和节点数，使总草稿数量可变而非仅被重新分配。两个自适应机制作用在正交轴上，效果可叠加。

Result: 在SGLang生产级推理引擎上实现，相比最先进的回归式投机解码方法EAGLE-3，吞吐量最高提升56%；在GSM8K、MATH-500和HumanEval三个基准上以及三个目标模型（DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B）上恢复了93%至完全无损的任务准确率。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSpec证明通过从解码过程中已有的内部信号驱动两个正交维度的逐步骤自适应，可以在无需训练的前提下同时获得更高的吞吐量和几乎无损的任务准确率，突破了现有方法将验证规则与树结构固定或仅在约束下单独优化的局限。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting candidate tokens and verifying them in parallel. Tree-attention drafters such as EAGLE-3 are widely adopted, yet typically hold two decisions fixed: (1) a strict token-match verification rule and (2) a static draft-tree shape. Prior work relaxes each in isolation under limiting assumptions: long draft chains for training-free lossy verification, and adaptive tree shaping under a fixed token budget. We introduce AdaptiveSpec, a training-free per-step speculative decoding method that adapts both decisions from internal signals already produced during decoding. A per-step margin rule promotes a mismatched draft-proposed token when the ratio of the target's probability on the drafted token to its top-1 probability exceeds a threshold with no dependence on draft length or underlying drafter architecture. A per-step tree policy adjusts the draft tree's depth, width, and node count directly from a fused signal of draft top-1 confidence and a rolling acceptance history capturing recent draft-target agreement, allowing the total draft count to vary rather than only be redistributed. The two adaptations operate on orthogonal axes and compound in effect. Implemented on the SGLang production-grade serving engine, AdaptiveSpec improves throughput over the state-of-the-art autoregressive speculative decoding method EAGLE-3 by up to 56%, recovering 93% to fully lossless task accuracy across GSM8K, MATH-500, and HumanEval on three target models (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B).

</details>


### [112] [Enhancing Financial Question Answering: A Novel Benchmark Dataset of Banks' financial statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03654)
*Arianna Miola,Bruno Spaccavento,Lorenzo Silotto,Marco Bianchetti,Luca Cagliero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinRAG-QA，一个包含999个从业者构建问题、基于209份2019-2023年欧洲和美国24家主要银行年报及第三支柱报告的新型金融问答基准数据集。作者评估了多阶段RAG流水线中各组件贡献，发现上下文分块增强与检索优化嵌入模型将NDCG@10从0.322提升至0.710，推理优化生成器在正确检索条件下将准确率提升34.4个百分点，并发现交叉编码器重排序会降低检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融问答基准集中于美国申报文件和单机构分析，缺乏跨机构检索能力的评估。银行财务报表分析复杂、冗长且异构，对自动化问答系统构成重大挑战，亟需一个更贴近实际的高质量基准数据集。

Method: 构建包含999个从业者策划问题的FinRAG-QA基准，覆盖10个标准化指标，基于24家银行2019-2023年的209份报告（平均19.8万字）。评估多阶段RAG流水线，分别隔离并测量各组件贡献。

Result: 上下文分块增强与检索优化嵌入模型结合使NDCG@10从0.322提升至0.710；在正确检索的前提下，推理优化生成器将答案准确率从44.6%提升至79.0%（+34.4个百分点），但生成延迟约为20倍；交叉编码器重排序在第一阶段排序已强时反而降低检索性能。

Conclusion: 针对超长、跨机构的金融文档，检索质量主要由分块策略和嵌入模型决定，而非重排序；生成阶段需要更强大的推理优化模型。FinRAG-QA为金融QA研究提供了更具挑战性和现实意义的基准。

Abstract: The comparative analysis of banks' financial statements poses significant challenges for automated question answering systems due to their complexity, substantial length, technical language, and inhomogeneity of both textual and numerical content across different jurisdictions and institutions. We introduce FinRAG-QA, a novel benchmark dataset for financial question answering, which comprises 999 practitioner-curated questions on 10 standardised indicators, grounded in 209 annual and Pillar 3 reports from 24 major European and U.S. banks spanning 2019-2023. Unlike prior financial QA benchmarks, which centre on U.S. filings and single-institution analysis, FinRAG-QA targets cross-institutional retrieval over documents averaging 198k words, longer than any existing financial QA resource. On this benchmark we evaluate a multi-stage RAG pipeline and isolate the contribution of each component. Contextual chunk enrichment combined with a retrieval-optimised embedding model raises NDCG@10 from 0.322 to 0.710; conditional on the ground truth being retrieved, a reasoning-optimised generator raises answer accuracy from 44.6% to 79.0% (+34.4 percentage points), at roughly 20x the generation latency. We further show that cross-encoder reranking degrades retrieval when the first-stage ranking is already strong, and that a single top-ranked chunk outperforms larger contexts at generation time. Experiments were run in late 2024-early 2025 with the models available at that time.

</details>


### [113] [Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET): Parameter-Efficient Biomedical Domain Adaptation via Priority-Based Embedding Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02898)
*Girish Sundaram,Daniel Berleant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DRET（Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer），一种知识迁移范式，将大模型（如BioBERT/ClinicalBERT）的生物医学领域知识注入小型通用模型（DistilBERT），而无需在原始专业语料上重新训练。DRET经多代迭代（令牌合并、混合嵌入平均、优先级嵌入迁移、嵌入层冻结/差异学习率/标签传播/不平衡感知损失），在EBM-NLP语料PICO分类任务上，使66M参数的DistilBERT达到与规模大一个数量级的模型相竞争甚至超越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型生物医学领域语言模型（BioBERT、ClinicalBERT）虽表现优异但计算成本过高，难以实际部署；而通用参数高效模型（DistilBERT）轻量却缺乏专业领域知识。二者的矛盾催生了对一种既能保持轻量高效、又能获得医学领域知识的小型模型方案的强烈需求。

Method: DRET作为迭代替换的知识迁移策略家族：DRET 1.x为统一分词器合并；DRET 2.0为混合嵌入平均；DRET 3.x为按优先级从最具权威源模型分层选择嵌入的优先级嵌入迁移机制；DRET 4.x进一步融合嵌入层冻结、差异学习率、标签传播和不平衡感知损失函数。评估采用EBM-NLP语料的token级PICO分类，在严重类不平衡条件下使用十二指标矩阵。

Result: DRET增强的DistilBERT（66M参数）在平衡准确率、召回率和ROC-AUC上与比其大一个数量级的模型竞争，且在若干类级指标上超过这些大模型，同时保留DistilBERT的高效性。余弦相似度、语义偏移和t-SNE分析证明迁移确实在嵌入层面发生。

Conclusion: DRET提供了可扩展、资源高效地接近领域专家级性能的路径，适用于生物医学文本挖掘，可直接用于自动化系统文献综述与临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large domain-specific language models such as BioBERT and ClinicalBERT achieve strong performance on biomedical NLP tasks, but their computational demands make them impractical for many real-world deployments. General-purpose, parameter-efficient models such as DistilBERT are lightweight yet lack the domain knowledge required for specialized tasks such as PICO (Population, Intervention, Comparison, Outcome) classification. We introduce Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET), a knowledge-transfer paradigm that injects biomedical domain knowledge from large specialized models into a smaller general-purpose model without retraining on the original specialized corpora. DRET is developed as an iterative family of strategies: a unified tokenizer-merge strategy (DRET 1.x), hybrid embedding averaging (DRET 2.0), and a priority-based embedding-transfer mechanism (DRET 3.x) that hierarchically selects embeddings from the most authoritative source models, further combined with embedding-layer freezing, differential learning rates, label propagation, and imbalance-aware loss functions (DRET 4.x). We evaluate DRET on token-level PICO classification using the EBM-NLP corpus under severe class imbalance, across a twelve-metric battery. DRET-enhanced DistilBERT (66M parameters) attains balanced accuracy, recall, and ROC-AUC competitive with, and on several class-wise metrics exceeding, models an order of magnitude larger, while retaining DistilBERT's efficiency. We further show that transfer occurs at the embedding level through cosine-similarity, semantic-shift, and t-SNE analyses. DRET offers a scalable, resource-efficient route to near-domain-expert performance for biomedical text mining, with direct application to automated systematic literature reviews and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [114] [Contamination Inflates Scores but Rarely Reorders Large Language Model Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02899)
*Xingyao Xiao,Yihong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了大语言模型基准测试中的污染问题，将其区分为"绝对分数虚高"与"模型排名重排"两个不同问题，并提出了一种基于锚定项不变性的测量方法（原始题目与语义等价改写题的对比）。通过对47个公开模型和74个人为污染模型的测试发现：公开模型中的污染基本是均匀的，虽然会虚高绝对分数但几乎不会改变排名（秩相关0.997），只有罕见的差异化污染才能导致排名扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准污染（测试题目泄露到训练数据中）被广泛认为威胁LLM排行榜的可靠性。但现有讨论混淆了"污染是否虚高绝对分数"和"污染是否改变模型排名"两个不同问题。作者希望澄清这一混淆，并提供一个可校准、可量化的测量方法来判断污染对排行榜的实际影响。

Method: 作者将污染重新建模为锚定项不变性的违反，通过比较原始题目与语义等价改写题目的表现差异来测量（置于同一技能水平，隔离记忆与能力）。在四个基准（ARC、GSM8K、HellaSwag、MMLU）上，对47个公开模型和74个带已知污染剂量微调的模型进行校准，验证测量方法的剂量-响应有效性，随后量化对排行榜的实际影响。

Result: 校准实验表明该方法能剂量-响应式地恢复注入的污染（测试集泄露的校正效应为+0.187准确率点），且对仅用合法训练集训练的阴性对照模型无误报（-0.012）。标准排行榜与改写控制排行榜的秩相关为0.997；敏感性分析显示观测到的差异化污染远低于改变排名所需的水平，188个模型-基准组合中仅3例表现出跨两个参照得到验证的差异化污染。

Conclusion: 公开LLM模型间的污染大体上是均匀的：它会虚高绝对分数但不会重排排行榜。排名失真需要罕见的差异化污染。作者建议排行榜应同时报告改写控制的排名与置信区间，并提供校准后的不变性审计作为参考实现。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination, the leakage of test items into training data, is widely described as a threat to the reliability of large language model (LLM) leaderboards. We argue that this concern conflates two distinct questions: whether contamination inflates absolute scores, and whether it reorders the ranking of models. We recast contamination as a violation of anchor-item invariance and measure it through the differential functioning of original versus semantically equivalent paraphrased items, a within-item contrast that holds the measured skill fixed and isolates memorization from capability. Using per-instance responses from 47 publicly released models and 74 models finetuned with a known dose of contamination, across four benchmarks (ARC, GSM8K, HellaSwag, MMLU), we first calibrate the measure against ground truth: it recovers injected contamination dose-responsively (a corrected effect of +0.187 accuracy points for test-set leakage) and never flags a negative-control model trained only on the legitimate training split (-0.012). We then quantify leaderboard impact: the rank correlation between a standard leaderboard and a paraphrase-controlled leaderboard is 0.997, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the observed differential contamination is far below the level needed to move rankings, with only 3 of 188 model-by-benchmark cases showing differential contamination corroborated across two references. Contamination among these public models is therefore largely uniform: it inflates absolute scores without reordering the leaderboard, and ranking distortion requires the rare case of differential contamination. We provide a calibrated invariance audit, released as a reference implementation, and recommend that leaderboards report paraphrase-controlled rankings alongside confidence intervals.

</details>


### [115] [Dual-Form ASR: Semantics-Aware Inverse Text Normalization for Chinese Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02901)
*Fengrun Zhang,Li Fu,Wangjin Zhou,Lu Fan,Youzheng Wu,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-ASR框架，通过成对的语音形式与书写形式监督数据，将语义感知的逆文本正则化能力扩展到语音识别模型中，同时保留提示词级别的转录形式选择，在中文基准上优于开源级联ASR-ITN系统并与强闭源参考系统性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统级联式ASR-ITN系统中，语音形式转录与书写形式转录由分离模块产生，导致：1) 分隔的ITN组件对ASR识别错误敏感，易于传递错误；2) 规范化过程与声学-上下文建模解耦，难以处理语义上依赖的数字表达（如"邮编"、"金额"等语义相关数字的规范化）。

Method: DF-ASR框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 通过配对语音形式与书写形式的监督信号扩展语音识别模型，同时在提示词级别保留转录形式选择能力；(2) 使用大语言模型驱动的"生成-评判"工作流自动构建双形式监督数据；(3) 引入ITN-MWER序列级训练目标，对规范化敏感片段上的错误赋予更高代价。此外提出决策感知的REQUIRE-ITN/FORBID-ITN评估协议，分别衡量必要规范化和禁用规范化的保留程度。

Result: 在SpeechIO人工标注中文子集上，DF-ASR一致优于开源级联ASR-ITN系统，与强闭源参考系统具有竞争力，并在语音形式与书写形式输出之间保持了可靠的提示词级控制能力。

Conclusion: DF-ASR证明了将语义感知ITN能力与语音识别集成在统一框架中的可行性和有效性，通过联合双形式监督和序列级优化，既改善了规范化质量又保持了双形式输出的灵活可控性，为该方向提供了一种替代级联架构的端到端方案。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) scenarios require both spoken-form transcripts for faithful transcription and readable written-form transcripts with inverse text normalization (ITN). However, these forms are typically produced by cascaded modules, where a spoken-form ASR output is rewritten by a separate ITN component, making written-form ASR-ITN vulnerable to recognition errors and decoupling normalization from acoustic-contextual modeling, especially for semantically dependent numeric expressions. In this paper, we propose Dual-Form ASR (DF-ASR), a framework that extends spoken-form ASR capability to semantics-aware written-form ITN through paired spoken-form and written-form supervision while retaining prompt-level selection between transcript forms. The dual-form supervision is constructed via a large language model (LLM)-driven generate-and-judge workflow, and training is further enhanced by ITN-MWER, a sequence-level objective that assigns higher cost to errors on normalization-sensitive spans. We also introduce a decision-aware REQUIRE-ITN/\FORBID-ITN protocol to separately measure required normalization and forbidden-span preservation. On manually annotated Chinese subsets from SpeechIO, DF-ASR consistently outperforms open-source ASR-ITN systems, remains competitive with strong closed-source references, and preserves reliable prompt-level control between spoken-form and written-form outputs.

</details>


### [116] [RL-ADA: A World-Feedback Framework for Adversarially Robust Enterprise Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02902)
*Ram Narayanan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RL-ADA框架，通过客服智能体（DA）与对抗性客户智能体（CA）在无人类标注的共进化对抗竞技场中协同训练，以"世界反馈"（结果性奖励）替代人工标注。CA学习策略屏蔽真实意图，制造误路由；隔离训练场基于先前失败记录迭代重训较弱智能体。在银行客服试点中，五次共进化循环后工具路由错误清零、端到端PASS率翻倍，并自发涌现出CA的"上下文伪装"对抗策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向企业客服的任务型对话系统面临持续的标注瓶颈：大规模高质量训练依赖大量人工标注交互数据，但企业对话日志涉及隐私且标注昂贵，同时用户行为演化速度快于标注流水线更新速度，亟需消除人工标注依赖的训练方法。

Method: 提出RL-ADA共进化训练框架：①由DA（3B参数客服智能体）与CA（7B参数对抗客户智能体）构成对抗竞技场，二者以相反但独立结构化的奖励驱动不对称对抗压力——DA因成功解决多轮对话获奖励，CA因生成逼真且隐藏意图、诱发误路由的话术获奖励；②奖励信号来自固定自动评判器给出的"世界反馈"，即源于可测量交互结果的结果性信号，完全取代人类标签；③隔离训练场在每轮后针对弱者的失败记录进行迭代重训，全程无需人工标注。

Result: 在银行客服概念验证中，经过五次共进化循环，工具路由错误完全消除，严格端到端PASS率翻倍，全程仅依赖自动竞技场奖励、无任何标注数据；同时观察到"上下文伪装"这一对抗策略的自发涌现——CA仅凭奖励压力便学会将真实意图嵌入密集逼真的客户细节中，对企业红队测试与鲁棒性评估具有直接启示。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了通过无标注的共进化对抗训练可有效突破对话智能体训练的数据标注瓶颈，证明了基于结果性"世界反馈"的奖励机制足以驱动复杂对话策略的学习与进化，并在对抗过程中自发涌现出具有红队评估价值的高级对抗策略。

Abstract: Deploying task-oriented dialogue agents in enterprise customer support faces a persistent annotation bottleneck: robust training requires labelled interaction data at scale, yet enterprise conversational logs are privacy-sensitive and expensive to annotate, while user behaviour evolves faster than labelling pipelines can keep pace. We present RL-ADA (Reinforcement Learning with Adversarial Dialogue Agents), a co-evolutionary training framework that eliminates this bottleneck by replacing human labels with \emph{world feedback}: consequence-based reward signals derived directly from measurable interaction outcomes. A Customer Support Agent (DA, 3B parameters) and an Adversarial Customer Agent (CA, 7B parameters) co-evolve in an adversarial arena guided by a fixed automated judge: the DA is rewarded for correctly handling multi-turn customer conversations to successful resolution, while the CA is rewarded for producing realistic, intent-concealing utterances that cause misroutes, creating asymmetric adversarial pressure through opposing but independently structured rewards. An isolation gym iteratively retrains the weaker agent on prior-failure transcripts, requiring no human annotation at any stage. In a banking customer support proof of concept, tool-routing errors are eliminated and the strict end-to-end PASS rate doubles over five co-evolutionary cycles, driven solely by automated arena reward with no labelled data. We additionally observe the emergence of \textbf{Contextual Camouflage}, an adversarial strategy in which the CA learns to embed intent within dense realistic customer detail purely from reward pressure, with direct implications for enterprise red-teaming and robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [117] [Listen to the Latents: Self-Correcting Speech Recognition in Large Audio Language Models Through Hidden-State Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02940)
*Chan-Jan Hsu,Jaeyeon Kim,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shinji Watanabe,Hung-yi Lee,Carlos Busso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid Search策略，通过利用预适应基础LLM的交互特征，选择性细化在语义上高度依赖的token，从而进一步提升基于warm-initialization的LLM-ASR模型在推理阶段的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统日益将LLM集成进来，通常通过logit融合（外部）或warm初始化（内部）两种方式。然而，如何有效结合这两种策略仍未被深入探索，特别是warm-initialized LLM-ASR模型在推理阶段如何进一步利用其基础LLM来提升性能的问题。

Method: 提出Hybrid Search策略，具体为：1) 利用LLM-based ASR隐藏状态与base-LLM隐藏状态之间的交互特征，作为token语义依赖程度的信号；2) 仅对语义依赖性高的目标token进行选择性细化修正（采用LoRA适配设置以保留基础LLM），而非对所有token进行全局LLM修正。

Result: 实验表明，Hybrid Search在ASR性能上远超包括rescoring和late fusion在内的朴素全局LLM修正方法。此外，分析发现即使在通过warm初始化完成语义知识迁移后，LLM-based ASR模型仍可利用其基础LLM在推理阶段进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 选择性细化策略比全局LLM修正更有效，且语义知识转移后基础LLM仍可在推理时作为补充资源被利用，揭示了warm-initialized LLM-ASR模型与基础LLM之间的互补关系。

Abstract: Recent automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems increasingly integrate large language models (LLMs) to leverage their semantic knowledge, either externally through logit fusion or internally through warm initialization. However, how to effectively combine these two strategies remains underexplored. In this work, we refine warm-initialized LLM-based ASR models by leveraging their own pre-adaptation base LLMs, focusing on LoRA-adapted settings where the base LLM is preserved. To achieve this, we propose Hybrid Search, a targeted correction strategy motivated by two observations. First, interaction features that characterize the relationship between LLM-based ASR hidden states and base-LLM hidden states provide informative signals about a token's degree of semantic dependence. Second, selectively refining targeted tokens with high semantic dependence improves ASR performance far beyond naive global LLM-correction methods including rescoring and late fusion. Our analysis suggests that, even after semantic knowledge transfer through warm initialization, LLM-based ASR models can still leverage their base LLM to further improve inference-time performance.

</details>


### [118] [Judging LLM-as-a-Judge: Concerning Rubric Artifacts in LLM-based Automated Text Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02942)
*Anshul Bagaria,Sowmya S Sundaram,Gokul S Krishnan,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑LLM-as-a-Judge评估管线的核心假设：评判应基于对候选回复与评分标准的推理。研究发现仅用评论文本训练的分类器（不接触被评回复）即可获得非平凡预测性能；反向扰动实验表明评判者面对反转候选回复或评分标准时未能可靠更新决策，对基于评分的LLM自动评估可靠性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge评估管线被广泛用于评估AI生成文本，其前提假设是评判结果源于对候选回复相对于评分标准的推理。作者认为这一假设需要进一步审视，遂设计实验检验其有效性。

Method: 三个关键实验：(1)仅用评论文本、不访问任何被评回复训练分类器，检验其能否预测评判输出；(2)对候选回复进行反向扰动，观察评判者决策是否更新；(3)对评分标准/基准条目进行反向扰动，检验评判决策的稳定性。

Result: 仅基于评论文本的分类器即可实现非平凡的预测性能，说明评分标准本身编码了可恢复的评估信号，评分可部分预判而无需依赖模型输出。同时，无论反转候选回复还是评分标准，评判者在多数情况下无法可靠地更新决策。

Conclusion: 研究发现削弱了基于评分标准的LLM评估的可靠性假设，进一步说明自动评估中的方法学问题亟待深入研究，包括评估信号的来源偏差与评判对输入变化的敏感性缺失。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge pipelines are increasingly used to evaluate AI-generated text, based on the assumption that judgments arise from reasoning over candidate responses with respect to a rubric. We show that this assumption warrants further scrutiny. Classifiers trained only on rubric text, without access to any evaluated response, achieve nontrivial predictive performance on judge outputs. This suggests that rubric formulations encode recoverable evaluative signals, allowing scores to be partially anticipated independently of model outputs. Finally, counterfactual perturbations reveal that judges often fail to reliably update their decisions when either the candidate response or the rubric criterion is reversed. Our findings raise concerns about the reliability of rubric-based LLM evaluation and highlight the need for further methodological study of automated evaluation via LLMs.

</details>


### [119] [LexIssue: Benchmarking Legal Issue Identification in Chinese Civil Litigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02954)
*Huiyuan Xie,Yuqin Huang,Zhicheng Hao,Yida Cai,Shaochun Wang,Zhenghao Liu,Yuxiao Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究诉讼中法律争议点识别的计算建模问题，提出了一个法律依据的分层模式（自由文本描述+结构化法律类别），将法律争议点识别形式化为生成与分类两个互补任务，构建了包含430个真实中国民事案件和1303个专家标注争议点的LexIssue基准，并开发了覆盖27个案由和441个候选争议点的争议点中心法律知识库以支持检索增强推理。实验表明检索增强生成方式在识别争议点及其法律属性上均持续优于多种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别诉讼双方争议的法律问题是真实诉讼中的关键环节，但法律争议点在法律AI研究中相对未充分探索，缺乏系统的计算建模和基准数据集。

Method: 提出一个法律依据支持的分层模式（同时包含自由表述的争议点描述和结构化的法律类别）；将法律争议点识别表述为两个互补任务——法律争议点生成与法律争议点分类；构建LexIssue基准（430个真实中国民事案件，1303个专家标注争议点）；开发争议点中心法律知识库（27个案由，441个候选法律争议条目）用于检索增强推理。

Result: 跨多种模型的实验表明，使用构建的法律争议点知识库进行检索增强生成，在识别争议法律点和相应法律属性方面持续提升了性能。

Conclusion: 基于争议点中心法律知识库的检索增强生成方法能有效提升法律争议点识别及其法律属性判断的准确性，为法律AI领域提供了新的基准和方法。

Abstract: Identifying the issues disputed between litigating parties is a crucial component of real-world litigation. However, legal issues remain comparatively underexplored in legal AI research. In this work, we study the computational modelling of legal issue identification in litigation. We introduce a legally grounded hierarchical schema that represents legal issues through both free-form issue descriptions and structured legal categories, and formulate legal issue identification as two complementary tasks: legal issue generation and legal issue classification. Based on this formulation, we construct LexIssue, a benchmark containing 430 real-world Chinese civil litigation cases and 1,303 expert-annotated disputed legal issues. We further develop an issue-centric legal knowledge base spanning 27 causes of action and 441 candidate legal issue entries to support retrieval-augmented reasoning. Experimental results across a diverse set of models show that retrieval-augmented generation using the constructed legal issue knowledge base consistently improves performance in identifying disputed legal issues and their corresponding legal attributes.

</details>


### [120] [Unifying Conformal Language Tasks with In-Context Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03005)
*Xiao Shi Huang,Chen-Yuan Lin,Bruce Kuwahara,Kin Kwan Leung,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Relevance框架，利用上下文学习示例筛选和集成方法自动构建评分函数，在保证覆盖度（coverage）的同时提升简洁性（conciseness），减少人工提示工程成本，并在七个NLP任务上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多NLP任务（如摘要、抽取式问答）需要在覆盖度和简洁性两个约束下从文档中检索相关内容。现有最优评分函数依赖手工设计的LLM提示来评估内容重要性，但人工提示工程耗时且任务特定，缺乏通用性和自动化。
