Method: 提出Conformal Relevance框架：(1) 使用上下文学习（in-context learning）示例筛选来构造评分函数，替代手工提示工程；(2) 通过集成（ensembling）多个评分函数提升表现；(3) 从理论上研究了集成对共形评分的多样性影响，提出互补性条件（complementarity condition）刻画集成何时改善最坏情况下的句子分数，以及集成改进的饱和上界（saturation bound）。

Result: 在七个NLP任务上验证了该框架的有效性，在保持共形预测覆盖度保证的同时提升了检索内容的简洁性，并最小化了人工输入。理论分析给出了集成多样性与评分改善之间的量化关系。

Conclusion: Conformal Relevance框架通过自动化评分函数构建，在覆盖度与简洁性之间取得更优平衡，减少了人工提示调优的负担，并提供了集成立论的数学基础，为共形检索提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks, such as summarization and extractive question answering, reduce to retrieving relevant content from documents under two constraints: coverage, retaining enough pertinent information to achieve some goal, and conciseness, removing as much irrelevant information as possible. Conformal prediction methods have been used to guarantee coverage, and must be optimized for conciseness through design of a score function. State-of-the-art scoring functions use hand-engineered LLM prompts asking the model to rate the importance of content, but manual prompt engineering is labor-intensive and task-specific. We introduce the Conformal Relevance framework which uses in-context learning example curation and ensembling to create a score function which maintains coverage while improving conciseness with minimal manual input. We demonstrate this framework's application on seven NLP tasks, and also theoretically study the impact of diversity for ensembled conformal scores, giving a complementarity condition that characterizes when ensembling improves worst-case sentence scores, and a saturation bound on ensemble improvement.

</details>


### [121] [SHELF: A Synthetic Harness for Multi-Task Bibliographic Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03047)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍SHELF（Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness），一个用于评估图书馆与档案馆文献编目工作中大语言模型性能的Python基准测试系统。系统将带标签的分类学、写作规范与生成预算转化为受控基准数据和评估任务，首个版本包含62,899篇基于美国国会图书馆词汇生成的文档及六类任务。实验发现主题分类F1达0.8887，而体裁形式分类仅0.2605，稀疏方法在分类上仍具竞争力，TF-IDF速度最快。代码与数据以宽松许可开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆和档案馆在有限的人力和算力预算下管理大量馆藏，但现有通用基准并未系统性测试它们实际的文献编目工作任务。领域从业者需要知道哪些方法适用于其具体任务，以及这些方法的运行资源需求，然而缺乏针对性的、受控的、可复现的评测工具。

Method: SHELF作为一个Python系统，将带标签的分类学、写作规格说明和生成预算转化为受控基准数据与评估任务。首个发布版本基于美国国会图书馆词汇生成62,899篇文档，包含分类、聚类、检索、配对分类和指令检索等任务，并独立变化文献学要素（bibliographic facets），可生成模型训练截止后确实不可见的新文档。

Result: 对比了TF、TF-IDF、BM25、主流编码器以及零样本解码器（仅用于主题分类），每种方法仅在支持它的任务上评测。主题分类达到0.8887，而体裁形式分类仅0.2605，若干配对与聚类任务接近随机水平。稀疏方法在分类任务上仍有竞争力，TF-IDF是主题分类时间实验中最快的方案。与LCSHBench和Project Gutenberg的比较表明，模型排名比绝对分数更具迁移性，但SHELF分数不能估计生产编目数据的准确率。

Conclusion: SHELF填补了图书馆文献工作中系统化评估LLM的空白，提供了受控、可扩展且可复现的基准。其结论提示：不同编目子任务的难度差异显著（体裁形式分类远难于主题分类），稀疏传统方法在许多任务上仍可与现代密集方法竞争且资源要求更低。所有源码与数据以宽松许可发布在GitHub和Hugging Face上。

Abstract: Libraries and archives manage large collections with limited staff and computing budgets, yet common benchmarks do not systematically test their bibliographic work. They need to know which methods work for their tasks and what those methods require to run. SHELF, the Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness, addresses this gap. It is a Python system that turns labelled taxonomies, writing specifications, and a generation budget into controlled benchmark data and evaluation tasks. This first release contains 62,899 model-written documents based on Library of Congress vocabularies, with tasks for classification, clustering, retrieval, pair classification, and instruction retrieval. We compare TF, TF-IDF, BM25, popular encoders, and, on subject classification only, zero-shot decoders; each method appears only on tasks that support it. Subject classification reaches 0.8887, while genre-form classification reaches only 0.2605, and several pair and clustering tasks remain near chance. Sparse methods remain competitive on classification, while TF-IDF is the fastest measured arm in the subject timing experiment. SHELF also varies bibliographic facets independently and can generate new, verifiably unseen documents after a model's training cutoff. Comparisons with LCSHBench and Project Gutenberg show that model rankings transfer more reliably than absolute scores, but SHELF scores do not estimate accuracy on production catalogue data. We release all source code and data under permissive licenses on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [122] [Large Language Models in Resolving Contextual Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03148)
*Xinye Yang,Zhenyang Liu,Ruisi Li,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM处理上下文内部知识冲突（而非参数知识与上下文冲突）的问题，提出六类上下文冲突分类体系，构建了包含5,781个样本的ContextConflict数据集，覆盖推理与摘要任务。实验显示九种LLM在解决上下文冲突上表现不足，并通过机制可解释性分析揭示模型对冲突的潜在感知及偏向早期证据的位置偏差。作者进而提出一种无需训练和标签的激活引导方法，可一致提升推理准确率和摘要质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究聚焦于LLM参数化知识与外部提供的上下文之间的冲突，但忽略了上下文本体内部所存在的知识冲突。作者希望系统性地研究LLM如何处理这种上下文内部冲突，填补该研究空白。

Method: 提出六类上下文冲突分类体系（事实性、推理性、时间性、粒度性、视角性、歧义性）；构建ContextConflict数据集（5,781样本，覆盖推理与摘要任务，含显式矛盾和需多步推理的隐式冲突）；在九种LLM上进行实验；进行机制可解释性分析揭示模型对冲突的潜在感知与表示几何特性；提出一种训练无关、标签无关的激活引导（steering）方法，促进更全面地整合证据以改善冲突解决。

Result: 当前LLM在解决上下文知识冲突上仍表现不足；机制分析揭示模型存在对冲突的潜在感知能力，但表现出对早期证据的一致偏向，该位置偏好是关键障碍；所提出的激活引导方法在推理任务上一致提升准确率，在摘要任务上生成更高质量、更均衡的摘要。

Conclusion: 上下文内部冲突是LLM面临的重要挑战，模型普遍存在早期证据偏向问题；通过激活引导可在无需训练和标签的前提下有效缓解该问题，提升冲突解决的性能。

Abstract: Most prior works focused on conflicts between an LLM's internal parametric knowledge and externally provided context. In contrast, we investigate how LLMs handle conflicts that arise within contextual knowledge itself. We introduce a taxonomy of six types of contextual conflicts (factual, inferential, temporal, granularity, perspective, and ambiguity) and contribute a comprehensive dataset ContextConflict for this setting. The dataset contains 5,781 samples, covers both reasoning and summarization tasks, and includes both explicit contradictions and implicit conflicts that require multi-step reasoning. Experiments on nine LLMs show that current models still fall short in resolving contextual knowledge conflicts. We further provide mechanistic interpretability insights into how LLMs process such conflicts, revealing their latent awareness of conflicts and the representational geometry underlying conflict processing. In addition, our analysis uncovers a consistent model bias towards earlier evidence, and this positional preference serves as a key obstacle to effective conflict resolution. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a simple training-free, label-free steering method that steers activations to encourage a more comprehensive incorporation of evidences for better conflict resolution. On our dataset, the method consistently improves accuracy on reasoning tasks and generates higher-quality, more balanced summaries for summarization tasks.

</details>


### [123] [No country for old linguists: LLM-brain alignment underdetermines neural computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03160)
*Elliot Murphy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Nastase等人（2026）提出的"LLM可作为语言处理机制模型"的论点提出批判性审视，指出表征对齐本身并不能推导出共享的机制或计算原理，存在逻辑、因果与计算层面的"欠定性"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认可LLM与大脑共享分布式、上下文敏感表征且均由统计学习塑造的观点，也肯定LLM-大脑对齐研究的价值，但核心质疑是：这种对齐究竟能支撑何种科学推断？作者担心Nastase等人从"表征对齐"不当跳转到"共享计算原理"乃至"LLM作为语言的完全机制模型"这一更强结论。

Method: 采用概念分析与逻辑论证的方法，考察Nastase等人论证中"表征对齐"与"机制建模"之间的推论链条，并指出其中存在的逻辑、因果与计算三方面的欠确定性问题，尤其指出其方法论上的谨慎声明（对齐不建立共享架构或算法）与其强结论（LLM可作完全机制模型）自相矛盾。

Result: 作者得出狭义结论：表征对齐原则上可以约束机制假说，但其本身不足以识别出一种机制。Nastase等人承认编码模型无需共享架构或算法即可捕获神经表征特征，却在推导过程中过度从对齐滑向"共享计算原理"与"完全机制模型"的表述，构成了主张强度与证据支撑之间的落差。

Conclusion: 对齐研究是有价值的约束性工具，但不应被误解为能直接揭示或确证语言处理的底层机制。LLM-大脑表征对齐能筛除部分假说，却无法唯一锁定受支持的计算机制，研究者需警惕从相关性推论到机制身份的跳跃。

Abstract: Nastase et al. (2026) argue that large language models (LLMs) may illuminate language processing because both rely on distributed, context-sensitive representations shaped by statistical learning. Their rejection of simple cortical "boxology" is persuasive, and they articulate a strong case for the value of LLM-brain alignment research. The key question is what kind of inference LLM-brain alignment licenses. My claim here will be narrow: representational alignment can in principle constrain mechanistic hypotheses, but it does not by itself identify a mechanism. Nastase et al. acknowledge that an encoding model can capture features represented in neural activity without establishing a shared architecture or algorithm. Yet the authors sometime move from alignment to "shared computational principles" and ultimately to LLMs as mechanistic models of natural language. Indeed, their methodological caveat that alignment does not establish a shared architecture or algorithm sits uneasily with their conclusion that LLMs might instantiate the same computational principles as biological brains and provide a "fully mechanistic model" of language. I discuss what I consider to be problems of logical, causal, and computational underdetermination in Nastase et al.'s (2026) proposal.

</details>


### [124] [Jina-OCR-v1: Efficient Document Parsing with Speculative Decoding and Dense Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03181)
*Alejandro Barón García,Feng Wang,Emilia Garcia Casademont,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Jina-OCR-v1，一个端到端文档解析模型，专为低预算 GPU 设计。它采用 DeepSeek-OCR 的压缩视觉编码器与 3B 混合专家解码器（每 token 激活约 5.7 亿参数），并结合 FastMTP 投机解码头部，通过跨 K=3 预测步骤递归共享单一草稿块实现无损加速。后训练结合指令对齐、困难文档鲁棒性微调及 GRPO 密集可验证奖励。在 OmniDocBench v1.6 得 91.14，olmOCR-Bench 得 83.4，页面吞吐量达 2.57 页/秒；在 NVIDIA L4 上 FastMTP 使解码速度较贪心自回归解码翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高级文档解析模型性能虽强但计算开销大，难以在低预算 GPU 上高效运行。作者希望构建一个在不牺牲准确率的前提下，能在低端显卡上实现高吞吐解码的端到端文档解析模型，并进一步加速投机解码以提升推理效率。

Method: 将 DeepSeek-OCR 的压缩视觉编码器和 3B MoE 解码器（每 token 约激活 570M 参数）结合 FastMTP 投机解码头，该头跨 K=3 个预测步骤递归共享单一草稿块，通过贪婪验证实现无损解码。后训练包含三阶段：指令对齐、针对困难文档的鲁棒性微调、以及基于确定性公式/表格/结构检查（给予部分奖励）的 GRPO 强化学习。训练数据为清洗后的公开语料与定向合成页面的混合。

Result: 默认动态分辨率设定下，Jina-OCR-v1 在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上取得 91.14 分，在 olmOCR-Bench 上取得 83.4 分，并在对比中达到最高页面吞吐量 2.57 页/秒。在 NVIDIA L4 低预算 GPU 上，FastMTP 相比贪心自回归解码将解码速度提升一倍。

Conclusion: Jina-OCR-v1 证明通过高效架构组合（MoE+投机解码）与针对性后训练，可以在低预算 GPU 上实现领先的文档解析精度和高吞吐解码，且模型已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: We present Jina-OCR-v1, an end-to-end document parsing model built to serve on low-budget GPUs. It combines the compressed-vision encoder and the 3B mixture-of-experts decoder of DeepSeek-OCR, which activates about 570M parameters per token, with a FastMTP speculative decoding head that shares a single draft block recursively across K=3 prediction steps. Greedy verification makes decoding lossless. Post-training combines instruction alignment, robustness fine-tuning on difficult documents, and GRPO under dense verifiable rewards: deterministic formula, table, and structural checks that award partial credit. The training data mixes cleaned public corpora with targeted synthetic pages. At the default dynamic-resolution setting, Jina-OCR-v1 scores 91.14 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and 83.4 on olmOCR-Bench, and reaches the highest page throughput in our comparison at 2.57 pages per second. On a low-budget GPU such as the NVIDIA L4, FastMTP doubles decoding speed over greedy autoregressive decoding. The model is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/jinaai/jina-ocr-v1.

</details>


### [125] [MemoryLACE: Memory Lifecycle-Aware Consolidation and Evidence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03201)
*Meriem Yacoubi,Pia Schmidt,Nenad Petrovic,Ahmed Frikha,Martin Kirchhoff,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryLACE（MemLACE），一种轻量级记忆框架，通过稀疏的合井、取代和矛盾关系显式建模文本证据的生命周期，同时保留原子自然语言记忆及其来源。通过重构关系感知的证据单元来呈现当前、历史、支持和冲突证据，在BEAM和StructMemEval基准上取得同骨干网络下的最优性能，并将BEAM上端到端运行时间相对Hindsight降低66.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期LLM代理需在交互间保留信息，同时区分重复证据、历史状态、更新和未解决的矛盾。现有文本记忆系统虽能高效检索语义相关记忆但常使关系隐式化，而结构化方法通过全局图、层次抽象或反思建模复杂度高。作者旨在以轻量级方式显式建模证据生命周期关系，避免全局知识图或全局反思带来的复杂度。

Method: MemoryLACE通过稀疏merge、supersession和contradiction三种关系显式建模文本证据的生命周期，保留原子自然语言记忆及其来源（provenance）。与独立检索记忆不同，MemLACE重构关系感知的证据单元，为下游推理暴露当前、历史、支持和冲突证据。

Result: 在BEAM和StructMemEval上，使用开放权重和专有LLM骨干，MemLACE在同骨干对比中取得最高综合性能，且相对最强反思式记忆基线Hindsight将BEAM端到端运行时间降低66.6%。消融研究表明生命周期扩展和时间感知是性能提升的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本证据的局部生命周期足以显著改进长期记忆推理，而无需全面的知识图谱或全局反思，为记忆系统在效果与效率之间提供了新的平衡点。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents must preserve information across interactions while distinguishing repeated evidence, historical states, updates, and unresolved contradictions. Existing textual memory systems retrieve semantically relevant memories efficiently but often leave these relationships implicit, whereas richer structured approaches model them through global graphs, hierarchical abstractions, or reflection at greater complexity. We introduce MemoryLACE (MemLACE), a lightweight memory framework that explicitly models the lifecycle of textual evidence through sparse merge, supersession, and contradiction relations while preserving atomic natural-language memories and their provenance. Rather than retrieving memories independently, MemLACE reconstructs relation-aware evidence units that expose current, historical, supporting, and conflicting evidence for downstream reasoning. Across BEAM and StructMemEval, using open-weight and proprietary LLM backbones, MemLACE achieves the highest overall performance in same-backbone comparisons while reducing end-to-end runtime on BEAM by 66.6% relative to Hindsight, the strongest reported reflective-memory baseline. Ablation studies identify lifecycle expansion and temporal awareness as the principal contributors to these gains. Together, the results demonstrate that explicitly modeling the local lifecycle of textual evidence is sufficient to substantially improve long-term memory reasoning without requiring comprehensive knowledge graphs or global reflection.

</details>


### [126] [LLMs Learn Better In-Context from Rules than from Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03213)
*Xiang Fu,Seungmin Cho,Yukyung Lee,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了大型语言模型中两种上下文学习模式——基于规则描述的学习（指令跟随）与基于输入输出示例的学习（少样本提示）——的学习效率，发现模型通常从规则中学习比从示例中学习更可靠，且指令微调会放大规则学习的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型具备上下文学习能力，可以在不更新权重的情况下从提示上下文中学习新任务。然而，两种主要的上下文学习模式（基于规则的指令跟随 vs. 基于示例的少样本提示）在哪些条件下更有效尚不清楚，对这一问题的理解有助于优化提示设计并深入理解模型的学习机制。

Method: 研究者设计了五个涵盖不同领域（游戏、算术、语言推理）的学习任务，在每个任务中分别用规则描述和输入输出示例两种模式指定相同的底层任务。此外，他们还探索了模型属性（如基础模型vs.指令微调模型）和任务属性（如任务是否需要代数抽象、分布敏感性或参数知识）对两种学习模式效率的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（1）模型普遍从规则中学习比仅从示例中学习更可靠；（2）在规则之外添加更多示例或单纯扩大示例数量并不能带来一致且显著的改进；（3）指令微调放大了基于规则学习的优势，同时保持基于示例学习的能力不变；（4）令人惊讶的是，基础模型中示例学习并无特权效应，规则在代数任务领域仍然有效；（5）当任务需要代数抽象和计算时，规则相对示例的优势更大；当任务需要分布敏感性和/或参数知识时，这种优势更小。

Conclusion: 总体而言，规则在上下文学习中通常优于示例，且这种优势的程度取决于任务所涉及的抽象计算需求程度以及任务对分布敏感性和参数知识的依赖程度。该研究为理解和利用LLM的上下文学习机制提供了重要的实证见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit in-context learning capabilities, where they can learn new tasks from prompt contexts without weight updates. We compare the learning efficacies of two prominent modes of in-context learning: (1) learning from descriptions of rules (instruction following); and (2) learning from examples of input-output demonstrations (few-shot prompting). Through five learning tasks that cover diverse domains (games, arithmetic, linguistic inferences), we compare two modes of learning (rules vs. examples) specifying the same underlying task. We furthermore explore model and task properties that modulate the learning efficacies. We find that models generally learn more reliably from rules than from examples alone, and additional examples on top of rules or simply scaling up the number of examples do not lead to consistent and significant gains. Instruction tuning amplifies the benefit of rule-based learning while keeping example-based learning capacities intact. Surprisingly, we find no privileged effect of example-based learning in base models, and rules still lead to gains in algebraic task domains. Overall, the comparative efficacy of rules over examples is larger when the task recruits algebraic abstractions and computations, and smaller when the task requires distributional sensitivity and/or recruits parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [127] [SWIM: Student Writing Simulation via Proficiency-Conditioned Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03215)
*Heejin Do,Jakub Kontak,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIM任务，探索语言模型能否真实模拟不同熟练度学生的书面写作。实验表明，单纯提示工程对熟练度的控制有限（尤其在词汇、语法和组织层面），监督微调（SFT）显著提升对齐度，而基于熟练度对齐奖励的强化学习（RL）在所有写作特质和提示词上取得进一步增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生写作熟练度体现为内容发展、观点组织、词汇选择和语言运用的多维差异。尽管基于LLM的学生模拟日益受到关注，但LLM能否在扩展写作中复现这种多维变化仍未被充分探索，因此需要系统研究LLM模拟学生写作的能力边界。

Method: 引入SWIM任务，将学生写作模拟形式化为以熟练度为条件的作文生成任务；比较提示工程（含基于评分标准的策略）、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）三种方法；以自动化作文评分作为衡量写作画像对齐度的指标；并提出一种熟练度对齐奖励用于RL训练。

Result: 实验发现：提示工程对熟练度的控制有限，即使强专有LLM配合基于评分标准的策略也仅能调整内容导向的特质，难以复现不同熟练度水平的词汇、语法和组织变异；SFT大幅提升对齐效果；RL配合所提出的熟练度对齐奖励在所有写作特质和作文提示上带来进一步提升。然而，真实的低熟练度写作仍难以复现。

Conclusion: 显式监督（SFT和RL）相比单纯提示工程能够实现显著更强的写作画像对齐，说明结构化的训练信号对于模拟学生写作多维变化至关重要；同时指出低熟练度写作的真实还原仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Writing proficiency manifests in how students develop content, organize ideas, choose words, and use language. Despite growing interest in LLM-based student simulation, whether LLMs can reproduce such multidimensional variation in extended writing remains largely unexplored. In this work, we explore if language models can realistically simulate student writing, and introduce SWIM, a task that formulates Student Writing sIMulation as proficiency-conditioned essay generation. We evaluate prompting, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) methods for writing simulation using automated essay scoring as a measure of profile alignment. Extensive experiments reveal that prompting provides limited proficiency control, even for strong proprietary LLMs with rubric-grounded strategies. In particular, while models can adjust content-oriented traits, they struggle to reproduce the lexical, grammatical, and organizational variation in different proficiency levels. SFT substantially improves alignment, while RL with the proposed proficiency-alignment reward yields further gains across all writing traits and essay prompts. Our findings suggest that explicit supervision enables substantially stronger profile alignment than prompting alone, while authentic low-proficiency writing remains challenging to reproduce.

</details>


### [128] [Counterfactual Fairness Audits of Multi-Step Clinical LLM Agents Require a Measured Per-Action Instability Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03221)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu,Manpreet Singh,Deepak Parashar,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 反事实审计中报告的翻转率本身不可解释，因为即使在完全相同的条件下重复运行，也会产生约6.7%-8.7%的"地板效应"（base flip rate），且该噪声在不同动作间差异巨大（可达8倍）。多数投票聚合可消除39%的噪声但无法完全消除。因此在没有逐动作地板率作为参照的情况下，任何反事实公平性估计都不能被解读为存在差异的证据。论文发布了FairMedAgent评估框架及地板测量协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实审计是检验临床智能体是否对不同人口统计学特征的相同临床病人施加不同处理的标凊工具，其报告的核心指标是翻转率（更改病人描述时动作改变的频率）。作者发现该指标单独呈现时根本无法解释，因为系统本身存在随机性产生的"地板"噪声，使得当前所有基于翻转率的公平性判断都可能失实。

Method: 作者设计了FairMedAgent评估框架，在完全相同的条件下（相同叙述、相同描述字符串、无任何变化）对16个vignette重复运行10次，测出临床智能体的动作翻转率地板。使用两个不同模型交叉验证地板稳定性，用Spearman秩相关检验动作排序一致性，并通过多数投票聚合和零假设模拟（null simulation）分析噪声来源与缓解效果。所有测量基于"范围内反事实翻转率"这一estimation目标，即仅统计在已发布的决策规则和临床医生裁定的动作之间的翻转。

Result: 相同条件重复运行导致8.7%的cell发生动作翻转，且不稳定性在各动作间存在八倍异质性（ICU升级0.022到管制物质警示0.179）。数据中的任何人口统计学对比都无法与这个地板区分开。第二个模型的地板为6.7%，且对六个动作的排序几乎一致（Spearman=0.94, p=0.017），证明地板并非单一系统的伪影。五轮抽样后的多数投票消除了39%的噪声并使曲线趋于平缓，但零假设模拟表明剩余噪声源于各cell的异质翻转率，因此复制只能缓解而无法消除此问题。

Conclusion: 任何反事实公平性估计在没有逐动作地板率并列报告的情况下，不能被解读为存在差异的证据。论文未声称任何差异结果（band裁定仍在进行），但发布了评估框架、地板测量协议和全部分析脚本以供复现。

Abstract: Counterfactual audits are the standard tool for checking whether a clinical agent treats demographically distinct but clinically identical patients differently. They report a flip rate: how often an action changes when only the patient descriptor changes. We show that this quantity is uninterpretable on its own. Re-running an identical condition ten times over sixteen vignettes (same narrative, same descriptor string, nothing varied) moved a clinical agent's action in 8.7% of outcome-vignette cells, and instability was heterogeneous across actions by a factor of eight, from 0.022 for ICU escalation to 0.179 for controlled-substance caution. No demographic contrast in our data was distinguishable from that floor. A second model gives a pooled floor of 6.7% and ranks the six actions almost identically (Spearman 0.94, exact p=0.017), so the floor is not one system's artefact. Majority-vote aggregation over five draws removes 39% of it and then flattens, and a null simulation attributes the residue to heterogeneous per-cell rates, so replication mitigates without eliminating. Any counterfactual fairness estimate reported without a per-action floor beside it therefore cannot be read as evidence of disparity. The measurements were taken with FairMedAgent, an evaluation harness for disparity in the actions of clinical LLM agents whose estimand, the within-range counterfactual flip rate, counts only flips between actions a published decision rule admits and a clinician has adjudicated. That estimand requires band adjudication, which is under way; no disparity result is claimed here. Each synthetic vignette runs a six-stage trajectory (five model-facing decisions around a deterministic environment step) under fixed-form conditions spanning race, sex, age, insurance, English proficiency, and their intersections. The harness, the floor protocol, and every analysis script are released.

</details>


### [129] [SGD-KV: Summarization Guided KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03235)
*Zeyu Liu,Woomin Song,Xuandi Fu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vivek Govindan,Aram Galstyan,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Srikanth Ronanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SGD-KV是一个基于注意力头感知的KV缓存压缩框架，通过新颖的块摘要诊断任务识别和优先分配擅长层级信息聚合的注意力头，在Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3-32B上实现了长达100万token上下文的最先进性能，同时将KV缓存内存占用降低高达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长导致严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩技术通常依赖简单启发式方法，忽视了不同注意力头在模型中承担的不同功能角色，缺乏对注意力头功能差异的细粒度理解。

Method: 提出SGD-KV框架：设计了一种新颖的块摘要（chunk-summarization）诊断任务，用于系统性地识别并优先分配擅长层级信息聚合（hierarchical information aggregation）的注意力头；基于注意力头的摘要得分分布来策略性地分配KV缓存预算，实现头感知的缓存压缩。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3-32B上、跨多个长上下文基准测试中达到最先进性能；支持最长100万token的上下文；KV缓存内存占用最多降低75%；证明了基于注意力头摘要得分分布进行缓存预算分配能获得更优的效率-准确率权衡。

Conclusion: 基于注意力头功能角色的差异化分析来分配KV缓存预算，相比简单启发式压缩方法可以取得更好的效率与准确性平衡，为长上下文推理中的KV缓存管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face severe memory bottlenecks in long-context inference due to the linearly growing size of key-value (KV) caches. Existing KV cache compression techniques typically rely on simple heuristics, overlooking the distinct functional roles of different attention heads. We present SGD-KV (Summarization-Guided KV Cache Compression), a head-aware framework that leverages a novel chunk-summarization diagnostic task to systematically identify and prioritize attention heads specialized in hierarchical information aggregation. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3-32B across diverse long-context benchmarks demonstrate that SGD-KV achieves state-of-the-art performance with contexts up to 1M tokens, while reducing KV cache memory usage by up to 75%. Our findings show that strategically allocating the KV cache budget based on the summarization score distribution of attention heads yields a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off for long-context inference.

</details>


### [130] [What Else Needs Fixing? Exploring Cost-Effective Test-Time Compute for Revision Propagation in Artifacts Generated Through Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03254)
*Daisuke Kikuta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在对话式生成工件场景中传播局部修订到依赖组件的能力，提出了新基准并评估了九种修订方法（覆盖多个开源LLM），发现基于三个并行样本选择（LLM评分或medoid选择）是最具性价比的方法，可将准确率提升2.2-9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户通过多轮对话迭代生成工件时经常只指定局部修改，但LLM需要识别并传播修改到所有受影响的依赖部分。现有研究缺乏对这一能力在对话式生成（工件上下文及其依赖可能嵌入在对话历史中）场景下的系统评估，同时需要探索成本效益高的测试时计算方法以支持实际应用。

Method: 引入一个新的基准测试用于评估对话式工件中的修订传播；在该基准上评估九种修订方法，包括顺序反思和并行采样变体；使用多个LLM（gpt-oss-20b/120b、gpt-5.4-mini、qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b）进行评测；比较基于LLM的样本选择与medoid选择等成本效益策略。

Result: 基线方法在基准上的准确率为68.3%-93%；最具成本效益的方法是从三个并行样本中通过LLM评分或medoid选择选出最优结果，能够将准确率提升2.2-9.7%。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: LLM在对话式生成中的修订传播是一个具有挑战性的问题，但通过简单的并行采样加自动选择策略（LLM评分或medoid），可以在不显著增加成本的情况下有效提升修订传播的准确率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often help users generate artifacts through iterative cycles of generation and revision in conversation. A challenge here is that, when users specify only a local change during revision, LLMs must instead identify the relevant dependencies and propagate the revision to all affected parts of the artifact. This paper studies this ability of LLMs on conversationally generated artifacts, where the artifact context and its dependencies may be embedded in the conversation history. Toward practical use, we also explore cost-effective test-time compute for this new setting. Specifically, we introduce a new benchmark for this setting, and evaluate nine revision methods, including sequential reflection and parallel sampling variants, using gpt-oss-20b/120b, gpt-5.4-mini, and qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b on the benchmark. The results show that baselines achieve accuracies of 68.3--93%, and the most cost-effective method is selecting from three parallel samples using either LLM-based or medoid selection, which improves accuracy by 2.2--9.7%. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ntt-dkiku/llm-revision-propagation.

</details>


### [131] [Contextual Tamil Spelling and Grammar Correction Using Progressively Fine-Tuned Sequence-to-Sequence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03273)
*Karthikeyan A,Jaya Nirmala S,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Indhu R,Pranav Kumar,Bharat Jude Johnson,Vishnu Ram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出端到端序列到序列模型用于泰米尔语拼写与语法纠错，通过微调mT5-small和mBART-50，并设计四阶段渐进式训练计划（表面噪声→上下文语法→单词边界sandhi→跨词sandhi），在含657,720对合成噪声-干净句对的语料上训练。最佳模型mBART-50 v5在1,000句平衡诊断集上达到69.3%的top-1精确匹配准确率，其中sandhi 87.5%、主谓一致43.5%。研究还首次量化了sandhi召回与身份准确率之间的单调取舍关系，并发现泰米尔适应的指令模型（Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct）缺乏任务特定监督时无法迁移到句级纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰米尔语是一种黏着型低资源语言，具有丰富的动词形态、复杂的词边界sandhi（音变）规则和247个字母的文字系统。现有方法（基于规则、统计n-gram、最小编辑距离、或带transformer重排序器的混合流水线）只能处理词级表面错误，无法可靠处理需要句级理解的上下文错误（如主谓一致、时态一致性、跨词sandhi）。作者旨在提出一种能够端到端处理这些挑战性错误的方法。

Method: 采用端到端序列到序列（seq2seq）建模，微调mT5-small和mBART-50两个骨干模型。在多达657,720对覆盖十类错误的合成泰米尔语噪声-干净句对语料上训练。两种骨干均遵循相同的四阶段渐进式训练计划：v2针对表面噪声、v3针对上下文语法、v4针对单词边界sandhi、v5针对多位置跨词sandhi，每阶段针对一个弱点逐步增强。

Result: 在1,000句、与训练数据完全不相交的平衡诊断集上，最佳模型mBART-50 v5达到69.3%的top-1精确匹配准确率，其中sandhi类别87.5%、主谓一致类别43.5%。渐进式训练计划贡献显著：引入上下文对后主谓一致准确率从1.0%升至52.5%，引入多位置sandhi对后sandhi准确率从0%升至87.5%。此外，首次量化了文献中未报告的精度-召回权衡：sandhi召回以身份（identity）准确率的单调下降为代价。对照实验中，Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct在零样本下仅达19.0%，三次示范（few-shot）下达24.7%，均低于20.0%的复制基线，显示泰米尔适应的指令模型在缺乏任务特定监督时无法迁移到句级纠错任务。

Conclusion: 四阶段渐进式课程训练与端到端seq2seq框架相结合，能有效提升泰米尔语句级拼写与语法纠错性能，特别是在上下文语法和跨词sandhi这类此前难以处理的问题上。然而，sandhi纠错的提升以身份保持准确率下降为代价，存在固有的精度-召回折衷。同时，通用泰米尔指令调整大模型无法零样本或少量示例地胜任专业句级纠错任务，凸显了任务特定监督的必要性。

Abstract: Tamil spell and grammar correction is challenging because Tamil is an agglutinative low-resource language with rich verbal morphology, complex sandhi (phonetic transformation) rules at word boundaries, and a script of 247 distinct letters. Prior work targets word-level surface errors with rule-based methods, statistical n-gram models, Minimum Edit Distance, or hybrid pipelines with a transformer re-ranker; such methods cannot reliably handle contextual errors - subject-verb agreement, tense consistency, or cross-word sandhi - which require sentence-level understanding. We propose an end-to-end sequence-to-sequence formulation and fine-tune mT5-small and mBART-50 on a synthetic corpus of up to 657,720 noisy-clean Tamil sentence pairs spanning ten error categories. Both backbones follow the same four-stage progressive schedule, each stage targeting one weakness: surface noise (v2), contextual grammar (v3), single-site sandhi (v4), and multi-site cross-word sandhi (v5). On a 1,000-sentence balanced diagnostic set verified disjoint from all training data, our best model, mBART-50 v5, reaches 69.3% top-1 exact-match accuracy, with 87.5% on sandhi and 43.5% on subject-verb agreement. The schedule is what produces these gains: subject-verb accuracy rises from 1.0% to 52.5% once contextual pairs are introduced, and sandhi from 0% to 87.5% once multi-site sandhi pairs are. We additionally quantify a precision-recall trade-off this literature has not reported: sandhi recall is paid for monotonically in identity accuracy. Finally, Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct reaches 19.0% zero-shot and 24.7% with three demonstrations against a 20.0% copy baseline, showing that a Tamil-adapted instruction model does not transfer to specialised sentence-level correction without task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [132] [PACE: Towards Surfacing Hidden Conflicts in User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03293)
*Yoojin Kim,Jihyoung Jang,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE数据集和PaceMaker多智能体框架，解决个性化助手在隐含知识约束下识别用户请求是否合适（冲突/拒绝）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化助手不仅要执行请求，还需判断请求在当前用户环境下是否恰当。然而现有工作主要关注准确执行请求，忽视基于上下文的冲突拒绝能力；且已有冲突/安全检测依赖显式提供的因素，现实场景中往往是需要从知识库检索的隐含因素。

Method: 1) 提出PACE数据集：将基于明确定义人设的用户请求与自我中心知识库事实配对，要求模型整合上下文证据判断请求是否冲突。2) 提出PaceMaker多智能体框架：由专门智能体协调查询改写、多跳图遍历和冲突感知过滤，检索上下文决定性证据。

Result: 在PACE数据集上实验评估证据检索质量和冲突决策准确率，PaceMaker持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PACE为隐含约束下的个性化助手冲突评估提供了基准，PaceMaker证明了多智能体检索框架在此类隐含上下文任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Personalized assistants should not only comply with user requests but also assess whether those requests are appropriate given the user's current circumstances. However, prior work has primarily focused on accurately executing requests, overlooking the need for assistants to account for context and engage in conflict-based refusal. Furthermore, while existing work on conflict or safety detection relies on explicitly provided factors, real-world scenarios often involve implicit factors that must be retrieved from a knowledge base (KB). To this end, we introduce Personalized Assistants for Conflict Evaluation (PACE), a dataset for evaluating whether models can identify latent constraints, expressed as egocentric knowledge or events, that render seemingly reasonable user requests inappropriate. PACE pairs user requests grounded in well-defined personas with egocentric KB facts, requiring models to integrate contextual evidence to determine whether a request is conflicting. This implicit retrieval setting hinders the direct association between user requests and conflict-inducing knowledge, making it difficult for existing models to identify relevant user-specific facts. To address this challenge, we further propose PaceMaker, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents coordinate across query reformulation, multi-hop graph traversal, and conflict-aware filtering to retrieve contextually decisive evidence. Experiments on PACE evaluate both evidence retrieval quality and conflict decision accuracy, showing that PaceMaker consistently outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [133] [Decoupling Turn-Taking from Semantics: A Decoupled Data Approach for Finite-State-Machine-Based Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03321)
*Yihang Li,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦数据方法(DEF-FSM)来提升神经有限状态机(NFSM)在全双工对话中的轮转自然度：从真实人类对话学习轮转控制，用可配置的人机文本对话塑造语义行为，并引入规则驱动的事件引导数据转换方法和源感知校准(SAC)损失函数，实验证明该方法显著提升轮转能力同时恢复基础LLM的语义能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NFSM框架依赖合成文本数据构建对话磁带，而LLM无法忠实地模拟真实人类对话中细粒度的声学时间动态，导致轮转(turn-taking)自然度存在根本性局限。为了克服这一瓶颈，需要一种能从真实人-人(HH)口语对话中学习轮转能力的数据与方法。

Method: (1) 提出解耦数据方法：从真实HH口语对话学习轮转特征，通过可配置的人机(HA)文本对话塑造语义行为；(2) 提出规则驱动的事件引导数据变换方法：通过分类轮转事件并应用确定性映射规则，将HH口语对话序列化为FSM磁带，无需LLM生成的注释即可实现可扩展监督；(3) 提出源感知校准(SAC)损失：联合校准状态转移Token的长尾分布，并将每个数据源引导到其最擅长的能力上。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的方法显著提升了全双工对话系统的轮转熟练度，同时恢复并保持了基础LLM的语义能力，验证了解耦数据策略与SAC损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 用真实口语对话数据替代合成数据来训练NFSM的轮转控制是可行且有效的。解耦数据来源、规则化的数据变换以及校准损失的设计为全双工对话系统在保持语义能力的同时提升轮转自然度提供了务实路径，相关代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: The Neural Finite State Machine (NFSM) framework offers a pragmatic path to full-duplex dialogue by serializing turn-taking control and response generation onto a single causal tape under the standard next-token prediction objective, thereby preserving semantic prowess at a low fine-tuning cost. However, its reliance on synthetic text data fundamentally limits turn-taking naturalness, as Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot faithfully simulate the fine-grained acoustic temporal dynamics of real human dialogues. In this work, we propose a decoupled data approach that learns turn-taking from real Human-Human (HH) spoken dialogues while shaping semantic behavior through configurable Human-Agent (HA) text dialogues. To operationalize this approach, we introduce a rule-based event-guided data transformation method that serializes HH spoken dialogues into FSM tapes by classifying turn-taking events and applying deterministic mapping rules, enabling scalable supervision without LLM-generated annotations. We further propose a Source-Aware Calibrated (SAC) Loss that jointly calibrates the long-tailed distribution of state transition tokens and channels each data source toward the capability it best supervises. Experiments show that our approach substantially improves turn-taking proficiency while recovering the foundation LLM's semantic capability. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/Liyht/def-fsm.

</details>


### [134] [How Perturbations Propagate: A Multi-Level Analysis of Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03322)
*Dun Li Chan,Emily Liu,Niyathi Allu,Christian Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了六种自然与合成输入扰动如何在三层面上（输出行为、隐状态几何、注意力头功能）影响仅解码器语言模型，发现扰动类型产生可区分的度量轮廓，单一指标评估具误导性，主张多层面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型经常遇到拼写错误、文本损坏、词替换和词序打乱等扰动，但现有鲁棒性评估通常只关注输出行为，忽略了模型内部计算过程如何受到扰动影响的深层分析。

Method: 在四个GPT-2和两个Qwen2.5检查点上，从三个层面研究六种自然及合成输入扰动的影响：1）输出行为层面；2）通过中心核对齐（CKA）和内在维度分析逐层几何结构；3）GPT-2中的注意力头功能分析。同时使用梯度引导的HotFlip扰动与速率匹配的随机词替换进行比较，并利用激活修补测量复制分数。

Result: 扰动类型产生可由度量区分的特征轮廓，这些特征无法被输出指标完全反映，且在不同检查点间仅部分一致。复制分数与词替换和词序打乱下的激活修补恢复尤其相关。HotFlip扰动比随机词替换在GPT-2中引起更强的行为和表征破坏，其行为效应在全部六个检查点中一致。

Conclusion: 基于单一行为或表征指标的鲁棒性声明可能具有误导性，需要从多层面评估扰动如何改变语言模型的计算过程，以获得更全面的鲁棒性理解。

Abstract: Language models encounter typos, corrupted text, altered words, and disrupted token order, yet robustness is usually evaluated only through output behavior. We study how six naturalistic and synthetic input perturbations propagate through decoder-only language models at three levels: output behavior, hidden-state geometry, and attention-head function. We evaluate behavioral effects across four GPT-2 and two Qwen2.5 checkpoints by analyzing layerwise geometry using centered kernel alignment and intrinsic dimension, and examine attention-head responses in GPT-2. Perturbation types produce distinguishable metric profiles that are not fully captured by output measures and are only partly consistent across the tested checkpoints. Copying scores are especially associated with activation-patching recovery under token substitution and shuffling. Gradient-guided HotFlip perturbations also cause stronger behavioral and representational disruption than rate-matched random token substitutions in GPT-2; their behavioral effects are consistent across all six tested checkpoints. Our results show that robustness claims based on a single behavioral or representational metric can be misleading, and motivate multi-level evaluation of how perturbations alter language-model computation.

</details>


### [135] [Less Is Moral: A CHARMing Framework for Moral Foundations Detection in Endorsement Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03330)
*Huixiang Fu,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARM框架，一种基于轻量级微调大语言模型的道德基础检测系统，通过MAC交叉注意力、理性对齐和仇恨语音调制三个组件分别操作化不同的心理学构念，在域内AUC提升最高15.3%，在所有域外数据集上超越监督基线，并提供低成本、可扩展的替代提示式LLM检测器的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德基础检测系统存在三个主要问题：跨域泛化能力差、缺乏理性（rationale）依据、且过度依赖昂贵的大语言模型提示（prompting）方法。此外，传统词典法、微调法或提示法都将计算与心理学理论分离，缺乏理论驱动的组件设计。

Method: CHARM构建在轻量级微调的大语言模型之上，包含三个核心组件：1）MAC交叉注意力机制，整合互补的道德基础（complementary moral grounding）；2）理性对齐（rationale alignment），利用MFTCXplain中更丰富的监督信号；3）极性感知的仇恨语音调制（polarity-aware hate speech signals）。每个组件都操作化一个明确的心理学构念，而非将计算与理论脱钩。训练数据使用MFTC、MFRC和News数据池的30%子样本结合MFTCXplain的富监督。

Result: 在30%子样本训练条件下，CHARM在域内AUC上最高提升15.3%；在全部域外数据集上，AUC和F1指标均超越监督基线；同时作为提示式LLM检测器的低成本、可扩展替代方案。应用到大规模Twitter COVID-19话语分析中，发现道德价值对齐与在线认可行为强相关。

Conclusion: CHARM通过理论驱动的组件设计实现了更强的跨域泛化能力和稳定性，使道德框架在大规模上可测量，为研究道德化错误信息的传播提供了实用工具，证明道德价值对齐是在线认可行为的重要驱动因素。

Abstract: Moral language plays a central role in shaping online endorsement and the diffusion of information, yet existing moral foundation detection systems often suffer from poor cross-domain generalization, weak rationale grounding, and reliance on costly prompting-based large language models (LLMs). We introduce CHARM, a MA\textbf{C}- and \textbf{H}ate-speech-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}ationale-aligned \textbf{M}oral foundation detection framework built on a lightweight fine-tuned LLM, which integrates complementary moral grounding, rationale alignment, and polarity-aware hate speech signals to support more robust and faithful moral prediction. Unlike prior dictionary-, fine-tune-, or prompt-based detectors, which decouple computation from psychological theory, CHARM is built so that each component -- MAC cross-attention, rationale alignment, and hate-speech modulation -- operationalizes a distinct psychological construct. Using a 30\% subsample of the MFTC, MFRC, and News training pools together with the richer supervision in MFTCXplain, CHARM improves AUC by up to 15.3\% in-domain, surpasses the supervised baselines on every out-of-domain dataset in both AUC and F1, and offers a scalable, low-cost alternative to prompting-based LLM detectors. We further apply CHARM to large-scale COVID-19 discourse on Twitter and show that moral value alignment is strongly associated with online endorsement behavior. By making moral framing measurable at scale, CHARM offers a practical tool for studying the spread of morally charged misinformation.

</details>


### [136] [FPCO-Dialog: A Multi-Turn False-Premise Benchmark for Correction and Cooperation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03331)
*Jiayuan Ma,Yuqi Lu,Weiyang Guo,Chenrui Wang,Junyi Shu,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FPCO-Dialog基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在多轮对话中面对持续错误前提时的纠正与合作行为，包含1,080张图像和10,800个问题回合，并引入CorrTP@K指标衡量纠正率，对20个商业和开源VLM进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的VLM评估很少隔离研究当同一个视觉错误的假设在多轮对话中持续存在时，模型如何响应。作者因此构建了一个在重复错误前提下评估VLM修正与合作行为的基准。

Method: 构建FPCO-Dialog基准：包含1,080张图像和10,800个问题回合，按视觉复杂度、物体类别和错误前提类型进行分层；采用10轮交互协议，先给出正确对话前缀，随后反复使用错误前提的指代表述进行评估；提出模型无关的评估协议和CorrTP@K纠正率指标，由两个独立检测器评分。

Result: 对20个商业和开源VLM的评估显示，各模型在总体纠正倾向、逐轮动态以及不同错误前提类型下均存在显著且持续的差异。

Conclusion: 数据、评估协议、模型输出、检测器标注和代码均已公开，为后续VLM多轮对话中错误前提处理能力的系统研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings where users may describe visual content with incorrect assumptions. Yet existing evaluations rarely isolate how models respond when the same visually grounded false premise persists across dialogue turns. We introduce FPCO-Dialog, a benchmark for evaluating correction and cooperation behavior in VLMs under repeated false premises. FPCO-Dialog contains 1,080 images and 10,800 question turns, stratified by visual complexity, object category, and false-premise class, and uses a 10-turn protocol in which a correct dialogue prefix is followed by repeated false-premise referring expressions. We evaluate 20 commercial and open-source VLMs with a model-agnostic protocol and CorrTP@K, a correction-rate metric over false-premise turns, scored by two independent detectors. FPCO-Dialog reveals substantial and persistent cross-model differences in aggregate correction tendency, model-specific turn-wise dynamics, and systematic variation across false-premise types under the benchmark's substitution distribution. The dataset, evaluation protocol, model outputs, detector labels, and code are available.

</details>


### [137] [Accountable AI with Grounded, Faithful, Consistent, Actionable Rationales: A Case Study in Clinical Trial Matching with VERDICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03366)
*Zikai Zhou,Yufei Jin,Yilin Xu,Yu-Chiang Wang,Chieh-Ju Chao,Monica S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT系统，将决策任务、约束和政策转化为SMT求解问题，通过SMT/MaxSMT求解器获得决策结果，从而在临床试验匹配这一高价值任务中实现可问责AI——即决策可被检查、辩解和质疑。相比LLM-only和神经符号基线，VERDICT在决策准确性、政策一致性、理由可信度及反事实自忠实性上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出流畅但可能缺乏依据、不完整或与决策过程不符，难以实现问责（即决策可检查、可辩解、可质疑）。问责需要验证理由、假设、政策一致性以及关键条件。现有LLM方法在临床试验匹配等高风险任务中虽能较准确匹配，但决策政策应用不一致且理由与自身决策不忠实。

Method: 提出自忠实性（self-faithfulness）作为问责的自动测试标准：改变关键条件应改变决策。引入VERDICT——一个基于LLM的智能体，将决策任务、约束与政策翻译为可满足性模理论（SMT）表达，再通过SMT和MaxSMT求解器推导决策，从而确保政策一致性应用且决策在构造上就是可问责的。

Result: 在SIGIR 2016衍生数据集和TREC 2021上，VERDICT在纯LLM和神经符号基线中实现最强决策准确性，政策应用具有完美一致性，产生临床医生更偏好的、基于显式假设和关键条件的理由，并具有改进的反事实自忠实性。

Conclusion: 将LLM与形式化求解器（SMT/MaxSMT）结合可以克服LLM在问责性上的固有缺陷，在临床试验匹配等高价值场景中实现既准确又具备构造性问责保障的AI决策系统。

Abstract: Accountability means a decision can be examined, justified, and contested. LLMs make this hard: fluent output may be ungrounded, incomplete, or unfaithful to the decision process. Achieving accountability requires verified rationales (how was the decision reached), assumptions (what was assumed rather than known), policy consistency (the same treatment for the same facts), and pivotal conditions (what would change the outcome). We introduce self-faithfulness as an automatic test of accountability: changing the pivotal conditions should change the decision.
  We examine accountable AI through clinical trial matching, a high-stakes task central to evidence-based medicine. Although LLM-based matchers match patients to trials reasonably accurately, they apply decision policies inconsistently and produce rationales that are unfaithful to their own decisions.
  We introduce VERDICT, an LLM-based agent that translates a decision task, its constraints, and its policy into Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT), then derives the decision with SMT and MaxSMT solvers -- so policies are applied consistently and decisions are accountable by construction.
  Across a SIGIR 2016-derived dataset and TREC 2021, VERDICT achieves the strongest decision accuracy among LLM-only and neurosymbolic baselines, applies policies with perfect consistency, and produces clinician-preferred rationales grounded in explicit assumptions and pivotal conditions, with improved counterfactual self-faithfulness.

</details>


### [138] [FrameBench:A Language Understanding Benchmark Based on Frame Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03370)
*Chihiro Yano,Ryohei Sasano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FrameBench基准，用于评估大语言模型(LLM)是否能在句子理解中复现人类基于框架语义(frame semantics)进行的隐含信息补全能力。基准通过多选问题测试模型是否能区分同一动词在不同语境下所唤起的语义框架，涵盖英语和日语，实验发现小模型面临挑战，而多个大模型超过了人类参考分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在框架语义学中，句子理解依赖将词汇意义与背景知识（语义框架）联系，从而隐式补全未明说的信息。虽然LLM在下游任务表现优异，但其是否能像人类一样在理解中进行这种隐式信息补全仍不清楚。本文旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 作者引入FrameBench基准，它由多选问题构成，测试模型能否区分同一动词在不同上下文中唤起的语义框架。基准使用FrameNet风格资源为英语和日语构建，采用"生成-验证"流水线并结合母语者判断来确保质量。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验表明，小模型在此任务上面临显著挑战，而多个大模型的表现超过了人类参考分数。

Conclusion: FrameBench为评估LLM是否具备类人的框架语义理解与隐含信息补全能力提供了一个多语言基准，结果显示大模型在该能力上已能超越人类表现。数据集与代码已公开发布供后续研究使用。

Abstract: In frame semantics, sentence comprehension is assumed to proceed by relating lexical meaning to background knowledge called semantic frames, thereby enabling readers to implicitly enrich the text with unstated information. Recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether they can reproduce the kinds of implicit enrichment that humans naturally make during comprehension. To address this question, we introduce FrameBench, a benchmark grounded in frame semantics. FrameBench consists of multiple-choice questions that test whether models distinguish the frames evoked by the same verb across contexts. We construct the benchmark for English and Japanese using FrameNet-style resources and a generation-and-verification pipeline with native-speaker judgments. Our experiments on a diverse set of models reveal challenges for small models, while several large models surpass the human reference scores. We release the constructed FrameBench dataset and the code for dataset construction and evaluation at https://github.com/SasanoLab/FrameBench.

</details>


### [139] [Chiaroscuro for Emotions: A Contrastive Emotion Benchmark Grounded in Appraisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03394)
*Divyesh Bommana,Mohammad Saim,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHIARO，一个包含1,000条人工标注句子的对比性情绪推理基准数据集，其核心特点是同一事件引发两人相反情绪（一方积极、一方消极）。评测显示最强LLM仅达67.3宏F1（低于人类一致性），现成情绪分类器接近随机水平；该数据集还可作为训练信号提升下游分类器在多个外部基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪识别基准通常为单个文本预测一种情绪，无法覆盖两人从同一共享事件中产生相反情绪的真实场景（如孩子兴奋踢前排座椅而乘客生气），缺乏对此类对比性情绪推理的评测资源。

Method: 基于评价理论（appraisal theory）构建CHIARO数据集，每个场景描述一个因果触发物在两个人物身上分别引发积极与消极情绪，采用十类情绪分类体系。招募人类标注员完成1,000条句子标注，并评测7个前沿大语言模型与4个现成情绪分类器。

Result: 最强LLM在CHIARO上达到67.3宏F1，显著低于人类一致性水平；现成情绪分类器表现接近随机猜测。将CHIARO与现有情绪语料结合训练的模型在CHIARO自身及十个外部情绪基准中的六个上均取得提升。

Conclusion: CHIARO既是一个具有挑战性的对比性情绪推理评测基准，同时也可作为互补性训练信号，弥补现有情绪识别方法在多人相反情绪场景中的不足。

Abstract: Emotion recognition benchmarks often predict one emotion per text, missing many real-world scenarios where two people arrive at opposing emotions from a single shared event. For example, a child kicks the seat in front of her in excitement while the passenger ahead grows angry. We introduce CHIARO, a 1,000 human-annotated sentence benchmark for contrastive emotion inference grounded in appraisal theory. Each scene describes one causal trigger eliciting a positive emotion in one person and a negative emotion in the other, drawn from a ten-class taxonomy. We benchmark seven frontier LLMs and four off-the-shelf emotion classifiers. The strongest LLM reaches 67.3 macro-F1, well below human agreement, while existing emotion classifiers score near chance. Beyond evaluation, CHIARO also serves as a training signal. When combined with an existing emotion corpus, the resulting downstream classifier improves on CHIARO itself and on six of ten external emotion benchmarks, which positions our dataset as a complementary signal for emotion recognition.

</details>


### [140] [TabScope: Question-Adaptive Scope Selection for Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03395)
*Yuxiang Wang,Junhao Gan,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自适应框架，根据问题类型动态选择局部化推理或全表推理来处理长表格问答，通过操作感知的表格分解构建问题特定子表，实验表明自适应选择优于单一推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在表格问答中随表格增大性能下降，且这种下降在不同问题类型间不均匀——对局部化敏感的问题受无关表格内容影响大，而需要更广泛证据的问题可能受益于全表推理。这提示长表QA不仅需要决定如何局部化，还需要决定何时局部化。

Method: 提出问题自适应框架：1) 通过操作感知的表格分解构建问题特定的子表；2) 预测问题类型以决定采用局部化推理还是全表推理；3) 引入银标准参考子表用于评估证据选择；4) 构建基于真实世界长表的SLQA基准。

Result: 在WikiTQ和SLQA上的实验表明，局部化对于查找和局部推理类问题特别有效，而局部化与全表推理的自适应选择实现了最佳总体性能。

Conclusion: 长表问答的关键挑战不仅是"如何局部化"（如何选择相关子表），还包括"何时局部化"（判断是否需要局部化），需要根据问题类型动态决定推理策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on table question answering, yet their accuracy often degrades as table size increases. We find that this degradation is not uniform across question types. Localization-sensitive questions are particularly affected by irrelevant table content, while questions requiring broader evidence may still benefit from full-table reasoning. Based on this observation, we propose a question-adaptive framework that dynamically selects between localized and full-table reasoning. The framework constructs question-specific sub-tables through operation-aware table decomposition and uses the predicted question type to determine the appropriate reasoning mode. We further introduce silver reference sub-tables for evaluating evidence selection and construct SLQA, a benchmark based on real-world long tables. Experiments on WikiTQ and SLQA show that localization is particularly effective for lookup and local reasoning questions, while adaptive selection between localized and full-table reasoning achieves the best overall performance. These results highlight that long-table QA requires deciding not only how to localize, but also when to localize. Our code and datasets will be made available upon publication of the paper.

</details>


### [141] [To What Extent Do Large Language Models Understand Bangla Idioms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03410)
*Mousumi Akter,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了孟加拉语习语的大规模基准数据集及配套的合成选择题数据集，并在释义、习语跨度检测和词义识别三项任务上对多个大语言模型进行了零样本和少样本评估。结果显示不同模型在不同任务上表现各异，无单一模型全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语是自然语言的重要组成部分，承载着文化内涵，但在低资源语言中给计算模型带来独特挑战。目前缺乏针对孟加拉语习语的大规模评测资源，亟需构建基准数据集并系统评估大语言模型在该语言习语理解上的能力。

Method: 构建了首个大规模孟加拉语习语基准数据集及合成多项选择（MCQ）数据集用于习语词义识别；采用零样本和少样本提示策略，在释义、习语跨度检测和词义识别三项任务上对近期大语言模型进行全面评测。

Result: 不同模型在各项任务中表现差异显著，无单一模型在所有任务中一致优于其他模型。Phi-4-mini-instruct在释义任务中表现最佳，Kimi-K2-32b-instruct在跨度检测任务中表现最佳，Gemini-2.5-flash在词义识别任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 所构建的数据集和系统性分析为未来改进大语言模型对习语表达（尤其是孟加拉语及其他低资源语言）的理解提供了宝贵资源，可作为后续研究的基准与参考。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions are an integral part of natural language, reflecting cultural nuances and posing unique challenges for computational models, particularly in low-resource languages. In this paper, we present the first large-scale benchmark dataset of Bangla idioms, complemented by a synthetic multiple-choice question (MCQ) dataset for idiom meaning identification. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of recent large language models (LLMs) across three idiom-related tasks: paraphrasing, idiom span detection, and meaning identification, leveraging zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies. Our results reveal substantial variability in model performance, with no single LLM consistently outperforming others across all tasks. Notably, Phi-4-mini-instruct excels in paraphrasing, Kimi-K2-32b-instruct in span detection, and Gemini-2.5-flash in meaning identification. We believe that our datasets and analyses will provide valuable resources to guide future research in improving LLM comprehension of idiomatic expressions, particularly in Bangla and other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [142] [Lngram v2: Latent N-Gram Memory with Interpretable Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03426)
*Yunao Zheng,Bin Wen,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram v2 是一种解耦路由数、记忆维度和骨干宽度的离散潜在条件记忆机制，通过上下文感知的组查询注意力读出和零值 Sink 与反事实替代梯度等技术，实现了记忆容量独立扩展，在不增加记忆参数的同时提升多规模 VLM 的语言建模性能，并在 30B 参数模型上成功扩展，其离散 ID 还保留了语义结构，可作为分析模型内部表征的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 缺乏原生的查找机制，需要重复的密集计算来识别和复用局部静态模式。Lngram v1 通过离散潜在 n-gram 寻址引入了与 tokenizer 无关的条件记忆，但其记忆容量与骨干网络宽度耦合，高参数和激活成本限制了可扩展性，因此需要一种能够独立扩展记忆容量的高效记忆机制。

Method: Lngram v2 解耦了路由数量、记忆维度和骨干宽度三个关键维度，引入上下文感知的组查询注意力读出机制以独立扩展记忆容量；同时采用零值 Sink 和反事实替代梯度改进读出选择性和路由可训练性，同时保持硬离散寻址特性。

Result: 在不同规模的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）上实验均显示一致改进，成功扩展到 30B 参数模型。相比 Lngram v1，Lngram v2 大幅减少总记忆参数和激活记忆参数，同时保持或提升语言建模性能。离散 ID 保留了连续隐状态的大量语义结构，仅凭 ID 即可进行语义恢复，且 ID-语义关联在不同数据集间保持稳定。

Conclusion: Lngram v2 被确立为一种高效且可扩展的潜在条件记忆机制，其离散寻址不仅改善了记忆性能，还为分析模型内部表征提供了结构化的接口，表明离散条件记忆在大规模模型中的实用性和可解释性价值。

Abstract: Transformers lack a native lookup mechanism, requiring repeated dense computation to recognize and reuse local static patterns. Lngram v1 introduces tokenizer-independent conditional memory through discrete latent n-gram addressing, but its memory capacity is coupled with the backbone width, limiting scalability due to high parameter and activation costs. We propose Lngram v2, which decouples the number of routes, memory dimension, and backbone width, and introduces a context-aware grouped-query attention readout to scale memory capacity independently. A zero-value Sink and counterfactual surrogate gradients further improve readout selectivity and routing trainability while preserving hard discrete addressing. Experiments across vision--language models (VLMs) of different scales show consistent improvements, including successful scaling to a 30B-parameter model. Compared with Lngram v1, Lngram v2 substantially reduces both total and activated memory parameters while maintaining or improving language modeling performance. Further analysis shows that its discrete IDs preserve substantial semantic structure of continuous hidden states, enabling semantic recovery from IDs alone and stable ID--semantic associations across datasets. These results establish Lngram v2 as an efficient and scalable latent conditional memory mechanism whose discrete addresses also provide a structured interface for analyzing internal model representations.

</details>


### [143] [Random Attention: Rethinking KV Cache Eviction for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03430)
*Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Liangwei Yang,Jiawei Han,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现KV缓存压缩中的token选择信号几乎不起作用，提出Random Attention——不计算任何评分、在每个注意力头内均匀随机逐出token，即可在四个模型和六个推理任务上匹配最强先验逐出器，同时vLLM部署吞吐量高出32-43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要长链推理的任务上表现优异，但长思维链使得KV缓存成为严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法都遵循同一范式：为每个缓存token打分以估计其未来重要性并保留高分者。作者质疑这一评分范式，希望探究选择信号是否真的必要。

Method: 提出Random Attention方法：保留prompt部分，在每个注意力头内均匀随机逐出token，完全不计算任何评分。通过与最强先验逐出器对比进行受控实验，检验选择信号的作用。实验从两个层面解释：1) prompt是缓存中最脆弱的部分，选择器之间的差距大多仅取决于其选择信号是否恰好保留了prompt；2) 推理轨迹通过文本层面（模型在工作时重述其仍需内容）和跨注意力头层面（每个头保留自己的轨迹副本）的冗余来自我保护，因此只要prompt安全，随机抽取便能保留足够所需副本。

Result: 随机注意力在四个模型和六个推理任务上匹配最强先验逐出器的性能，同时vLLM部署中吞吐量高32-43%。受控实验证实选择信号本身几乎无贡献，真正决定性能差异的是prompt是否被保留。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩中复杂的评分选择机制可能是被过度设计的冗余，随机逐出在推理任务中已足够有效。这源于推理轨迹自身的双层次冗余结构（文本重述+头间副本），使得无评分的选择信号即可维持性能。

Abstract: Large language models achieve superior performance on tasks that require extended reasoning, but long chains of thought make the KV cache a severe memory bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods share one paradigm: score each cached token by some estimate of how much it will matter later, and keep the top-scoring ones. We show that the selection signal contributes almost nothing. Random Attention keeps the prompt and evicts uniformly at random within each attention head, computing no score at all; across four models and six reasoning tasks it matches the strongest prior evictor while serving 32-43% higher throughput than it in vLLM deployment. Controlled experiments explain this by showing that 1) the prompt is the fragile part of the cache, and most of the gap between selectors is just whether their selection signal happened to keep it; 2) the reasoning trace protects itself against eviction with redundancy at two levels, in the text (the model restates what it still needs as it works) and across attention heads (each keeps its own copy of the trace), so once the prompt is safe, a random draw retains enough copies of what the model still needs, and no score is required to pick them. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/Random-Attention.

</details>


### [144] [Decoupled Analysis-Judging: An Automated Creativity Evaluator Using LLMs in Complex Multi-step Creativity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03432)
*Xiangyu Wang,Jin Wu,Xiaoyu Li,Chanjin Zheng,Yifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CreaEval是一个面向上下文驱动的程序化结构任务（CGPST）的自动化创造力评估器，它将LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为记忆增强分析和基于证据的评判两个阶段，以缓解偏差并提升评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge在创造力任务评估中易受冗长偏差和宽大偏差影响，尤其在CGPST这类多步骤、高主观性、评分区间宽的复杂任务上判断更不稳定和偏差更大。现有方法（任务特定训练或直接使用LLM-as-a-Judge）均难以保证复杂场景下的可靠评估。

Method: CreaEval将典型LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为两个阶段：（1）记忆增强分析阶段，使用SoT-LLM将多步骤响应转化为结构化评估证据，并融入跨步骤记忆；（2）基于证据的评判阶段，Judge-LLM仅基于提取的证据进行评分，不直接访问原始响应，从而避免偏差干扰。

Result: 在CGPST及两个经典简单创造力任务上，CreaEval相较第二名基线平均提升22.74%的性能，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过"分析-评判"的解耦架构，CreaEval有效提升了LLM-as-a-Judge在复杂创造力评估任务中的准确性和稳定性，为高复杂度、主观性任务的自动评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of creativity tasks remains challenging for LLM-as-a-Judge, as LLM is susceptible to biases such as verbosity bias and leniency bias. Such limitations are particularly evident in Contextually-Grounded and Procedurally-Structured Tasks (CGPST), a complex multi-step creativity task where inter-step dependencies, highly subjectivity, and wide scoring ranges lead to more unstable and biased judgments. Existing approaches either rely on task-specific training or directly apply LLM-as-a-Judge, both of which struggle to ensure reliable evaluation under such complexity. To bridge these gaps, we propose CreaEval, an automated creativity evaluator for CGPST that decouples typical LLM-as-a-Judge into analysis and judging. Correspondingly, CreaEval involves two critical phases: Memory-augmented Analysis, a SoT-LLM converts multi-step responses into structured evaluation evidence, incorporating cross-step memory; and Evidence-based Judging, a Judge-LLM uses the extracted evidence for judging without accessing raw responses. Comprehensive experiments show that CreaEval achieves an average performance improvement of 22.74% over the second-best baselines across CGPST and two classic simple creativity tasks, demonstrating its generalizability. The code is available at https://github.com/Jaong/CreaEval.

</details>


### [145] [When Retrieval Helps: Selective Retrieval for Single-Turn Mental-Health QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03454)
*Hyunseo Oh,Chong-Kwon Kim,Yoonhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在单轮心理健康问答场景中，检索增强生成（RAG）并非总是有益。作者提出了一种轻量级的选择性检索策略，通过基于草稿的三维效用（心理教育需求、应对需求、响应特异性）和安全规则触发器来判断何时需要检索，实验表明选择性检索在低需求场景保留闭卷行为，避免无条件检索带来的额外质量退化和安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在通用场景中能提升大语言模型响应的具体性和事实基础，但在单轮心理健康问答中并非均匀有益——用户查询常混合情绪困扰、治疗关切与安全敏感需求。研究者希望搞清楚检索何时改善、何时损害心理健康问答质量，并探索能否用轻量级选择性检索策略更好地控制这种权衡。

Method: 作者将检索需求操作化为三个基于草稿的效用维度：心理教育需求、应对需求及响应特异性，并结合基于规则的触发器处理安全敏感情形。参考心理治疗启发的RAG系统（如coTherapist），构建了紧凑可控的指南语料库（涵盖应对策略、心理教育和安全资源）。使用QLoRA在MentalChat16K上微调指令型生成器，对比Closed-book、Always Retrieval（总是检索）和Selective Retrieval（选择性检索）三种设置在CounselBench-Eval和CounselBench-Adv上的表现。

Result: 实验显示检索在心理健康领域并非始终有益：Always Retrieval虽然提升了响应特异性，却降低了整体质量并引入了额外的安全敏感失败；而Selective Retrieval在低需求场景保持闭卷行为，同时避免了无条件检索所带来的附加质量退化。

Conclusion: 选择性检索有效控制检索激活的权衡，支持将检索激活视为一种安全敏感的控制决策的观点——即在心理健康问答中不应无条件启用RAG，而应根据具体需求动态决定是否检索，以兼顾响应质量与安全性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve the specificity and grounding of large language model responses, but its effect is not uniformly beneficial in single-turn mental-health question answering, where user queries often combine emotional distress, treatment concerns, and safety-sensitive needs. We study when retrieval helps or hurts mental-health QA, and whether a lightweight selective retrieval policy can better control this trade-off. We operationalize retrieval need using three draft-conditioned utility dimensions: psychoeducational need, coping need, and response specificity, together with a rule-based safety trigger. Following psychotherapy-grounded RAG systems such as coTherapist, we construct a compact and controllable guideline corpus comprising coping-strategy, psychoeducational, and safety resources. We fine-tune an instruction-tuned generator on MentalChat16K using QLoRA and compare Closed-book, Always Retrieval, and Selective Retrieval settings on CounselBench-Eval and CounselBench-Adv. Experiments show that retrieval is not uniformly beneficial in this domain. Always Retrieval improves specificity but lowers overall quality and introduces additional safety-sensitive failures. Selective Retrieval preserves closed-book behavior for low-need cases while avoiding the additional degradation caused by unconditional retrieval, supporting the view that retrieval activation is a safety-sensitive control decision.

</details>


### [146] [When Users Don't Ask: Benchmarking Context-Driven Memory Retrieval in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03467)
*Wen-Yu Chang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文介绍了LOCOMO-CONV，一个基于LoCoMo派生的对话式记忆基准，包含四种查询风格（对话式、隐式、反事实和组合式）。通过评估五种代表性记忆系统，发现对话式框架暴露了QA基准忽略的检索差距，尤其是在隐式和组合式查询上；强检索能力并不完全转化为响应质量，且隐式查询存在"静默锚定"现象。研究指出基于推理的记忆深化是未来方向，并发布了辅助性的支持性记忆标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要通过QA式探测评估记忆系统，而非在实际对话场景中原位使用，导致对话场景中的记忆系统性能被高估或未充分暴露。

Method: 从LoCoMo派生构建LOCOMO-CONV对话基准，设计了四种查询风格（dialog、implicit、counterfactual、composed），对五种代表性记忆系统同时评估检索召回率和端到端响应质量，并引入多面查询改写和支持性记忆标注。

Result: 对话式框架暴露了QA基准忽略的显著检索差距，尤其体现在隐式和组合式查询；多面查询改写能改善原始轮次记忆但对抽象记忆无效；强检索不完全转化为响应质量；隐式查询表现出静默锚定现象。

Conclusion: 结论指向基于推理的记忆深化是未来有前景的方向，作者发布了超越原始金证据的、对话上有用的辅助性支持性记忆标注以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increas- ingly deployed as long-horizon conversational agents, motivating growing interest in mem- ory systems. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate memory through QA-style probing rather than in-situ conversational usage. We introduce LOCOMO-CONV, a conversa- tional memory benchmark derived from Lo- CoMo with four query styles: dialog, implicit, counterfactual, and composed. Across five rep- resentative memory systems, we evaluate both retrieval recall and end-to-end response qual- ity. Our experiments show that conversational framing exposes substantial retrieval gaps over- looked by QA benchmarks, especially on im- plicit and composed queries, which multi-facet query rewriting narrows for raw-turn mem- ory but not abstractive memory. We further find that strong retrieval does not fully trans- late into response quality, and that implicit queries exhibit silent grounding, where mem- ory improves contextual grounding without ex- plicitly surfacing the gold fact. These results point to reasoning-based memory elaboration as a promising direction, and we release aux- iliary supportive_memory annotations captur- ing conversationally useful context beyond the original gold evidence.

</details>


### [147] [Pattern Over-Generalization of Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03487)
*Junsik Kim,Kangil Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PogRE方法，通过稠密线性变换和复合操作来表示关系，解决知识图谱嵌入中模式过度泛化（pattern over-generalization）导致的推理模式被普遍泛化的问题，在三个标准基准数据集上的链接预测任务中优于现有最先进的KGE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识图谱嵌入（KGE）模型虽然能够建模对称/反对称、反转和组合等多样推理模式，但存在固有的模式过度泛化缺陷：从单一模式实例中学到的嵌入会不可避免地将该模式普遍化到所有相关实例上，导致错误的链接预测。

Method: 提出PogRE框架，使用稠密线性变换和复合操作来表示关系。理论分析表明，稠密线性变换允许模式随着观察到更多三元组而逐渐变得普遍化；当观察到d+1个线性独立的实体（d为实体维度）后，线性变换可保证模式在所有相关实例上实现普遍泛化。

Result: 在三个标准基准数据集上的实验表明，PogRE在链接预测任务中优于现有最先进的KGE模型；实证结果进一步表明PogRE有效缓解了过度泛化的负面影响。

Conclusion: PogRE通过稠密线性变换和复合操作的设计，在理论上界定了模式普遍泛化的收敛条件，并在实验中验证了其缓解过度泛化问题及提升链接预测性能的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) demonstrates its effectiveness for predicting missing links in knowledge graphs (KGs) by projecting entities and relations into a low-dimensional vector space. It is crucial for KGE models to effectively capture inference patterns (patterns) inherent in KGs, such as symmetry/antisymmetry, inversion and composition. Although recent KGE models exhibit strong capabilities in modeling such diverse patterns, they suffer from inherent limitations stemming from pattern over-generalization, where embeddings learned from only a single pattern instance inevitably generalize that pattern to all related instances, i.e., generalize the pattern universally. To address this issue, we propose PogRE (Pattern Over-Generalization Robust Embedding), a simple but effective method that utilizes dense linear transformations and compound operations for relation representation. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that a dense linear transformation allows a pattern to become progressively universal as more triples are observed in the pattern. Furthermore, after observing d+1 linearly independent entities (d+1 denotes the dimension of entity), the linear transformation guarantees universal generalization of the pattern across all related instances. Experimental results on three standard benchmark datasets show that PogRE outperforms existing state-of-the-art KGE models in link prediction. Moreover, our empirical results indicate that PogRE effectively addresses the negative impact of over-generalization.

</details>


### [148] [Building and Evaluating Fixed-Voice Thai TTS from Synthetic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03502)
*Kunat Pipatanakul,Potsawee Manakul,Warit Sirichotedumrong,Sittipong Sripaisarnmongkol,Pakorn Nathong,Phatrasek Jirabovonvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究第三条路径：在低资源泰语TTS场景下，利用大型声音克隆模型作为可编程数据源，将15秒短语音参考转换为完全在合成语音上训练的紧凑型固定语音学生模型（Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge，82M参数），实现了无需参考音频的端侧泰语TTS。该模型在Challenge-Set关键词准确率上达到68.2%（Gemini 3.1的85.5%），停顿精度91.4%（超过其OmniVoice教师模型的89.9%），泰语和英语CER分别仅为3.7%和1.1%，并开源了模型与评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源环境中，现有TTS部署面临两难：大型声音克隆模型推理成本高，而紧凑固定语音系统又需要说话者特定语料库。作者探索第三条路径，利用大型克隆模型作为数据源来训练紧凑型固定语音学生模型，同时泰语特有的词边界模糊、声调、专有名词、数字朗读和泰英混合编码等挑战使流水线设计至关重要。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏思路：用大型声音克隆模型作为教师，基于短语音参考（约15秒）合成大规模训练数据，训练82M参数的紧凑型固定语音学生模型。系统性地研究了文本准备、合成生成、质量过滤、拒绝采样和前端的各个环节对最终学生模型的影响，并分析了教师模型的局限性所在。

Result: Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge在Challenge-Set关键词准确率上达68.2%（Gemini 3.1的85.5%），停顿精度91.4%（超过OmniVoice教师的89.9%，达Gemini 3.1的94.8%），并在三个系统中取得最低的停顿位置错误率和词内停顿率，泰语CER 3.7%、英语CER 1.1%。

Conclusion: 基于大模型合成数据训练的紧凑型固定语音模型是低资源TTS的可行路径，且可能在某些维度上超越教师模型；模型与评估框架已开源以促进泰语TTS发展。

Abstract: In low-resource settings, deploying TTS typically requires choosing between a large voice-cloning model with costly inference or a compact fixed-voice system that requires a speaker-specific corpus. We study a third route: using a large voice-cloning model as a programmable data source to turn a short voice reference (e.g., 15 seconds) into a compact fixed-voice student trained entirely on synthetic speech. This setting makes pipeline design consequential: teacher errors become training targets, while filtering failed generations can reduce coverage of difficult texts. Thai further introduces challenges from ambiguous word boundaries, lexical tone, names and loanwords, numeric verbalization, and Thai-English code-switching. We study how text preparation, synthetic generation, quality filtering, rejection sampling, and frontend choices affect the resulting student, and where teacher limitations remain. We evaluate CER, Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy, Prosody Pause Accuracy, speaker similarity, and speaking rate. The resulting 82M-parameter model, Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge, enables on-device Thai TTS without reference audio. It achieves 68.2% Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy (85.5% of Gemini 3.1) and 91.4% pause precision, outperforming its OmniVoice teacher (89.9%) and reaching 94.8% of Gemini 3.1. It also achieves the lowest pause-placement error and intra-word pause rates among the three systems, and 3.7% and 1.1% CER on Thai and English, respectively. We open-source the model and evaluation framework for Thai TTS development.

</details>


### [149] [Lost in Reordering: Structural Sensitivity of Multilingual LLMs under Semantics-Preserving Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03511)
*Karthika Nhayakkat,Rajat Verma,Maharaj Brahma,Vetcha Gnana Mahesh,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar,Ganesh Ramakrishnan,Rohit Saluja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言大语言模型对语义等价但结构变化（语序重排和主动-被动语态转换）的鲁棒性，构建了IndicReStruct基准（含GSM8K-Reordered和GSM8K-Voice两个变体），在印地语和马拉雅拉姆语上对六个SOTA LLM进行测试，发现其数学推理性能在结构扰动下显著下降，并通过错误分析及激活修补实验揭示失败源于实体-数量对齐被破坏以及中间Transformer层对推理恢复贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM的多语言推理能力，但对语义等价的句法结构变化（特别是在自由语序语言中）的鲁棒性缺乏系统性探索。理解LLM是否具备组合不变性，对评估其真正的语言理解深度至关重要。

Method: 作者基于GSM8K构建了IndicReStruct基准，包含两种语言（印地语、马拉雅拉姆语）和两种语言学驱动的扰动设置：受限成分重排（GSM8K-Reordered）和主动-被动语态转换（GSM8K-Voice），在保持语义不变的前提下改变表层句法结构。随后在六个SOTA多语言LLM上、结合多种提示策略进行评估，并辅以定性错误分析和基于残差流激活修补（activation patching）的机制可解释性实验。

Result: 研究发现所有模型在结构扰动下均出现一致且显著的数学推理性能下降；错误分析表明推理失败常源于实体-数量对齐（entity-quantity alignment）的破坏；机制可解释性实验显示中间Transformer层对推理恢复的贡献最强。

Conclusion: 当前多语言LLM对表层句法实现高度敏感，缺乏面对结构不同但语义等价输入时的组合不变性（compositional invariance），其多语言推理能力建立在脆弱的表层句法模式上而非稳固的语义理解之上。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong multilingual reasoning performance, yet their robustness to semantics-preserving structural variation remains underexplored, particularly for relatively free word-order languages. We investigate the structural sensitivity of multilingual LLMs using two linguistically grounded perturbation settings in Hindi and Malayalam: constrained constituent reordering and active-passive voice transformation. We introduce a benchmark dataset IndicReStruct, with two variants, GSM8K-Reordered and GSM8K-Voice, constructed from GSM8K while preserving semantic meaning. Across six state-of-the-art LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, we observe consistent and significant degradation in mathematical reasoning performance under structurally perturbed inputs. To further understand these failures, we perform qualitative error analysis and mechanistic interpretability experiments using residual-stream activation patching. Our analyses show that reasoning failures frequently arise from disruptions in entity-quantity alignment and that intermediate transformer layers contribute most strongly toward reasoning restoration. Overall, our findings suggest that current multilingual LLMs remain highly sensitive to surface syntactic realization and lack robust compositional invariance under structurally different but semantically equivalent inputs.

</details>


### [150] [Language, Language Models, and What We're Talking About](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03577)
*Malvina Nissim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以意大利语语言模型为证据，质疑在翻译和合成数据上训练、微调并进一步策展语言模型，再在同样不自然的数据上测试这些模型的做法。作者认为需要厘清“作为技术产品的语言模型”与“作为研究语言本身工具的语言模型”之间的区别，才能回答“我们究竟希望语言模型产出什么样的语言”这一根本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被视为纯粹的技术产物，但实际上它们受到训练数据所传达的语言世界的影响。作者希望通过意大利语语言模型的案例，引起学界对训练在翻译与合成数据上的模型系统本质的关注，并质疑在这些不自然数据上进行测试的意义，进而追问：这些模型究竟是意大利语模型吗？是语言模型吗？NLP是否仍然关心语言本身？

Method: 本文采取批判性论证与概念辨析的方法：以意大利语语言模型为实证案例，系统性地检视翻译数据与合成数据在预训练、专精化训练及后续策展过程中对模型语言能力的影响，并在概念层面区分两类语言模型——技术产品型（为工程任务设计）与语言研究工具型（为探索语言本质服务）。

Result: 作者并非给出一个统一答案，而是指出当区分了技术产品型与语言研究工具型语言模型之后，“我们想要什么语言”这一问题的答案可能多样化：不同目的对应不同的语言定义与质量标准。这一视角或许会使整体图景并不像人们担心的那样悲观。

Conclusion: 要回答语言模型应产出何种语言，必须先厘清技术产品与语言研究工具两类模型的本质差异。承认这种区分后，关于语言多样性的答案可能各不相同，但前景未必悲观——关键在于更清晰地界定模型的定位与研究目标。

Abstract: Language models are commonly discussed as technical artefacts, but they are obviously shaped by the linguistic worlds conveyed by data during their training. Using Italian language models as evidence, I want to bring attention to the nature of the systems which result from training and specialising models on translated and synthetic data, and further curating them, and to the meaning of testing them on equally unnatural data. Are these eventually models of Italian? Are they models of language? Does NLP still care about language? These questions yield another, more concrete question: what language do we actually want language models to produce? I argue that this question cannot be answered if we do not first consider a clearer distinction between language models designed as technical products and language models designed as tools for studying language itself. The answers then might be diverse, the languages we are talking about might be diverse, and the picture might not be as pessimistic as we fear.

</details>


### [151] [How Far Can Synthetic Data Take Thai OCR?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03595)
*Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索合成OCR监督如何有效迁移到真实泰文文档，基于受控文档重建实验解耦影响迁移的因素（字体、版面、文本上下文、字形），据此构建Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero模型：在无真实OCR标签下用45,723张合成页面微调0.9B参数的PaddleOCR-VL-1.6，将打印文档中位字符错误率从6.64%降至1.24%，手写文档从74.87%降至20.55%，在所有五个评测集上均优于Typhoon OCR v1 7B，证明纯合成训练具备竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实泰文文档缺少OCR标注，而合成数据虽能提供精确标签但"真实性"混杂了源域、页面上下文、排版、空间结构与字形变化等多个因素，缺乏对这些因素如何影响迁移的系统性理解，阻碍了高效合成数据管线的设计。

Method: 采用受控的文档重建管线解耦字体多样性、版面二维结构、非文本上下文与真实手写字形等因素；在页面级与裁剪级两种训练粒度下，针对打印与手写泰文文档分别评测各变体的迁移效果；基于发现将PaddleOCR-VL-1.6适配为Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero，使用45,723张合成页面进行训练。

Result: 非文本上下文影响微弱且不稳定；字体多样性、二维版面结构与真实手写字形显著提升迁移。源域匹配依赖训练粒度：页面级训练下域内重建接近真实打印监督（1.82% vs 1.31%中位CER），而裁剪级训练下域内重建反而逊于跨域（15.59% vs 5.52%）。Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero将打印CER从6.64%降至1.24%、手写从74.87%降至20.55%，并在全部五个评测集超越Typhoon OCR v1 7B。

Conclusion: 合成OCR数据的迁移效果取决于具体因素与训练粒度，而非笼统的"真实性"；通过有针对性地纳入字体多样性、二维版面与真实手写字形，纯合成训练的0.9B级模型可以接近甚至优于更大规模的真实数据模型，为低资源语言的OCR标注困境提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate what makes synthetic OCR supervision transfer to real Thai documents and use the resulting insights to build Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero, a Thai OCR model adapted without OCR labels from real Thai document pages. Synthetic data provide exact labels at scale, but "realism" conflates source domain, page context, typography, spatial structure, and glyph variation. We disentangle these factors with a controlled document-reconstruction pipeline and evaluate each variant under page- and crop-level training on printed and handwritten Thai documents. Non-text context has little consistent effect, whereas typeface diversity, two-dimensional structure, and real handwriting glyphs improve transfer; moreover, source-domain matching depends on training granularity, with in-domain reconstruction approaching real printed supervision under page-level training (1.82% versus 1.31% median character error rate) but underperforming out-of-domain reconstruction under crop-level training (15.59% versus 5.52%). Guided by these findings, we adapt the 0.9B-parameter PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 into Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero using 45,723 synthetic pages: relative to its base checkpoint, it reduces median character error rate from 6.64% to 1.24% on printed pages and from 74.87% to 20.55% on handwriting and outperforms Typhoon OCR v1 7B on all five evaluation sets, showing that synthetic-only training can be competitive.

</details>


### [152] [KhatianDoc: A Human-Verified Benchmark Diagnosing Multimodal LLM Failure on Bengali Legal Land Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03597)
*Tasmiad Hasan,Arafat Zaman Ratul,Sarker Sadman Saalim,S. M. Shah Nawaz Hossain,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KhatianDoc基准，用于测试多模态大语言模型能否阅读孟加拉国土地所有权记录（RS Khatians）中的base-16分数系统（Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til）。基准包含四个任务、107份真实记录和1,634对QA。评估六个多模态LLM后发现：39.3%的QA类别无任何模型能答对，算术任务上所有模型均不如常量均值基线，表明这不是性能差距而是能力的完全缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国的土地所有权以base-16分数系统记录在RS Khatian手写文件中，这是数百万地块的权威权属记录且频繁涉及民事诉讼。然而，这些分数有专门的Unicode字形、无主流字体、无任何OCR管道或tokenizer覆盖，且此前没有任何基准测试过机器能否阅读这些记录。研究者希望填补这一空白，系统性地评估现有模型处理该领域任务的能力。

Method: 从孟加拉国Munshiganj的Vumi（土地）办公室收集107份真实RS Khatian记录，构建四任务基准：(1)符号识别、(2)base-16到十进制转换、(3)结构化字段提取、(4)基于1,634对QA的法律文档问答。地面真值由人工手写转录、土地法律从业者验证至完全一致，并通过位置token实现匿名化以保留多跳问题所需的指称区分。在固定零样本协议下评估六个多模态LLM（8B到72B+，含开源与闭源）。研究者还审计了自身评估指标，修正了拒绝评分bug并标记了膨胀的元数据指标。

Result: 五个QA类别中有39.3%的分层集合被所有模型零正确回答。在算术任务上，所有输出数字的模型表现都不如常量均值基线，精确匹配与近似匹配分数重合（表明模型是去相关的，不是近似）。评估中还发现两个方向相反的人工制品：修正了拒绝评分bug后报告修正分数，并将一个膨胀的元数据指标标记为上界。

Conclusion: KhatianDoc记录的不是现有系统的性能差距，而是能力的完全缺失。该基准附带经验证的地面真值，可供未来系统使用。代码和数据（含脱敏图像发布）均公开可用。

Abstract: Land ownership in Bangladesh is recorded in Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til, a base-16 positional fraction system with dedicated Unicode glyphs, no mainstream font, and no coverage in any OCR pipeline or tokenizer. The handwritten records that carry these fractions, RS Khatians, are the authoritative title record for millions of parcels and a frequent subject of civil litigation, yet no benchmark has asked whether a machine can read one. We introduce KhatianDoc, a four-task benchmark built from 107 real RS Khatian records from the Vumi (land) Office of Munshiganj, Bangladesh: symbol recognition, base-16-to-decimal conversion, structured field extraction, and legal document question answering over 1,634 QA pairs. Ground truth was transcribed by hand, verified by a land-law practitioner to full agreement, and anonymized through positional tokens that keep the referential distinctions multi-hop questions depend on. We evaluate six multimodal LLMs (8B to 72B+, open and closed) under a fixed zero-shot protocol. Five QA categories, 39.3% of our stratified set, return zero correct answers from every model; on the arithmetic task, every model that emits a number does worse than a constant-mean baseline, with exact- and near-match scores coinciding: decorrelation, not approximation. Auditing our own metrics surfaced two artifacts in opposite directions: we correct a refusal-scoring bug and report the fixed scores beside the originals, and flag an inflated metadata metric as an upper bound. KhatianDoc documents not a performance gap but the absence of a capability, with verified ground truth for future systems. Code and data, with a redacted image release, are publicly available.

</details>


### [153] [Remember and Reweight: Enhancing Multi-Agent Debate with Experience Memory and Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03619)
*Xuanfa Jin,Zhijian Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出R²-MAD框架，通过记忆历史辩论经验并动态重加权，缓解多智能体辩论中共享误解导致的错误放大问题，在多个基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论(MAD)虽能提升大语言模型推理能力，但存在"共享误解"的关键漏洞：当多数智能体初始收敛于错误答案时，辩论过程会放大而非纠正错误。现有方法主要处理同行偏差，但未解决智能体本身固有的概念先验偏差。

Method: 提出R²-MAD(Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate)框架，包含两个互补机制：(1)基于辩论状态感知的检索策略，根据当前共识程度动态检索相关历史证据以校准概念先验；(2)利用检索到的经验估计每个智能体的可靠性，生成置信权重以调节同行影响力。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，R²-MAD相较于现有的单智能体和MAD基线取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过记忆与重加权机制同时干预共享误解和同行偏见两类失败模式，R²-MAD能有效缓解MAD系统的系统性弱点，验证了利用历史辩论经验增强多智能体协同推理的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by having multiple agents iteratively refine their responses through discussion. However, MAD suffers from a critical vulnerability known as shared misconception: when a majority of agents initially converge on an incorrect answer, the debate process tends to amplify rather than correct the error. Existing methods primarily address peer skew but leave the agents' inherently biased concept priors unaddressed. To mitigate this systematic weakness, we propose R$^2$-MAD (Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate), a framework that equips agents with an experience memory accumulated from past debates. R$^2$-MAD intervenes on both failure modes through two complementary mechanisms: A debate-state-aware retrieval policy dynamically calibrates the concept prior by retrieving relevant historical evidence based on the current consensus level. Then these retrieved experiences provide a basis for estimating per-agent reliability, yielding confidence weights to modulate peer influence. Experiments on various benchmarks show that R$^2$-MAD achieves consistent improvements over existing single-agent and MAD baselines.

</details>


### [154] [</think> Doesn't Stop Reasoning: Analysis of Spurious CoT Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03633)
*Seunghee Koh,Sungjae Choi,Minchan Kwon,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型推理模型（LRM）中早期退出方法注入的结束思考令牌（EoT，</think>）并不总能触发干净的回答阶段，而可能产生"虚假CoT终止"——推理式生成继续进入回答阶段。作者提出Exit-token Attention Biasing (EAB)方法，通过增强对注入EoT的注意力来减少该现象，并在四个LRM、五个基准和两种早期退出方法上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理能提升LRM在复杂任务上的表现，但产生冗长的推理轨迹。近期无训练的早期退出方法通过在中间点注入EoT令牌来提前终止推理，但研究者发现该策略并不总能引发干净的回答阶段，这是一种被忽视的局限性，需要深入理解并加以改进。

Method: 作者首先系统分析注入EoT后模型的行为，发现回答阶段可能在模型重新生成另一个EoT之前就继续产生推理式内容，且这段跨度与早期退出节省的推理令牌数呈正比。基于对"对注入EoT关注不足导致虚假CoT终止"的假设，提出Exit-token Attention Biasing (EAB)方法，在推理时增强对注入EoT令牌的注意力权重。

Result: 在四个LRM、五个基准和两种早期退出方法上，EAB方法能有效减少虚假CoT终止现象并缩短回答阶段长度，支持了其核心假设。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过外部匹配模型显式think-block格式来控制LRM的局限性——注入EoT令牌虽符合格式要求，但并不保证能实现预期的推理到回答的转换。这为理解LRM控制机制和设计更有效的早期退出策略提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large reasoning models (LRMs) on complex tasks but often produces long, redundant traces. Recent training-free early-exit methods shorten these traces by choosing an intermediate point to stop reasoning.
