We show this design, which we call \emph{guide, not bind}, fails for two independent, precisely characterized reasons, and that directly repairing both restores it only partially. First, sequential penalty-ramping ALM suppresses a wrongly-forbidden true edge before any counterfactual check can detect it: we give three necessary conditions any adaptive relaxation must satisfy to avoid this (Proposition~\ref{prop:conditions}), prove that DADU---the natural relaxation rule this paper introduces as the object of study---violates all three (Corollary~\ref{cor:dadu_failure}), and confirm the failure across 3{,}072 training runs spanning graphs from 4 to 32 nodes, where a single wrong prior suppresses a true edge in 87--97\% of trials under DADU. Second, and independent of any fix to the mechanism, we prove in closed form that the standard correlation-matching objective ties a true edge and its reverse to an identical cost of exactly $2r^2$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:tie}), not because the underlying equal-variance model is unidentifiable, but because normalizing to correlation discards exactly the variance information that would make it identifiable; covariance matching instead separates the two directions by a provable margin of at least $w_0^4$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:separation}).

</details>


### [221] [Beyond Straightness: Non-Crossing Flow Matching via Quantile AlignTree Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03443)
*Junyi Lin,Mengyu Li,Jingxuan Hu,Kejun He,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAT-FM（Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching），一种基于分位数对齐树结构的高效结构化耦合策略，用于Flow Matching中的源分布与目标分布配对，以O(Nd log N)的构建时间和O(d)的逐对采样复杂度实现大规模高维生成任务的可扩展训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching的性能高度依赖源分布与目标分布之间耦合的质量。独立耦合常导致路径交叉和局部速度模糊，而基于OT的耦合构建成本过高，亟需一种兼具高效性与良好路径性质的耦合策略。

Method: QAT-FM通过分位数对齐的树结构在高斯先验与目标数据分布之间构建层次化耦合。具体包括：O(Nd log N)时间构建耦合、O(d)复杂度支持逐对源采样，并自然扩展到条件生成场景，在保持全局高斯对齐的同时实现结构化条件耦合。

Result: 理论上证明QAT耦合满足边际一致性，诱导无交叉的线性插值路径，并在中间时刻一致性地改善路径分离程度，从而缓解局部速度模糊问题。实验在多个基准数据集上表明QAT-FM在显著降低耦合构建成本的同时实现了有竞争力的生成性能。

Conclusion: QAT-FM提供了一种兼顾效率与理论保证的结构化耦合方案，在降低耦合构建开销的同时保持甚至改善生成质量，为大规模高维Flow Matching生成任务提供了一种实用的耦合策略。

Abstract: The performance of Flow Matching largely depends on the quality of the coupling between the source and target distributions. However, independent coupling often leads to path crossings and local velocity ambiguity, while OT-based couplings typically incur high construction costs. To address this challenge, we propose Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching (QAT-FM), an efficient structured coupling strategy that constructs a hierarchical coupling between a Gaussian prior and the target data distribution via a quantile-aligned tree structure. QAT-FM constructs the coupling in $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log N)$ time and supports per-pair source sampling with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ complexity, enabling scalable training for large-scale high-dimensional generative tasks. Theoretically, we prove that the QAT coupling satisfies marginal consistency, induces non-crossing linear interpolation paths, and consistently improves path separation at intermediate times compared with independent coupling, thereby alleviating local velocity ambiguity. QAT-FM further extends naturally to conditional generation, enabling structured conditional coupling while preserving global Gaussian alignment. Experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that QAT-FM achieves competitive generative performance while substantially reducing coupling construction cost.

</details>


### [222] [A Two-Stage Forecasting System for CPU Workload Prediction in Private Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03457)
*Ashir Javeed,Anton Borg,Håkan Grahn,Lars Lundberg,Dhyey Patel,Sogand Shirinbab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段集成云资源预测模型，先预测客户服务请求(TPS)，再据此估计CPU工作负载，采用XGBoost级联学习架构与自适应在线重训，在私有云真实数据上SMAPE低于7%，优于传统直接CPU预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云资源预测方法直接从历史资源迹线预测CPU负载，忽略了客户服务需求与后续资源消耗之间的依赖关系，导致预测精度与可解释性不足。

Method: 构建两阶段级联预测架构：第一阶段用XGBoost预测客户服务请求(TPS)，第二阶段将TPS预测结果输入另一XGBoost模型以估计未来CPU工作负载；采用扩展窗口策略进行自适应在线重训练以应对概念漂移。

Result: 在含十个应用的私有云真实迹线数据集上，多数应用SMAPE低于7%；最佳应用MAE为0.7372、RMSE为1.1866、SMAPE为3.57%、R2为0.9185。60步预测窗口内的漂移分析表明误差累积可控。

Conclusion: 与直接CPU预测方法相比，该两阶段集成模型在预测鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性上均有提升，适用于云环境中的主动资源管理与智能自动伸缩。

Abstract: Accurate cloud resource forecasting is essential for proactive resource provisioning, maintaining Quality of Service (QoS), and reducing operational costs in dynamic cloud environments. The existing forecasting approaches predominantly estimate future CPU workload directly from historical resource traces, which often overlook the relationship between customer service demand and subsequent resource consumption. This study proposes a two-stage integrated forecasting model that explicitly models this dependency by first forecasting customer service requests, expressed as Transactions Per Second (TPS), and subsequently estimating future CPU workload from the TPS forecast. Both the forecasting component and resource prediction component employed the XGBoost model within a cascaded learning architecture, complemented by adaptive online retraining using an expanding-window strategy to address concept drift in continuously evolving cloud workloads. The proposed work was evaluated using real-world traces collected from a private cloud environment comprising ten applications. Experimental results demonstrate robust forecasting performance by achieving Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE) below $7\%$ for most applications, with the best-performing application achieving an MAE of $0.7372$, RMSE of $1.1866$, SMAPE of $3.57\%$, and an R2 of $0.9185$. Horizon-wise drift analysis confirmed stable recursive forecasting behavior with controlled error accumulation across a 60-step prediction horizon. Compared with the conventional direct CPU forecasting method, the proposed two-stage integrated model gives improved forecasting robustness, computational efficiency, and interpretability, making it well-suited for proactive resource management and intelligent auto-scaling in cloud computing environments.

</details>


### [223] [Mind the Gap: Robustness Risks in PII Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03464)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出PII（个人身份信息）检测系统在标准基准上表现强劲，但在真实部署中遇到自然分布偏移时会显著退化。作者构建了覆盖七类分布偏移的压力测试基准，评估三种主流架构（SpaCy、Presidio、Qwen2.5-3B），发现它们各有不同且互补的失败模式，并提出一个带QA反馈循环的混合检测管线以缓解风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测系统的评估过于依赖标准基准，掩盖了真实部署中噪声、非结构化、非正式输入下的鲁棒性缺陷。作者旨在揭示不同PII检测范式在分布偏移下各自失效的具体方式，而非仅比较最高精度。

Method: 构建覆盖七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准；选取三种广泛部署的架构家族作为代表进行评估：基于编码器的NER（SpaCy）、基于规则的混合检测（Presidio）和生成式LLM抽取（Qwen2.5-3B）；分析各架构的失败模式；提出QA驱动的反馈循环混合检测管线。

Result: 三种架构在OOD输入上均出现显著性能退化，但失败模式各异：编码器模型主要失败于未见表面形式和边界检测；规则系统失败于非标准格式；LLM表现出实体类型混淆和生成不稳定性。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数会掩盖部署关键期的弱点，没有单一架构在所有PII类别上均匀可靠。需采用OOD感知的评估方式，并建议通过混合检测管线结合QA反馈循环进行迭代式风险缓解。作者公开了该基准以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection is a foundational component of data protection infrastructure where missed entities constitute direct privacy and security risks. Although modern PII systems report strong performance on standard benchmarks, we show that these evaluations mask substantial robustness failures under realistic distribution shifts encountered in deployment. Rather than comparing state-of-the-art accuracy, we study how different PII detection paradigms fail under noisy, unstructured, and informal inputs. We construct a stress test benchmark spanning seven categories of natural distribution shift and evaluate representative systems from three widely deployed architectural families: encoder-based NER (SpaCy), rule-based hybrid detection (Presidio), and generative LLM extraction (Qwen2.5-3B).
  All three exhibit significant degradation on out-of-distribution inputs, but with distinct and complementary failure modes. Encoder models primarily fail on unseen surface forms and boundary detection, rule-based systems fail on non-standard formats, and LLMs exhibit entity-type confusion and generation instability. These results show that aggregate benchmark scores obscure deployment-critical weaknesses and that no single architecture is uniformly reliable across PII categories. Motivated by these findings, we propose a hybrid detection pipeline with a QA-driven feedback loop for iterative risk mitigation, and release our benchmark to support OOD-aware evaluation of PII systems.

</details>


### [224] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自编码器模型参数与训练数据统计特性之间的关系，提出将模型参数视为对应样本的稠密向量表示。通过基于参数矩阵谱特征的向量化方法，理论分析证明参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值相关联，实验在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上验证了该向量表示能高精度区分不同数据子集训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自编码器参数是否可以作为训练样本的向量表示，从而在不使用原始样本或复杂向量生成算法的情况下，实现基于模型参数的样本区分与表征。

Method: 假设自编码器参数可作为样本的稠密向量表示，基于参数矩阵的谱特征（奇异值）构建向量表示；通过理论分析建立参数矩阵奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵特征值之间的数学联系；在CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于谱特征的向量表示能够以高精度区分在不同数据子集上训练的自编码器模型，验证了参数作为样本表示的有效性，且无需使用原始样本或复杂向量生成算法。

Conclusion: 自编码器的训练参数可以被视为其所训练样本的向量表示，数据空间与参数空间之间存在信息传递关系，为基于模型的样本表征提供了新的理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [225] [Restricted Eigenvalues Beyond Gaussian Width: Threshold Occupancy under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03504)
*Shi Fu,Huibo Xu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地推翻了Banerjee等人（COLT 2015）提出的开放问题假设：仅凭均匀小球条件（uniform small-ball condition），重尾设计无法获得与各向同性亚高斯测量相同的限制特征值（RE）界。作者揭示了关键阻碍是同步阈值占用（simultaneous threshold occupancy），并给出了精确的最坏情况样本复杂度下界，以及分布无关的退化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banerjee等人（COLT 2015）提出了一个开放问题：对于重尾设计，是否仅需均匀小球条件就能获得与各向同性亚高斯测量相同的RE界（样本量约为1+w(A)²）。作者希望对该问题进行明确、系统的回答，检验该猜想在一般形式下的正确性。

Method: 作者通过构造反例来论证：(1) 构造具有固定小球常数和常数宽度的多面体下降锥，证明在任何样本路径上，直到环境维数一半时经验RE都为零；(2) 证明每个有限值域空间都能在任意窄的球冠中进行精确阈值编码，并提升为完整的多面体下降锥截线；(3) 推导出固定阈值VC维下尖锐的最坏情况样本复杂度Θ(β^{-1}[d log(1/β)+log(1/δ)])；(4) 通过高斯平滑构造具处处为正的C^∞密度但仍保留任意差RE的重尾分布；(5) 在各向同性条件下，提出由仿射维数乘以包围半径平方支配的分布无关退化方案并证明其在该族上是尖锐的。

Result: (1) 在一般、无维数限制、任意集合的形式下，Banerjee等人的猜想被推翻；(2) 同步阈值占用是缺失的核心阻碍；(3) 在完全各向同性和所有有限矩条件下，分离现象依然存在——高斯测量仅需O(1+log(1/δ))样本即可成功，而各向同性重尾设计在n≲√(p/log p)时逐路径失效；(4) 即使将密度光滑化为C^∞且处处为正，重尾设计的RE仍然可以任意差；(5) 分布无关的项由此问题的本质决定。

Conclusion: 本文对COLT 2015开放问题给出了明确的否定回答：仅凭均匀小球条件不足以支持Banerjee等人提出的通用RE界形式。即使考虑精确各向同性和有限矩条件，重尾与亚高斯设计之间在RE行为上仍存在本质的本质分离。该工作确立了一类新的障害（同步阈值占用），并为重尾高维规范估计下RE分析提供了分布无关的替代框架。

Abstract: Restricted eigenvalue (RE) bounds govern stable recovery by norm-regularized estimators. For isotropic sub-Gaussian measurements, the benchmark sample size is $1+w(A)^2$, where $w(A)$ is the Gaussian width of the normalized descent cone. The COLT 2015 open-problem note (Banerjee et al., 2015) asked whether the same law follows for heavy-tailed designs from a uniform small-ball condition alone. We give an explicit and systematic negative answer to the general question as formulated there: the proposed law fails in its full dimension-free, arbitrary-set form, and the missing obstruction is simultaneous threshold occupancy. A constant-width polyhedral descent cone with fixed small-ball constants has zero empirical RE on every sample path up to half the ambient dimension. More generally, every finite range space admits exact threshold encoding in an arbitrarily narrow spherical cap and a lift to a full polyhedral descent-cone section. For every fixed threshold VC dimension $d$, as $β\downarrow0$, the sharp worst-case sample complexity is $Θ(β^{-1}[d\log(1/β)+\log(1/δ)])$. The separation persists under exact isotropy and all finite moments: on the same constant-width cone, Gaussian measurements succeed with $O(1+\log(1/δ))$ samples, whereas an isotropic heavy-tailed design fails pathwise for $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$. Gaussian smoothing yields an everywhere-positive $C^\infty$ density while retaining arbitrarily poor RE. Under isotropy, a distribution-free fallback governed by affine dimension times squared enclosing radius is sharp on this family.

</details>


### [226] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于序列VAE架构、结合对抗学习与对比损失的多元时间序列异常检测框架，实现无标签、零样本域自适应，并在六个IoT数据集、44个跨域场景下验证了跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络安全异常检测面临三大挑战：设备多样性、缺乏标注数据以及不同环境间的域差异。现有方法难以在无标签且隐私受限的条件下实现跨领域迁移。

Method: 提出基于序列VAE的框架，通过对抗学习与对比损失联合优化域不变潜在表征和语义结构化嵌入空间；引入编码器/解码器自适应层对齐跨域特征分布并保留上下文语义；提出基于目的地的分段策略以建模IoT通信结构。

Result: 在涵盖工业、企业、通用、智能家居和军事自动化等六个不同领域数据集的44个迁移场景中，框架展示了在多种跨域设定下的强零样本泛化能力，并在现实异构、隐私约束的IoT条件下与对比域自适应基线相比表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在不需标签和原始特征迁移的前提下实现有效的零样本跨域IoT异常检测，为隐私受限及数据异构场景提供可行性方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [227] [LongCounsel-8: A Benchmark Suite for Longitudinal Depression Tracking from Multi-Session Counseling Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03507)
*Jiayi Li,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel-8，一个包含7,749条五会话咨询轨迹的基准测试套件（由三个独立生成的数据集构成），用于从多会话咨询对话中追踪抑郁症变化。该基准结合了基于档案的仿真、经验驱动的状态构建和间接行为实现三大策略，解决纵向一致性、经验模式接地和自然表达三方面挑战。实验发现：（1）较低的单会话评分误差并不保证趋势判断准确；（2）现有方法对恶化轨迹的可靠性更差；（3）额外会话历史可能降低趋势预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多会话咨询对话中追踪抑郁症需要同时估计当前症状严重程度及其跨会话变化，但受限于缺乏带标准化会话级抑郁标签的纵向咨询数据。现有资源要么是有多会话对话但无抑郁标签，要么是仅单会话的有标签访谈，无法支撑该任务。

Method: 构建LongCounsel-8基准套件，包含三个独立生成的数据集。采用三种策略：（1）基于档案的仿真（profile-grounded simulation）以真实客户画像为基底，（2）经验驱动的状态构建（empirically informed state construction）确保状态变化符合真实抑郁模式，（3）间接行为实现（indirect behavioral realization）在自然对话中表达受控抑郁状态而不直接暴露标签。

Result: 模拟的自报告能较好地恢复受控状态，支持标签保真度。对现有抑郁追踪方法的实验揭示三点发现：（1）较低的单会话评分误差不保证趋势识别（改善或恶化）的准确性；（2）现有方法在恶化轨迹上普遍更不可靠；（3）增加会话历史可能反而降低趋势预测精度。

Conclusion: LongCounsel-8为抑郁症评估从静态单会话预测迈向可靠的纵向心理健康变化追踪奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Tracking depression from multi-session counseling dialogues requires estimating both current symptom severity and how it changes across sessions. Yet progress on this task is constrained by the scarcity of longitudinal counseling data with standardized session-level depression labels. Existing resources typically provide either multi-session conversations without depression labels or labeled interviews in a single session. Building such a benchmark poses three challenges: maintaining longitudinal consistency and diversity, grounding symptom progression in empirical patterns, and expressing controlled depression states naturally without exposing target labels. To address these challenges, we introduce LongCounsel-8, a benchmark suite of three independently generated datasets totaling 7,749 five-session counseling trajectories, grounded in real-world client profiles, depression trajectories, symptom compositions, and counseling patterns. We combine profile-grounded simulation, empirically informed state construction, and indirect behavioral realization to address these challenges. Across the benchmark, simulated self-reports closely recover the controlled states, supporting label fidelity. Experiments on existing depression tracking methods reveal three key findings: (1) lower single-session score error does not guarantee accurate identification of trend, i.e., improvement or worsening; (2) existing methods are consistently less reliable on worsening trajectories; and (3) additional session history may reduce the accuracy of trend prediction. Together, these findings establish LongCounsel-8 as a foundation for advancing depression assessment from static, single-session prediction toward reliable longitudinal tracking of mental-health change.

</details>


### [228] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeanGRPO，通过重构数据并行布局并引入两种免重算（recompute-free）的训练调度来消除扩散强化学习中rollout与更新阶段冗余的前向计算，在FLUX.1-dev和Wan上实现最高1.83x端到端加速且保持原优化目标不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散RL方法（如DanceGRPO、FlowGRPO）在rollout后需要对所选时间步用梯度跟踪重算前向传播，产生大量冗余计算。虽然直觉上rollout与策略更新可复用同一前馈骨干网络，但直接复用会引入rollout阶段巨大的显存开销，需要更优的调度策略来消除冗余并控制内存消耗。

Method: LeanGRPO通过重构数据并行布局，提出两种免重算训练调度：(1) LeanGRPO-Retain在rollout期间开启梯度跟踪，直接复用计算图与已保存激活用于反传，不进行任何重算；(2) LeanGRPO-Reweight在rollout期间开启梯度，对每个选中的时间步用临时优势立即反传并延迟梯度同步，在轨迹完成后用真实优势修正临时梯度。两种调度分别针对不同模型规模与输入尺寸。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO框架下基于FLUX.1-dev和Wan模型进行验证，LeanGRPO实现最高1.83x的端到端加速，同时保持原有的优化目标与训练效果不变。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO通过避免rollout后的冗余前向重算并重构计算调度，在不改变优化目标的前提下显著加速了扩散强化学习的后训练过程，为大规模图像/视频生成模型的RL训练提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [229] [Coupled Scaling: A Representational Accessibility Framework for Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03533)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Coupled Scaling"（耦合缩放）框架，用以解释为什么在相同数据上训练的不同架构-优化系统会呈现不同的神经缩放指数。该框架将有限预算下的缩放行为刻画为任务结构与系统可及几何（architecture-optimization可达的表示几何）之间的关系，并从模截断模型出发推导出残差指数落在由覆盖对数速率和目标尾部对数速率决定的区间内；在固定核特例下，可从独立于损失拟合的任务加权谱测度的近零尾部推出训练时间指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论从数据几何或预设的数据-模型谱出发推导神经缩放规律，但它们无法解释一个重要现象：即使训练数据完全相同，当架构或优化方式改变系统能有效到达的表示几何时，缩放规律也会随之改变。因此需要一个将任务结构与架构-优化系统可达几何耦合起来的框架。

Method: 提出任务条件化（task-conditioned）的耦合缩放框架。在可解的模截断（mode-truncation）模型中，损失分解为架构支撑之外的目标能量与未解决的受支撑尾部。针对任意优先顺序，残差介于"最佳N受支撑尾部"与"最大已完成高价值前缀之外的尾部"之间。定义累积尾部和覆盖对数速率分别为γ和ρ，则残差指数落在[ρ·γ, γ]区间内；在受界off-prefix增益条件下，完成前缀起速率决定作用，α=ρ·γ；对幂律权重则得α=ρ(b−1)。固定核特例可由独立于损失拟合的任务加权谱测度近零尾部推导训练时间指数。

Result: 框架将架构支撑（architectural support）与有限预算获取（finite-budget acquisition）分离，推导得出可检验的两个预测：一是在公共预算下，静态任务相关几何应追踪损失；二是多尺度几何应追踪耦合特定的指数排序，包括在对比任务间可能出现指数反转。此外，对已发布的涌现轨迹进行审计，识别出进行直接阶乘检验所需的关键控制条件，以便将几何度量与缩放拟合分离。

Conclusion: 耦合缩放框架表明，缩放规律本质上是任务结构、架构支撑与优化获取三者耦合的产物，单凭数据几何不足以预测神经缩放。通过将指数推导锁定在独立于损失拟合的任务加权谱测度上，并为实验设计提供可检验的标准与必要控制，该工作为更严谨地分离几何与缩放效应的实证研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Existing theories derive neural scaling from data geometry or a specified data-model spectrum, but systems trained on the same data can scale differently when architecture or optimization changes the representations they can efficiently reach. We introduce Coupled Scaling, a task-conditioned framework in which finite-budget scaling depends on the relation between task structure and the geometry accessible to an architecture-optimization system. In a solvable mode-truncation model, loss separates into target energy outside architectural support and an unresolved supported tail. For an arbitrary priority order, the residual lies between the best-N supported tail and the tail beyond the largest completed high-value prefix. If the cumulative-tail and coverage log-rates are $γ_{A,T}$ and $ρ_{A,O,T}$, the residual exponent lies in $[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T},γ_{A,T}]$. Under bounded off-prefix gain, the completed prefix is rate-determining and $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}$; for $a_{A,T,j}\asymp j^{-b_{A,T}}$, this gives $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)$. A fixed-kernel specialization derives the training-time exponent from the near-zero tail of a task-weighted spectral measure defined independently of the loss fit. The framework separates architectural support from finite-budget acquisition and motivates two tests: static task-relevant geometry should track loss at a common budget, while multiscale geometry should track coupling-specific exponent ordering, including reversal across contrasting tasks. An audit of released emergence trajectories identifies the controls needed for a direct factorial test that measures geometry separately from the scaling fit.

</details>


### [230] [WeatherNext 3: Increasing resolution and performance of global weather models with raw observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03582)
*Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Dominic Masters,Andrew El-Kadi,Samier Merchant,Guy Shalev,Ilan Price,Fred Zyda,Remi Lam,Sasha Shysheya,Matthew Willson,Stratis Markou,Shreya Agrawal,Suhani Vora,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Sunny Mak,Tom R. Andersson,Megan Bela,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Ferran Alet,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherNext 3 是一种新一代AI天气预测模型，通过每小时生成预报、整合低延迟卫星数据、达到与物理模型相当的时空分辨率（每小时时间步长、单层变量0.1度空间分辨率），并学习预测卫星降水估计、热带气旋和站点观测，解决了现有AI天气模型时空分辨率低及仅依赖分析数据的两大缺陷，建立了概率中期预报的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的AI天气模型虽然在中期预报技能和计算效率上表现优异，但存在两个关键不足：1) 预报的时空分辨率低于最好的基于物理的模型；2) 仅使用分析数据初始化和训练，无法直接利用观测数据，且继承了分析数据的偏差。因此需要开发一种能够直接使用观测数据并达到与物理模型相当分辨率的AI天气模型。

Method: WeatherNext 3 采取三项核心方法：(1) 通过摄入低延迟地球同步卫星数据，每小时生成一次预报（传统全球模型为每6小时）；(2) 将时间步长提升至小时级，并将单层变量的空间分辨率提升至0.1度，与基于物理的全球模型相当，涵盖太阳辐射和云量等变量；(3) 超越传统分析变量，学习预测卫星衍生的降水估计、热带气旋和站点观测，利用稀疏站点数据建模2m温度和露点预测，并可结合局部地理特征在任何位置和时间进行预测。

Result: WeatherNext 3 建立了概率中期预报技能的新SOTA。它在2m温度和露点预测方面大幅优于竞争全球模型，即使在针对未见站点的评估中也是如此。其预报分辨率与传统物理全球模型相当，且预报频率远高于此前所有模型。

Conclusion: WeatherNext 3 将运行化的AI天气预测从对数据同化、预报和后处理这传统分离阶段的模拟中解放出来，直接整合观测数据并输出终端预报产品，进一步推动了全球天气预报在性能和精细化程度上的边界。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI weather models have shown impressive medium-range forecast skill and computational efficiency, but suffer two key shortcomings: their forecasts have lower spatial and temporal resolution than the best physics-based models and they are exclusively initialized with and trained on analysis data. As a result, they cannot directly make use of observations, and any biases in the analysis are inherited by the forecast. WeatherNext 3 addresses these shortcomings and establishes a new state-of-the-art for probabilistic medium-range forecasting skill. First, WeatherNext 3 generates new forecasts every hour (rather than every 6 hours like traditional global models) by ingesting low-latency geostationary satellite data. Second, WeatherNext 3's temporal and spatial resolution are on par with physics-based global models, with hourly time steps and 0.1 degree resolution for single-level variables, including solar radiation and cloud cover. Third, WeatherNext 3 moves beyond traditional analysis variables by learning to predict satellite-derived precipitation estimates, as well as tropical cyclone and station observations. Modelling sparse station data allows WeatherNext 3 to make 2m temperature and dewpoint predictions at any location and time, conditioned on local geographical features, with substantially lower error than competing global models, even when evaluated against unseen stations. Together, WeatherNext 3's capabilities move operational AI-based weather forecasting beyond emulating the traditionally distinct stages of data assimilation, forecasting and post-processing, which helps to further push the frontier of performance and granularity for global weather prediction.

</details>


### [231] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN Maxent，一种将Maxent框架中的固定特征字典替换为门控循环单元（GRU）神经网络的方法，用于物种分布建模。该方法应用于沙漠蝗虫（Schistocerca gregaria）的栖息地适宜性制图，利用50天的环境时序数据（ERA5 Land、MODIS和Sentinel 3）进行预测。相比标准Maxent，RNN Maxent在ROC AUC（0.862 vs 0.792）和F1分数（0.671 vs 0.590）上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Maxent依赖手工选择特征变换的线性组合，无法捕捉生态监测中常见的非线性时序关系。传统实现将时间序列协变量展开为独立特征，丢失了蕴含关键信号的序列结构，限制了其对沙漠蝗虫等依赖快速演变环境条件的害虫的建模能力。

Method: RNN Maxent是Maxent框架的扩展，将固定的特征字典替换为神经网络（具体为GRU），通过反向传播进行端到端训练。该方法保留了Maxent的presence-only统计基础、背景归一化和概率校准，唯一区别在于非线性由数据学习而非预先固定。应用中使用50天环境时序数据并保持7天间隔以实现预测行为。

Result: 在与标准Maxent的比较中，RNN Maxent在所有指标上表现更优：ROC AUC从0.792提升至0.862（标准差0.036），F1分数从0.590提升至0.671（标准差0.056）。

Conclusion: 结论隐含RNN Maxent通过利用时序结构，能够有效提升对沙漠蝗虫等依赖动态环境条件的物种的栖息地适宜性预测精度，验证了用数据驱动的神经网络非线性替代固定特征变换在SDM中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [232] [On the Interaction Between Model Compression and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03604)
*Francesco Corti,Dong Wang,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了结构化压缩如何影响模型在测试时自适应（TTA）能力。在ResNet-18和ViT-Base上使用CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C评估多种压缩方法与标准TTA技术的组合，引入诊断框架检验表征表达力与自适应子空间兼容性。结果显示压缩模型在监督自适应下保持高精度，但TTA性能随压缩程度加深而显著退化，根源在于表征多样性降低和限制可恢复性的结构约束，且这些影响强烈依赖于压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在实际部署中既要高效又要具备适应性，这需要模型压缩和测试时自适应（TTA）两种技术。虽然两者在孤立场景下已被充分研究，但它们之间的交互作用仍未被充分理解。

Method: 在ResNet-18和ViT-Base架构上，结合CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C基准，将多种压缩方法与标准TTA技术组合进行系统评估；提出一个诊断框架，分别考察表征表达力（representational expressivity）与自适应子空间兼容性（adaptation subspace compatibility）。

Result: 发现一致性的性能差距：压缩模型在监督自适应下仍保持较高精度，但TTA性能随压缩程度增加而显著退化。该退化源于表征多样性的降低以及限制可恢复性的结构约束，且影响效果强烈依赖具体的压缩方法。

Conclusion: 压缩方法必须考虑保留模型的适应性，而非仅追求精度与效率的平衡，这为面向实际部署的压缩策略设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed in the wild must be both efficient and adaptable, requiring model compression and test-time adaptation (TTA). While both are well studied in isolation, their interaction remains poorly understood. We systematically analyze how structured compression affects a model's ability to adapt under distribution shift. Using ResNet-18 and ViT-Base on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C, we evaluate multiple compression methods combined with standard TTA techniques. We introduce a diagnostic framework that examines representational expressivity and adaptation subspace compatibility. Our results reveal a consistent gap: although compressed models retain high accuracy under supervised adaptation, their TTA performance degrades significantly with increasing compression. We show that this stems from reduced representational diversity and structural constraints that limit recoverability. These effects strongly depend on the compression method, highlighting the need to design compression strategies that preserve adaptability.

</details>


### [233] [Local Updates, Global Learning (LUGL): Playing Games with non-incremental Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03660)
*David Milec,Spyridon Samothrakis,Michael Fairbank,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUGL框架，将数据收集与模型拟合解耦，使梯度提升树（如LightGBM）这类非增量学习器能够在强化学习/自博弈场景中应用。通过在九种完美/不完美信息游戏中测试，LightGBM智能体的表现与DQN和DeepCFR持平或更优，挑战了神经网络在游戏智能体中的主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在RL中的优势部分源于其增量学习能力，适合自博弈训练在线、非平稳的特性。然而游戏状态本质上具有表格化特征（离散动作、类别化牌面、结构化棋局），这类数据上梯度提升树（如LightGBM）在监督学习中已被证明优于神经网络。作者旨在探索树模型在RL场景下的潜力，挑战NN在游戏智能体领域的固有偏见。

Method: 提出LUGL（Local Updates, Global Learning）框架：通过交替执行两个阶段，使非增量学习器（GBTs）得以在RL中运作。局部更新阶段——智能体进行自博弈游戏，将Q值/V值/策略/遗憾值等表格化更新累积到有限大小的表中；全局学习阶段——利用该表训练函数逼近器以泛化到未见状态，随后重置表。该方法避免了直接在线更新GBTs所面临的分布漂移问题。

Result: 在四种完美信息游戏（井字棋、四子棋、奥赛罗、六边形棋）和五种不完美信息游戏（Kuhn扑克、Leduc德州、掷骰骗子、Goofspiel、Flop5德州）中测试，LightGBM智能体在所有基准上均达到与DQN和DeepCFR竞争或更优的表现，证明了社区对NN在游戏中的强烈偏好可能是不必要的。

Conclusion: 实验证实，通过恰当的框架设计（如LUGL的局部-全局交替学习），基于LightGBM的树模型智能体能够在多种完美及不完美信息游戏中取得与NN基准相当甚至更好的性能，说明游戏博弈领域对神经网络的普遍偏好可能缺乏充分依据。

Abstract: The dominance of Neural Networks (NNs) in RL is partially due to their incremental learning capability, which naturally suits the online, non-stationary nature of self-play training. However, gradient-boosted trees like LightGBM are widely recognised as the state of the art for tabular data in supervised learning, often outperforming NNs in accuracy and efficiency. Game states are inherently tabular---discrete actions, categorical card identities, structured board positions---which makes them an ideal candidate for tree-based methods. We introduce LUGL (Local Updates, Global Learning), a framework that decouples data collection from model fitting, enabling non-incremental learners such as GBTs to operate in RL settings where they would otherwise fail due to distributional shift. LUGL alternates between a local updates phase, where the agent plays self-play games and accumulates tabular updates (Q-values, V-values, policies, or regret values) in a finite table, and a global learning phase, where the table is used to train a function approximator that generalises to unseen states before the table is reset. We test our approach in four standard perfect-information games (Tic-tac-toe, Connect-4, Othello, and Hex) and five imperfect-information games (Kuhn's poker, Leduc Hold'em, Liar's Dice, Goofspiel, and Flop5 Hold'em), and show that our results are competitive with or superior to DQN and DeepCFR. Our experiments demonstrate that the community's strong bias towards NNs in game-playing may be unwarranted, since LightGBM-based agents achieve competitive or superior performance across all tested benchmarks.

</details>


### [234] [Extracting Forgotten Prompts from Targeted Unlearned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03662)
*Au Ashley Hoi-Ting,Meghdad Kurmanji,William F. Shen,Nicholas D. Lane,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种新的机器遗忘漏洞：现有攻击通常假设遗忘提示词已知，而本文提出的TAS（Targeted Active Search）攻击可以在仅有黑盒访问和保留数据的情况下，先识别被遗忘的实体，再重构被遗忘的提示词，在三个数据集上实现100%的实体恢复准确率和最高95%的提示词重构率，同时比朴素探测节省高达99.7%的查询次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法（如NPO、DPO、LUNAR）通过拒绝对齐来抑制遗忘数据，但研究表明这可能会留下遗忘痕迹，且现有攻击能恢复部分被遗忘知识。然而这些攻击通常假设遗忘提示已知，本文旨在揭示一个更严重的新漏洞——遗忘提示词本身可以被从黑盒模型中提取出来。

Method: 提出TAS（Targeted Active Search）攻击方法，分两步：(1) 通过构建规范模板和实体池，在有限查询预算下选择最具信息量的模板-实体对来主动查询模型，识别被遗忘的实体；(2) 用识别出的实体实例化提示模板来探测未学习模型，重构被遗忘的提示词。该方法利用了保留数据和对模型的黑盒访问权限。

Result: 在三种未学习方法、三个数据集和三个LLM上的实验表明：TAS能够以100%的准确率恢复被遗忘实体，重构高达95%的被遗忘提示，同时比朴素探测方法减少高达99.7%的查询次数。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一个比现有攻击更严重的隐私漏洞——不仅被遗忘知识的答案可以被恢复，被遗忘提示词本身也能被主动提取，且查询效率极高，这对机器遗忘的安全性和隐私保护提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Recent unlearning methods (e.g. NPO, DPO, LUNAR) make use of refusal alignment to suppress forgotten data. However, it has been shown that refusal responses might leave traces of unlearning, and recent attacks have been able to successfully recover some of the unlearned knowledge. In this paper, we uncover a new vulnerability. Existing attacks typically assume that the forgotten prompts are already known to the adversary and focus on recovering their answers. However, we show that the forgotten prompts themselves can be extracted by using the retained data and black-box access to the model. Our attack, Targeted Active Search (TAS), first identifies the forgotten entities by constructing canonical templates and entity pool, and selectively querying the model using the most informative template-entity pair under a limited query budget. Once the entities are identified, TAS instantiates prompt templates with those entities to probe the unlearned model and reconstruct the forgotten prompts. Experiments across three unlearning methods with three datasets and three LLMs shows that TAS recovers the forgotten entity with $100\%$ accuracy and reconstructs up to $95\%$ of forgotten prompts, all while using up to $99.7\%$ fewer queries than naive probing.

</details>


### [235] [Out-of-Distribution Generalisation with Sequence Models in Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03667)
*Oussama Hidaoui,Omer Ebead,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Siddarth Singh,Juan Claude Formanek,Felix Chalumeau,Omayma Mahjoub,Sasha Abramowitz,Ruan John de Kock,Wiem Khlifi,Louay Ben Nessir,Simon Verster Du Toit,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Asim Awad Osman,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Refiloe Shabe,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中的零样本任务泛化进行了原理性分析和大规模实证研究，发现任务多样性（而非数据集规模）是决定零样本迁移性能的主导因素，并提出了一个扩展序列建模以处理多任务异构观测/动作空间与可变智能体数量的框架，在四个挑战性环境中相比单任务模型实现了平均3.2倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL中泛化到未见任务仍是一个根本性挑战，此前缺乏对任务多样性、数据集规模与网络容量之间缩放关系的系统性理解和严格分析。

Method: 将离线序列建模架构扩展至多任务设定，支持异构观测和动作空间以及任务间不同智能体数量的处理，并在四种环境（Connector, RWARE, SMAX, LBF）上进行大规模缩放实验，系统性地考察任务多样性、数据集大小和网络容量对零样本泛化的影响。

Result: 任务多样性是零样本稳健迁移的主导因素，而非单纯的数据集规模。多任务方法在未见测试任务上相比单任务模型平均提升3.2倍，并持续优于强行为克隆基线。

Conclusion: 开发可泛化的MARL智能体应优先考虑训练分布中带变数量智能体的多样性，为有效扩展离线MARL提供了路线图。

Abstract: Generalising to unseen tasks remains a fundamental challenge in offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). In this work, we present a principled analysis of zero-shot task generalisation in the offline setting and conduct an extensive empirical investigation into the scaling behaviour governing task diversity, dataset size, and network capacity. To facilitate this study, we extend offline sequence modelling architectures to handle multi-task observation and action spaces alongside variable agent counts across tasks. Our primary finding is that scaling task diversity---rather than sheer dataset size is the dominant factor in achieving robust zero-shot transfer. Through large-scale experiments across four challenging environments (Connector, RWARE, SMAX, and LBF), we demonstrate that our multi-task approach achieves a mean improvement of 3.2x on held-out test tasks compared to single-task models and consistently outperforms strong behaviour cloning baselines. These results suggest that the development of generalisable MARL agents should prioritise the diversity of the training distribution with varying numbers of agents, providing a roadmap for scaling offline MARL effectively.

</details>


### [236] [Resolution-Aware Experimental Design under Partial Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03686)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"分辨率感知实验设计"(RAED)，针对部分可识别性下实验选择的信息标准缺陷，通过最小化期望非空结构候选集并控制误排除率来选择实验，并给出了可操作的学习式实现与多重基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验设计以最大化期望信息量为目标。但在部分可识别(partial identifiability)情形下，持续的干扰项不确定性(nuisance uncertainty)会使相同的观测承载不同的结构含义，导致基于信息增益、平均分类或干扰边缘化信息量的实验选择标准可能与真正的结构分辨率严重背离。

Method: 引入RAED准则，即选择能使期望非空结构候选集最小、同时满足误排除(false-exclusion)控制的实验。理论上有精确的"交叉干扰混叠分离"证明：结构信息增益、全潜信息增益、平均分类与干扰边缘化信息量与有效结构分辨率可任意分离。开发了基于学习的分数实现，具备有限样本的干扰平均与正尾校准，并刻画了罕见的尾样本复杂度障碍。

Result: 在约束传感下，两个地下水流基准(WCA尤为突出)出现RAED与期望信息增益(EIG)选择的真实分歧；河流基准中尾保护改变了所选物理实验，将硬区误排除主要转化为显式模糊；甲烷氧化基准中，预设5%误排除容差对尾敏感干扰风险给出非平凡有限样本总体保证，三个结构族上具备95%联合置信。

Conclusion: RAED为部分可识别情形的实验设计提供了以结构有效分辨率与误排除控制为核心的新准则，理论上与经典EIG等标准分离，实操上可行，并在多个实测基准中展现可复现的优势与保证。

Abstract: Experimental design is commonly framed as choosing the experiment expected to provide the most information. Under partial identifiability however, persistent nuisance uncertainty can make the same observation carry different structural meanings. We introduce Resolution-Aware Experimental Design (RAED), which selects an experiment by the smallest expected nonempty structural candidate set achievable subject to false-exclusion control. We prove an exact cross-nuisance aliasing separation: an experiment can be preferred by structural and full-latent information gain, average classification, and nuisance-marginalized informativeness while having arbitrarily poorer valid structural resolution. RAED nevertheless preserves the expected ordering under a genuine composite Blackwell comparison. To make this criterion operational, we develop a learned score-based implementation with finite-sample nuisance-average and positive-tail calibration, and characterize a rare-tail sample-complexity obstruction. Under constrained sensing, two subsurface-flow benchmarks exhibit genuine RAED--expected-information-gain (EIG) experiment-selection disagreements, with the clearest and largest held-out resolution differences in WCA. In a fluvial benchmark, tail protection changes the selected physical experiment and replaces hard-region false exclusions primarily with explicit ambiguity. In a mechanistic methane-oxidation benchmark, a prospectively specified 5\% false-exclusion tolerance also yields a nontrivial finite-sample population guarantee for tail-sensitive nuisance risk, with 95\% joint confidence across all three structural families.

</details>


### [237] [Federated Causal Discovery via Regression-Directed Cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03705)
*Pablo Torrijos,Fabio Stella,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦环境下的线性非高斯无环模型（LiNGAM）。由于中央化数据受GDPR等法规限制，联邦学习成为兼顾隐私与因果发现精度的方案。标准中心化估计器DirectLiNGAM无法直接联邦化，而高阶累积量张量只需一轮通信即可在水平、垂直和混合分区下工作。但现有联邦方法FedISHC在近对称噪声下失效，为此作者提出FedRCD系列因果发现算法，并研究了三种变体以在通信轮数与代数噪声间权衡，其中两种是中心化HC和HC-LiNGAM算法的精确联邦对应物，单轮变体还支持任意粒度的精确遗忘。数值实验显示，在实际样本量下，整个基于累积量的联邦家族并非按群体不对称性排序变量，而是按DAG有向路径诱导的方差阶梯排序（即cumulant版本的varsortability）。边际标准化会使所有累积量方法退化为近似随机排序，而尺度不变的DirectLiNGAM不受影响但无法在该协议下联邦化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LiNGAM因果模型在联邦环境下的应用困境：数据分散且不宜中心化（GDPR限制），标准中心化估计器DirectLiNGAM不能直接联邦化，而现有联邦方法FedISHC在近对称噪声条件下失效，需要一种鲁棒、通信高效的联邦因果发现方法。

Method: 提出FedRCD家族因果发现算法，基于高阶累积量张量（具有可加性与仅依赖联合分布的特性）实现单轮通信联邦化；研究三种变体在通信轮数与代数噪声间权衡，其中两种为中心化HC和HC-LiNGAM的精确联邦对应物，单轮变体支持任意粒度的精确遗忘（从单个观测到整个客户端）。

Result: 数值实验表明，在现实部署的典型样本量下，基于累积量的联邦家族并非按总体不对称性排序变量，而是依据DAG沿有向路径诱导的方差阶梯排序——这是累积量版本的varsortability现象。边际标准化会使所有累积量方法退化为近似随机排序，而尺度不变的DirectLiNGAM不受此影响但无法在本协议下联邦化。

Conclusion: FedRCD家族克服了FedISHC在近对称噪声下失效的局限，提供了通信效率与代数稳健性之间的灵活权衡，并支持精确遗忘能力。然而，实验揭示了累积量方法在联邦环境中存在方差阶梯排序行为（cumulant varsortability），这一发现对基于累积量的联邦因果发现方法的实际有效性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: In this paper we study linear non-Gaussian acyclic models (LiNGAM) when used in federated environments. These causal models allow one to go beyond Markov equivalence. However, in many domains data are scarce, and increasing the sample size by centralising data from different clients is not advisable due to regulations such as the GDPR. The federated environment offers an attractive option to balance privacy and causal discovery accuracy. Unfortunately, the standard centralised estimator in the LiNGAM setting, i.e., DirectLiNGAM, cannot be straightforwardly federated. Higher-order cumulant tensors offer a way around this obstacle: they depend only on the joint distribution of the variables involved and add exactly across independent sample groups, so a single communication round suffices in horizontal, vertical, and hybrid partitions.
  However, FedISHC, i.e., the current federated method along these lines, breaks down under near-symmetric noise. To overcome the above limitation, we introduce the FedRCD family of causal discovery algorithms, and investigate three variants that trade off communication rounds against algebraic noise; two of them are exact federated counterparts of the centralised high-order cumulant (HC) and HC-LiNGAM algorithms, and the single-round variants further effectively support exact unlearning at any granularity, from a single observation to a whole client. Numerical experiments show that at sample sizes typical of real deployments, the entire cumulant-based federated family does not actually rank variables by the population asymmetry that the scores encode at zero. It ranks them by a variance ladder induced by the DAG along its directed paths, the cumulant counterpart of varsortability. Marginal standardisation collapses every cumulant method to near-random ordering, while scale-invariant DirectLiNGAM, not federable under this protocol, is unaffected.

</details>


### [238] [Projected Riemannian Gradient Descent for the Bures-Wasserstein Barycenter: Dimension-Independent Linear Convergence at Unit Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03762)
*A. Afham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Bures-Wasserstein (BW)重心计算中Riemannian梯度下降(RGD)算法的理论-实践二分问题：单位步长RGD收敛快但最坏情况保证呈维度指数依赖，而维度无关的保证需要小步长。作者提出Projected RGD算法，在单位步长下实现了维度无关的线性收敛，速率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，其中κ为系统的条件数，相比最好小步长保证在多项式上改进（迭代复杂度从κ^{5/2}降到κ^{3/2}）。核心是一个新颖的投影引理：将正定矩阵的特征值裁剪到区间[α,β]是对应集合在BW度量下的闭式非扩张(1-Lipschitz)投影。该投影是"免费"的，复用下一次迭代必需的矩阵分解，因此投影与非投影迭代每步成本相同。该分析还涵盖了Brahmachari等人(2025)的不变矩阵投影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bures-Wasserstein重心计算在机器学习、最优传输和量子信息中广泛出现。实践中的RGD单位步长迭代收敛速度快，但现有理论分析存在二分困境：单位步长的保证在维度上有最坏情况的指数依赖，而维度无关的保证需要小步长从而丧失实际速度。作者希望同时获得单位步长的高效率和维度无关的理论保证。

Method: 提出Projected RGD算法，在每次迭代中对矩阵特征值进行裁剪投影。关键贡献是新Projection Lemma：将正矩阵特征值裁剪到区间[α,β]是BW度量下到集合{S: αI ≤ S ≤ βI}的闭式非扩张投影。投影的1-Lipschitz性质不依赖于凸性（与实际已知的单侧投影不同）。由于投影复用了下一次迭代必然要执行的矩阵分解，因此不增加额外计算成本。此外，通过辨识Brahmachari等人(2025)的定点算法为全测地子流形上的单位步长RGD，将相同分析直接扩展到该设置。

Result: Projected RGD在单位步长下实现维度无关的线性收敛，收敛率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，迭代复杂度为κ^{3/2}，相比小步长最优保证的κ^{5/2}有多项式改进。投影是免费的（不增加每步成本），并且相同保证verbatim适用于不变矩阵投影问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入投影步骤而非改进单位步长RGD的现有保证，成功消除了理论保证与实际性能之间的二分。关键洞察是特征值裁剪在BW度量下是非扩张投影，这一结构性质使得可以在单位步长下获得维度无关的收敛保证。该结果同时推广到相关的不变矩阵投影设置，展示了方法的普适性。

Abstract: The computation of the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) barycenter of an ensemble of positive definite matrices arises throughout machine learning, optimal transport, and quantum information. Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) at unit step size -- the fixed-point iteration used in practice -- converges rapidly, yet existing analyses present a dichotomy: unit-step guarantees carry worst-case exponential dependence on the dimension, while dimension-independent guarantees require small step sizes that forfeit the empirical speed. We resolve this dichotomy, not by improving the guarantees for unit-step RGD, but by proposing a Projected RGD algorithm that achieves dimension-independent linear convergence at unit step size. The achieved rate, $(1 - κ^{-3/2})$, where $κ$ is the condition number of the ensemble, also polynomially improves on the best small-step guarantee ($κ^{3/2}$ versus $κ^{5/2}$ iteration complexity). The crux is a novel Projection Lemma: clipping the eigenvalues of a positive matrix to an interval $[α, β]$ is the closed-form, non-expansive (1-Lipschitz) BW-metric projection onto the set $\{S : αI \leq S \leq βI\}$ -- a statement which, unlike its known one-sided counterpart, does not follow from convexity. The projection is moreover free: it reuses an eigendecomposition the next iteration must perform in any case, so the projected and unprojected iterations cost the same per step. The same analysis covers the invariant matrix projection problem of Brahmachari et al. (2025), whose fixed-point algorithm we identify as unit-step RGD on a totally geodesic submanifold, thereby extending the dimension-independent guarantee to that setting verbatim.

</details>


### [239] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCast v2，一种基于观测初始条件的12小时云量预报机器学习模型。该模型先在哥白尼欧洲区域再分析数据上学习云演化动力学，再通过条件流匹配生成方法适配到卫星观测的云场。相比前代CloudCast v1，该模型在1-12小时范围内平均绝对误差降低10%，并在约3-6小时后（依云量类别而定）在分数技巧评分上超越前代，证明观测初始化的机器学习预报可将临近预报范围扩展到常规的1-3小时之外，同时保留卫星云场的空间细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时临近预报方法在最初几小时能保持观测到的云位置，但云场经历生成、消散和形变时其技巧下降；更长的提前时间需要考虑大气演化，但业务数值天气预报（NWP）在初始化时可能无法准确表示卫星观测的云状态。因此需要在观测初始条件下实现长时效且保持空间细节的云量预报。

Method: CloudCast v2采用两阶段训练：首先在哥白尼欧洲区域再分析数据上训练以学习云演化动力学，然后利用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）这一生成式方法将模型适配到卫星观测的云场。该方法将噪声转化为以观测初始云场和NWP输入为条件的云量预报。

Result: CloudCast v2在1-12小时范围内相比CloudCast v1平均绝对误差降低10%；在分数技巧评分（一种基于邻域的空间一致性度量）上，依云量类别不同，约在3-6小时后超越CloudCast v1。

Conclusion: 观测初始化的机器学习云量预报能够超越通常1-3小时的临近预报范围，同时保留卫星云场的空间细节，为更长时间尺度的云量预报提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [240] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OBER+，一个扩展的机构成果评估平台，用于计算从测量到的成果缺口到评估后的纠正行动之间的步骤。通过五阶段流程累积成果达成度、标记缺口和持续缺口、依据监管标准评级、记录决策、日志变更并量化缺口变化，同时通过规则比较成果陈述的变化以避免误读。在真实课程记录上应用发现：核心课程的所有成果在连续交付间被实质性重新定义；六成平台数据与文档规则计算不符并识别出缺陷；成果编号在重定义后对应不匹配。贡献是可供任何平台实施的计算规则及在实时机构记录中的可见证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以成果为基础的教育（OBE）机构例行计算学习成果达成度，但课程分析综述显示缺乏关于该计算如何指导决策的证据。论文旨在弥合从测量到的短感到评估后的纠正行动之间的鸿沟。

Method: 五个连接的阶段：跨课程交付累积达成度、发出短感和持续短感信号、依据监管机构已有阈值评级、将决策记录到附带证据的实践目录、记录变更并量化短感的后续变化。另有规则比较成果连续陈述以避免将成果变化误读为达成度序列。应用于两门真实课程的实时记录。

Result: 三个结果：（1）核心课程每个成果在连续交付间被实质性重新定义，主题内容在成果编号间移动，若直接读数会误报25点的下跌；（2）用文档规则重算平台数据发现十项中六项差异超出舍入误差，识别出已上报机构的缺陷；（3）15对陈述中有5对逐字符相同，余下10对中，同一编号的成果在6例中最接近逻辑不同的早期成果。排序结果无需阈值或标签。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为成果导向的报告提供计算设计，以任何平台可实施的规则形式呈现，并证据化这些规则在实时机构记录中揭示的内容。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results. Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [241] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定地标、每运动员-会话单表示的建模框架，解决运动员监测数据（分钟级）与损伤标签（会话级）粒度不匹配的问题。在2020 SoccerMon数据（48名精英女足、3,743个运动员-会话、22个损伤相关会话）上评估多种表征，ROC-AUC为0.367-0.607，PR-AUC为0.0080-0.0150，结果不确定性较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将每个分钟的数据赋予相同的会话级损伤标签，隐含假设损伤发生在任何时刻都是已知的，但实际会话内损伤发生时间未知，这种分钟级监督缺乏依据。需要一种与数据粒度匹配、同时保留会话级目标的建模方案。

Method: 提出固定地标（如10、20、30分钟）的每运动员-会话单表示框架：在每个地标时间点，仅利用此前观察到的信息构建一个会话级表示，目标是预测整个会话是否损伤相关。评估会话前、累积、动态及组合四种表征，采用运动员分离验证、运动员簇自助法、共同队列敏感性分析等方法，并与Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost对比。

Result: 在2020 SoccerMon数据上，CUM+DYN逻辑回归在所有地标上的ROC-AUC为0.367-0.607，PR-AUC为0.0080-0.0150，置信区间宽。含会话前（PRE）信息的表示在多个地标有较高的点估计，但统计上仍不确定。

Conclusion: 该固定地标框架比逐分钟标记方法更符合实际数据生成过程，能合理评估随会话内信息积累的判别能力变化。但精英女足球运动员损伤会话样本极少且高度异质，当前结果的区分性能有限且不确定性大，需要更大样本和进一步方法改进以得出可靠结论。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [242] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有序Bernoulli词核f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k)及其逆整数水平集生成的几何结构。二元水平2^(-n)选取p=1/2作为唯一的实分裂独立锚点，在互补保持复数延拓下形成共轭对称的垂直几何。二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2具有中心点尖锐极小值并允许精确整数量化。对于临界线零点纵坐标gamma_k，诱导水平L_k被精确分解为最近整数N_k与周期性第一Bernoulli余项delta_k之和，圆化后隔离出gamma_k^2 mod 1作为残余相位变量。唯一分解将整数壳分解为素数生成元坐标，复指数s将同一构造提升到Dirichlet原子m^(-s)上。精确恒等式、经典zeta联系、数值检验及开放条件Weyl检验保持明确分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图从组合与几何的视角重新审视zeta函数临界线零点结构，通过Bernoulli词核的逆整数水平集构造一种先于zeta函数输入的几何框架，为理解gamma_k^2的模1分布提供独立的组合学基础。

Method: 利用Bernoulli词核与互补保持复数延拓构造共轭对称几何，定义二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)并引入精确整数量化分解L_k=N_k+delta_k；通过圆化映射Z_k=exp(2πi delta_k)提取残余相位变量，借助唯一分解将整数壳映射到素数生成元坐标，并用复指数s将构造推广至Dirichlet级数与Euler乘积。

Result: 建立了从Bernoulli词核到zeta临界线零点纵坐标平方模1分布的完整几何与算术桥梁：整数-残差分解、相位变量提取、素数生成元坐标分解，并提供了精确恒等式与数值控制，但明确不声称证明黎曼猜想。

Conclusion: 该构造将组合词核、复几何、整数分解与zeta函数分布联系起来，为gamma_k^2 mod 1的相关问题提供了新的结构化视角，并设定了条件性Weyl检验作为开放研究方向。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [243] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"自由暂停令牌"(free pause token)机制：语言模型在每个下一个令牌预测时获得额外计算（类似思考令牌），但该计算通过权重共享主干上的并行预测流承载，而非作为序列中的额外令牌。该方法在1B参数模型上使下一个令牌预测困惑度提升2-3个百分位点。由于暂停令牌挂载于既有位置而非新增位置，推理时不增加上下文长度、KV缓存或延迟；主要成本仅存在于训练阶段，相比优化预训练流水线仅增加x1.14计算量。结果是相对于标准下一个令牌训练Transformer在等FLOPs、等参数、等令牌数条件下的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暂停/思考令牌方式通过将计算作为额外序列令牌引入，虽能给模型额外计算以改进预测，但会带来推理时的上下文长度增长、KV缓存增加和延迟上升等使用成本。作者旨在设计一种同样提供额外思考计算但不付出这些推理代价的机制，使额外计算与使用开销解耦。

本文核心动机在于：(1) 在推理时不增加上下文长度、KV缓存和明显延迟；(2) 保持等FLOPs、等参数、等令牌的训练比较有效性；(3) 在等资源条件下超越标准下一个令牌训练Transformer，获得真正可用的预测质量提升。

Method: 该机制的关键设计是：额外计算被放在一个与主序列预测平行的预测流(parallel prediction stream)中，而不是作为追加的序列令牌。两种流共享同一个权重共享主干(weight-shared backbone)，通过挂载于既有位置而非新增位置来实现"暂停"。训练阶段需额外计算以维护该并行流，但经过优化压缩后增量可降至x1.14；在推理阶段通过剪除并行流的序列化开销从而消除KV缓存及上下文长度代价，且由于增长主要在推理FLOPs（通常不是吞吐瓶颈）而几乎不增加延迟。

Result: 在1B参数模型上进行实证评估：(1) 下一个令牌预测的困惑度/性能指标相比基线提升约2-3个百分位点(centinats)；(2) 训练计算增量经优化后从一般开销降至最低x1.14，同时保留大部分性能收益；(3) 推理部署时不增加上下文长度、无额外KV缓存、几乎无额外延迟。综合而言，该方法是相对于标准下一个令牌训练Transformer在等FLOPs、等参数、等令牌条件下的系统改进。

Conclusion: 作者证明存在一种"免费"的方式为Transformer提供暂停/思考式额外计算：以平行预测流形式挂载于既有位置而非新增令牌。该方法在推理时是免费的（无上下文长度、无KV缓存、基本无延迟），主要代价集中在训练阶段且可压缩至约x1.14。由此在等FLOPs、等参数、等令牌设置下获得超越标准NTP训练的改进，为语言模型高效"思考"提供了新的架构设计路径。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [244] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的同类站点相对比较方法，通过引入能量感知的最小失真嵌入（MDE）公式，识别移动通信网络中能源效率低下的站点。该方法利用能量排斥机制使高能耗异常站点在嵌入空间中偏离其局部邻居，实现站点级能源异常的自动检测与现场调查优先级排序，且优于传统异常检测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗是移动网络运营商最大的运营支出之一，但站点级能源低效（如故障冷却控制器、闲置无线电设备、寄生辅助负载）通常难以检测，原因在于缺乏地面真实低效标签，且历史测量数据中可能已嵌入固有的低效模式。

Method: 提出一种无监督的同类相对比较方法，基于结构及运行特征相似的站点应具有可比能源消耗的假设。引入能量感知的最小失真嵌入（MDE）公式，扩展标准MDE目标函数并加入基于能量的排斥机制，促使相对于同类站点能耗异常偏高的站点在嵌入空间中从局部邻域偏离。所得低维表示同时保留结构相似性并编码能量相关偏差，通过同类相对比较实现异常站点识别。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法优于传统异常检测基准，能够为大规模移动网络能效优化提供稳健基础。导出的异常评分可为现场调查提供优先级排序机制，帮助运营商将工程资源集中在最可能产生节能效益的站点。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动通信网络中站点级能源低效检测提供了一种无需标签依赖的无监督解决方案，通过同类相对比较和嵌入空间中的能量偏差编码，实现了对低效站点的高效识别与工程资源优化配置。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [245] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAND是一种可解释的无监督表格异常检测器，通过在单位球面上的方向组织计算，以投影是否逃逸子高斯极值基线来打分。其创新在于标志点的"见证方向"本身即为特征级解释，无需额外成本即可获得；评分复杂度与样本量线性相关，并带有探针效率界保证。在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND以与16个无监督基线相当的ROC-AUC取得了最佳平均Friedman排名，而其原生解释比事后SHAP/LIME及ECOD更准确、更忠实，且查询成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流无监督异常检测器只输出分数，无法解释哪些特征驱动了异常判定；事后套用SHAP/LIME需对检测器进行数千次重复查询且仅为近似。论文旨在设计一种"天然可解释"的异常检测器，使解释成为计算的固有产物而非事后附加。

Method: WAND围绕单位球面上的方向组织计算，对每个点按其投影逃离子高斯极值基线的程度打分。标志该点的见证方向（既是特征空间中的向量）即构成其解释——即每个特征的归因，无需额外成本即可获得；由于分数可微，该归因还可通过梯度恢复。评分计算与样本规模线性相关，并通过探针效率界保证每个异常点都有见证方向，进而有解释。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND在ROC-AUC上与16种无监督基线持平的情况下取得最佳平均Friedman排名；其原生解释相比事后SHAP/LIME以及ECOD更准确、更忠实，且只需极小一部分查询成本。

Conclusion: WAND在无精度损失的前提下获得了可解释性收益，提供了一种实用、可解释的异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [246] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线强化学习的几何视角，将策略视为概率流形上的点，并将广泛的离线actor目标解释为单步邻近策略改进（SPI），进而提出多步邻近策略改进（MPI）作为插件式细化机制，通过序贯重中心邻近步实现受控的政策改进，在D4RL基准上提升TD3+BC、ReBRAC和IQL等强基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习需要在两个相互竞争的要求间取得平衡：策略更新应保持在数据集支持的邻近区域内以保证价值估计可靠，但实质性的提升往往需要超越行为分布。这种张力缺乏一种统一的几何视角来理解和设计策略更新机制。

Method: 将策略建模为具有特定度量几何的概率流形，证明一类离线actor目标等价于由critic定义能量诱导的流形梯度流的隐式离散化（SPI）。在此基础上提出MPI，通过序贯组合重中心邻近步实现多步策略改进，支持多种策略几何（确定性策略和对角高斯策略），可作为插拔式细化机制集成到现有离线RL算法中。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，少量MPI细化步即可在多个任务上提升强离线基线（TD3+BC、ReBRAC、IQL）的性能。诊断实验进一步区分了重中心细化与固定目标更新调度之间的差异，并刻画了critic误差存在时的局限性。

Conclusion: MPI提供了一种统一的几何理论框架来解释离线actor更新，并通过受控的多步邻近细化在超越行为分布的同时保持策略可靠性，为离线RL的策略优化提供了新的理论洞察和实用改进手段。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [247] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了XCal-FL，一种面向跨孤岛联邦学习的可解释性驱动的闭环本地训练算法，通过三个互补信号动态校准差分隐私噪声，在保持正式DP保证的同时显著提升模型准确性和解释保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带差分隐私的联邦学习虽能保护数据机密性，但DP噪声会扭曲学习表示并降低解释保真度。现有方法使用静态特征重要性信号来适配DP噪声，限制了噪声校准只能进行事后分析（post hoc），无法在训练过程中根据解释质量动态调整噪声。

Method: XCal-FL设计了一个闭环、可解释性驱动的本地训练算法，在跨孤岛FL图像分类中动态校准DP噪声。校准基于三个互补信号：（1）预测logit变化（衡量对模型置信度的因果影响）、（2）反事实间隔（捕捉决策边界敏感性）、（3）显著图集中度（量化模型注意力的空间一致性），并通过自适应隐私核算来执行正式的DP保证。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上的不同FL配置实验中，XCal-FL相比静态噪声FL将预测性能提升超10%、解释保真度最高提升5倍，并在保真度上优于最先进的adaptive DP方法。XCal-FL还实现了更高的隐私预算效率，将每个累积隐私损失单位转化为准确性和解释保真度的更大增益。

Conclusion: 分析揭示解释保真度与预测性能不同，不随隐私损失近似线性扩展而是呈现非线性动态。这说明可解释性是隐私权衡中一个独立维度，不能仅从效用推断，对决策关键型应用中的训练和隐私预算分配具有重要意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [248] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力机制（尤其是高秩注意力矩阵）在基础模型中的训练动力学。通过构建注意力索引模型的统一框架，在高维极限下刻画了总体损失景观与在线SGD训练动力学，揭示了三种注意力参数化方式（直接优化、绑定注意力S=WW^⊤、非绑定注意力S=UV^⊤）各自的隐式偏差及弱恢复样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动力学（尤其在注意力矩阵具有高秩时）仍缺乏理论理解。现有研究多聚焦于低秩或简化场景，对多层、多头架构下注意力参数化如何影响训练动态与收敛行为尚不清楚。

Method: 提出注意力索引模型（attention-indexed models）统一框架，可表示多层及多头注意力架构。在高维极限下将总体损失景观简化为有限迹序参数集合；对于在线随机梯度下降过程，证明其由无限矩阵矩层级支配，且可被有限截断系统指数级精确近似。通过该框架分析三种参数化方式：直接优化S、绑定注意力(S=WW^⊤)与未绑定注意力(S=UV^⊤)的训练动力学。

Result: 研究发现注意力参数化本身可构成架构层面的隐式偏差。直接优化注意力矩阵S可能陷入无信息状态；绑定注意力S=WW^⊤可自动触发对称性破缺机制，在Θ(d^2 log d)样本量下实现弱恢复；未绑定注意力S=UV^⊤则展现出快慢动力学机制——预激活均值在快时间尺度演化，重叠在慢时间尺度演化，当快动力学选择的态破坏初始对称性时，可在Θ(d^2 log d)尺度上实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 本文从统计物理与高维概率视角系统揭示了注意力参数化对训练动力学的隐式影响，表明参数化选择本身即是归纳偏置的重要来源，为理解不同注意力架构（如直接优化、绑定、非绑定）的收敛行为提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [249] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIFFINT是一种可解释的基于重建的异常检测器，其自编码器瓶颈由端到端学习的软轴对齐区间隶属度构成，将每个潜在单元对应为特征空间中的可读超矩形。它在48个ADBench基准上达到最佳平均排名（ROC-AUC 4.10，AUPR 4.16），是统计上并列的领先七方法中唯一的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于重建的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确但不可解释：当模型标记一个样本为异常时，无法告知从业者哪些特征范围使其异常。这限制了此类方法在高风险场景中的部署与审计能力。

Method: DIFFINT将自编码器的潜在瓶颈结构化为一组软的、轴对齐的区间成员度，直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形，实例通过各区间内的落入强度进行编码，重建误差作为异常分数。方法包含三个关键机制：(1) Lipschitz约束编码器的认证重建误差下界，保证落在学习支持之外的点被正确识别；(2) 针对仅少量特征异常情况的渐变抑制机制；(3) 闭式无标签重要性度量，仅从模型已有量中为每个(单元, 特征)对排序，将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对比22个基线（统一[-1,1]归一化协议），DIFFINT在两个指标上均取得最佳平均排名（ROC-AUC 4.10，AUPR 4.16）。在仅用内点训练的检测器中明显领先其所在类别，并与最强的含污染数据检测器具有竞争力。它是七个统计上并列的领先方法中唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT成功弥合了深度重建异常检测的性能与可解释性之间的鸿沟：它在保持可微分表示学习能力的同时，提供可检查的内部结构，并以可认证的理论保证和实证验证确保其归纳偏置的有效性，最终在主流基准上达到领先性能，同时成为领先集群中唯一具备可解释性的方法。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出持续知识更新方法仅用单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩次比较排名是不充分的。在24个月Wikidata流上，改变评估月份、回放LoRA秩次和查询表述，周期层次结构与累积回放方法间的胜负关系会发生逆转（如Qwen2.5-1.5B上周期层次从领先5.0分变为落后11.6分）。作者提出应报告轨迹和容量扫描，仅在排序跨评估区域稳定时才声明稳健胜者，否则应报告胜者区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新方法的评价方式通常只依赖单一最终检查点和一个固定的适配器秩次（如LoRA rank），作者认为这种评估不足以识别真正更优的操作点，可能导致方法优劣判断偏差。

Method: 在24个月的Wikidata数据流上，固定周期层次结构（periodic hierarchy）作为对比基准，与采用累积回放（cumulative replay）的方法进行系统比较。在评估中同时变化三个因素：评估月份、回放LoRA秩次（rank-8到rank-72）以及查询表述方式，并在Qwen2.5-1.5B与Llama-3.2-1B两个模型及保留改写数据上验证结果。

Result: 发现方法排名强烈依赖评估时刻与回放侧容量配置。例如在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，周期层次相对于rank-8回放的5.0分优势，在rank-72回放时变为11.6分劣势；高秩次下若以对齐后的端点评估看似平局，但时间平均回放反而领先9-13分。该秩次相关的逆转在Llama-3.2-1B及保留改写上也复现。

Conclusion: 应报告评估轨迹和容量扫描，仅当排序在评估区域内稳定时才可声明稳健胜者；否则比较应以胜者区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿呈现。在此协议下，周期层次结构仅能定位为更低更新成本的操作点，而非质量上的胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [251] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL是一种即插即用的残差检索与反馈校正方法,用于多变量时间序列预测。它冻结基础预测器,将历史预测残差存储为仅训练内存,推理时检索相似上下文中的残差轨迹,并通过集合感知路由器选择和组合这些轨迹以校正预测。实验表明RATL在多数设置下能提升冻结基础预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)通过引入外部证据补充参数化模型,这一思想对连续输出回归问题同样有吸引力。然而直接复用检索到的目标值在样本输出水平、数值尺度或局部动态不同时往往不够鲁棒;传统预测流程虽然用残差进行模型优化和误差诊断,但并未保留历史残差示例作为推理时可访问的记忆。因此缺乏一种利用历史残差作为可检索记忆来校正连续输出预测的方法。

Method: RATL采用即插即用(residual-retrieval)框架:(1)冻结基础预测器(base forecaster)以构建检索键;(2)将基础模型的历史预测残差转化为特定于该模型的仅训练内存;(3)推理时在满足因果可用性约束的前提下,从相似历史上下文中检索残差轨迹;(4)使用运行在预测块和变量上的集合感知路由器(set-aware router)来选择和组合检索到的轨迹,实现反馈校正;(5)通过验证集进行校正强度选择,限制残差过度注入。

Result: 实验表明:与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息;RATL在大多数实验设置下能提升冻结基础预测器的性能;消融实验显示学习路由能强化原始残差反馈,基于验证的校正强度选择能限制残差过度注入。以iTransformer为主冻结基础预测器,与多个强基线对比,并测试跨骨干迁移性,RATL在大多数设定中进一步提升了基础预测器性能。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为基础模型特有的历史预测误差,为连续输出预测提供了一种即插即用的、基于残差记忆的学习反馈校正范式。其核心贡献在于将历史残差作为可检索的记忆资源,通过检索和路由机制实现预测反馈校正,突破了传统预测中残差仅用于优化与诊断而不作为推理时可访问记忆的局限。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings.
